If you don't then learn that before trying this hunt. If you think you can handle it, head east out of the **Nomad Village** into the **Gizas** **North Bank**. Kill every creature in the area, then go back into the **Nomad Village** and save. Go back into the **Gizas North Bank** and look for an overpowered Chickatrice. Once you target it, be prepared for quite the fight.

> ##### Hunt: Cluckatrice <Chickatrice x3>
>
>
> | **Level** | 15 <13> | **LP** | 6 <1> | **CP** | 830 <500> |
> | **HP** | 7509<br><3067> | **Steal** | Small Feather (55/80%), Wind Stone (10/30%), <br>Taurus Gem (3/6%) <br><Potion (55/80%), Small Feather (10/30%), Large Feather (3/6%)> |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Earth <Earth> |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Lightning <Lightning> |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Stop, Reverse, Blind, Silence, Disease, Disable, Immobilize, Sap, Libra<br><Doom, Reverse, Sleep, Blind, Poison, Silence, Oil, Disease, Disable, Immobilize, Sap, Slow, Berserk, Libra> |
>
> There's a reason this is an elite mark. The Cluckatrice will be joined by three Chickatrice. All attack rapidly for quite a bit of damage. On top of that, Cluckatrice can use Stone Gaze that may afflict everyone in range with petrify. If you don't remove petrify before the timer is done, the character will turn to stone. Cluckatrice can also use Mucus, which causes slow on a single target. It can combo up to 5 times, with each hit doing around 100 damage if you are low level (around level 10-12). If you manage to put a status on it, it can also use Purify to remove it.
>
> To initiate the battle, target a Chickatrice. The Cluckatrice will immediately run over to protect the little one and will be joined soon by the other Chickatrice. The hunt is not complete until you kill the Cluckatrice and all three Chickatrice. With all of the Chickatrice in the battle, you will be taking a lot of damage. If you've gotten far enough, a quickening is usually the recommended way of removing them. If that's not an option, use your motes or any area attack spell you have. Try to use your motes so that they hit all four targets. Aquara can do about 350 damage, while Dark Motes do about 150. Make sure you do not use any Aero Motes, as they will heal the marks. If you have any fang items, don't bother - the foes seem to be immune. Hopefully, by the time you've burned through your motes, one of the Chickatrice will be down and the other two close. Don't hesitate to throw Ethers at your healer or Hi-Potions at your tank to keep everyone alive. If anyone gets afflicted with Petrify, be sure to remove it before the counter gets too low. Once all three Chickatrice are down, keep on pounding at the Cluckatrice while trying to keep everyone healed. If someone falls, you can swap in your party member in reserve. You can also use items (like phoenix downs) on reserve members, so you should be able to prevent a total party wipe.
>
> I took on this hunt with Fran and Balthier at level 10, Vaan at level 12, and Basch at level 14. Fran was set to cast cure for anyone below 70% health, and that's all she did the entire battle. Vaan was my highest HP character with 974. I started with Balthier in reserve, who was also my only party member that could remove Petrify with Remedies. The first time Vaan's health went critical, I used my Balance Mote to do about 650 damage to everything. I had wiped out one of the Chickatrices with that and two Aquara Motes before switching to Dark Motes. Even after getting through the three Chickatrice fairly quickly, I ran out of Hi-Potions as Cluckatrice got to HP critical. Vaan and Basch both got KOed, so I swapped in Balthier and used Phoenix Downs to bring the melee characters back. I used my only Elixir on Vaan, and finished the battle shortly after. So, it probably wasn't worth doing this early - but it can be done.

| **Hunt Completed** | Cluckatrice |
| **Rewards** | 1000 Gil<br>Amber Armlet (x1)<br>Rainbow Egg (x1) |

Return to Dania in the **Nomad Village** to claim your reward. She is still hanging out in the cockatrice pen. Immediately sell the Rainbow Egg to the nearby merchant Arjie to unlock a new bazaar item. The Hi-Potion Pack is one of the few bazaar items that you can cause to show up repeatedly, and it's the only item the Rainbow Egg is used for. So if you burned through your Hi-Potions, now you can get them back.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Rainbow Egg (x1) | Hi-Potion Pack | Hi-Potion (x10) | 1111 gil | 689 gil |

## Walkthrough Part 2 - Seeding the Revolution

### Tracking a Friend

#### Heading to Bhujerba

Make your way to **Rabanastre / Westgate**. You can speak to Fran by the moogling, or head straight into the **Aerodrome**. Head all the way to the back to find Balthier standing off to the left. Speak to him. You can find out more about your destination, and when you're ready to proceed reassure him you mean it. Enjoy the HD scenes and Balthier's vastly-improved audio compared to the PS2 version.

After the scenes, be sure to interact with the Teleport Crystal and save your game. You will not be able to teleport away, so you'll be stuck in this area at least until you finish this arc of the story. Exit the **Aerodrome** for a scene. Once you have control, head east to the first intersection.

| ##### Cartographer's Guild Moogle - _Bhujerba / Travica Way_ |
| **Area** | **Cost** |
| Bhujerba | 70 gil |
| Lhusu Mies | 650 gil |

There is a moogle from the Cartographer's Guild that is selling maps of **Bhujerba** and the **Lhusu Mines**. You can check out the shops throughout Bhujerba, but check out the upcoming loot (particularly in **Lhusu Mines / Site 2**) that you may find before buying anything. There is a lot of good treasure to be had, if you are patient enough to respawn it until you get what you want. Your way is forced eventually to the **Lhusu Mines**, so simply follow the path through the city until you reach the Save Crystal. As you approach the mines there will be a scene, followed by another after you enter the mine. Before progressing any deeper, you may want to turn off any gambits on your guest that use up your potions and change his attack gambit to the foe with lowest HP to match the others. Check the rest of your gambits to make sure you are not duplicating any healing or status restoration gambits, and then away we go.

#### Shaft Entry

| **Treasures** | 6 (or 5 if you already claimed _Silence_ in **Dalmasca Estersand**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Silence (magick) |
| **Enemies** | Steeling, Slaven, Skull Defender, Skeleton |

Make your way north to what looks like some rail tracks. If you didn't open the chest in the **Dalmasca Estersand** that contained the magick Silence, you should find it to the right. Head west and take your first left south, if you want to grab all the chests. You have chances to get an aero mote (or an ether) which may be useful in the Rocktoise hunt that we'll attempt soon. You also might find the katana Osafune, if you have anyone using katanas. Head back to the northern rails and look for an aqua mote or bronze mace at the west end (if the chest appears and isn't gil). Head back to the second path south and follow the path all the way south. Take the west exit.

#### Oltam Span

| **Treasures** | 3 |
| **Enemies** | Steeling, Slaven, Skull Defender, Skeleton, Aerieel (rare game) |

There is a 20% chance that the rare game Aerieel will spawn when you approach the center of the bridge. Aerieel is not much of a challenge, so you might as well attempt to pick it up now. There are possible chests on both lower walkways flanking the bridge, including opportunities for Dark Mote or Topkapi Hat and either an Aero Mote or Onion Bombs. On the west side of the bridge is a chance for a chest with either Dark Mote or Echo Herbs.

#### Transitway 1

| **Treasures** | 3 |
| **Notable Loot** | Numerology (technick) |
| **Enemies** | Steeling, Slaven, Skull Defender, Skeleton |

This is the area where you will take on an upcoming hunt called the Nidhogg. More on that later. There is a narrow path immediately to your right when you enter. It has two very closely spaced explosion traps that can do quite a bit of damage, along with pop-up enemies - but there is also a guaranteed chest containing the technick Numerology. Once you grab it, it will not respawn. If you return to the entrance of the area and head west, there may be a chest in the southern fork with a Balance Mote or Potion, and another chest in the northern fork along the east wall with either an Aero Mote or Leather Headgear.

#### Shunia Twinspan

| **Treasures** | 3 |
| **Enemies** | Skull Defender, Skeleton |

#### Site 2

| **Treasures** | 8 |
| **Enemies** | Skull Defender, Skeleton |

This is the area with the good stuff, but you have to be careful. There is a glitch that once you trigger the next part of the story you will no longer be able to spawn new chests in the area. If you have anyone as a Shikari, the most important treasure to grab is the Assassin's Dagger, which is located just before the scene trigger - so if you don't get it, run back to **Shunia Twinspan** and try again. When you first enter the area, there is a dead end path to the left after the curve. There is an unavoidable explosion trap on the way, but there is a chance for a chest with a Serpent Rod. Back on the rail tracks you entered on, at the end is a possible chest with an Aero Mote or the accessory Steel Gorget. Head south and follow the path around until you climb a staircase. At the top of the stairs, if you turn left there might be a chest with a Dispel Mote or an Ether. To the right is a chamber with up to three chests. The one straight across from the entrance is the one that might hold the Assassin's Dagger. Otherwise it will have gil or a Gravity Mote. The Assassin's Dagger is probably the highest attack weapon you have (unless you are already using the Kakarta), and your Shikari will start doing double to triple the damage of everyone else. The chest on the south side of the chamber has either a Cura Mote or a Handkerchief. The last chest in the northwest corner of the small chamber has either a Hi-Potion or Gold Needle. DO NOT GO FURTHER if you did not get the items you wanted. If you head north, you will trigger the next scene and you will no longer be able to spawn chests in this area. Instead, run back to **Shunia Twinspan** and keep trying until you get what you want. It took me about 5 or 6 tries to get the Assassin's Dagger - and it was worth it. By then I had at least one of everything else. There are two more chests in the area, but unless you are heavily overleveled or have the Kakarta, they aren't worth it. Head north from the small chamber with the three chests to trigger the next set of scenes. After they are done, hold down R2 and run all the way back to **Shaft Entry**. Head for the exit of the Lhusu mines to trigger a scene.

### "Basch lives!"

After you exit the **Lhusu Mines**, head up the stairs toward the Save Crystal to trigger another scene that initiates a minigame. Before you play this mini-game, you can unload your loot, unlock some new Bazaar Goods, and make enough to purchase any gear you couldn't afford before. Again, sell everything except the Teleport Stones. The Assorted Leathers offer a huge discount, but only if you need them still.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Wolf Pelt (x2)<br>Tanned Hide (x1)<br>Dark Stone (x2) | Assorted Leathers | Leather Breastplate<br>Leather Headgear | 300 gil | 500 gil |

Now that you're a bit richer, you can raise eyebrows by running around and pressing square near people with talk icons over the head. Avoid people walking around with swords, and focus on people standing holding books. Start by announcing to the people near the merchant to get your notoriety up to 10 or more, then exit the area. Just ahead is one of the parijanah guides that gives you a big notoriety boost. Try to catch him before the Sainikah gets close and penalizes you 30%. There are plenty of routes you can take, but one that is pretty quick involves heading south from that intersection getting the attention of everyone around the gambit shop, head west after passing the gambit shop and then turning south. Go all the way to the south, spreading the word, then turn back northeast. Just past the intersection is another parijanah guide. After announcing to him, you should be over 80%. Then run into **Kaff Terrace** to the east and find one more parijanah guide to finish it off. Now sit back and watch the next few scenes. After the scenes, you are free to leave Bhujerba again if you need to finish up any sidequests or hunts you passed on earlier.

### OPTIONAL Rocktoise and Nighogg Hunts, Pilika's Diary Sidequest

#### Starting the hunts

After the scenes, you are in The Cloudborne tavern. Inspect the Hunt Notice board to pick up the Nidhogg hunt. Exit the taverns and head west into **Travica Way**, and continue to make your way west until you are in **Khus Skygrounds**. Standing on the stage near the south exit you will find the moogle Pilika. Talk to her to accept the Rocktoise hunt.

Before moving on to start the hunts, make sure you do have the best gear that you still lack. You especially want to have a good shield on one of your melee characters. Also make sure that you have the technick Charge from Clio's Technicks, and set up a gambit on your healer to use charge when MP < 10% (if you have someone that carries it on their license board).

Make your way back to the Save Crystal by the Lhusu Mines, then speak with Aekom down the stairs to accept the Nidhogg hunt.

#### Marauder in the Mines

To find Nidhogg, retrace your steps through the mines until you get to **Transitway 1**. You should see Nidhogg immediately upon entering.

> ##### Hunt: Nidhogg
>
>
> | **Level** | 10 | **LP** | 5 | **CP** | 780 |
> | **HP** | 6079 | **Steal** | Antidote (55/80%), Tanned Hide (10/30%), Aries Gem (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Fire |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Water<br>\[Half Damage\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Stop, Confuse, Reverse, Sleep, Blind, Silence, Disease, Immobilize, Slow, Berserk, Libra |
>
> Nidhogg starts easy and gets tougher. It's a good idea to have one melee and two ranged. Your melee should have a shield and high HP. Nidhogg likes to kick things off by casting Saber, which increases the damage it deals. It also seems to have a high combo rate. So healing is going to be pretty constant. If you only have Cure magick, that probably won't keep up. Instead, it might be best to have someone with a gambit to toss a Hi-Potion if your melee character gets below the percentage necessary for a Hi-Potion to fully heal. Having a gambit to treat Posion status is also advisable. If you have Fran (or a black magick user) in the party, give her a gambit to start casting fire when Nidhogg's HP drops below 30%. Nidhogg's physical defense, speed, and attack power all increase quite a bit toward the end of the battle, and having someone spam Fire can help tear through the last little bit. When Nidhogg's health drops below 20%, it will use Tail Spear which can deal a substantial amount of damage if it targets one of your lower HP characters. It also begins to attack with no charge time, so the attack frequency increases tremendously.
>
> If another enemy jumps into the battle, let your ranged characters take them out while your melee character keeps Nidhogg's attention. Overall, as long as you can withstand the HP critical onslaught toward the end, this isn't too tough. If it is, you can always bypass Nidhogg and go grind Skeletons and Skull Defenders in the **Shunia Twinspan** to get a little stronger.

| **Hunt Completed** | Nidhogg |
| **Rewards** | 600 Gil<br>Rose Corsage (x1)<br>Balaclava (x1) |

After ridding the tunnels of Nidhogg, you can go straight on to Rocktoise, but it might be wise to head back to claim rewards and save. Rocktoise can be challenging. After giving you the hunt reward, Aekom will also hand over a Great Serpentskin. DO NOT SELL THIS. It is needed later as part of the Patient in the Desert sidequest that was started earlier with Dantro's wife. Save at the crystal and do any loot selling or shopping to get ready for the next hunt.

#### The Cry of Its Power

Rocktoise is located in the same place where you had the scene about the manufacted nethicite, **Lhusu Mines / Site 2**. Make your way back there, but do not charge in. Again, make sure you are not using Cure in your gambits for healing as they won't keep up. This fight can go really well, or really poorly...

> ##### Hunt: Rocktoise
>
>
> | **Level** | 16 | **LP** | 8 | **CP** | 1530 |
> | **HP** | 17548 | **Steal** | Potion (55/80%), Turtle Shell (10/30%), <br>Aged Turtle Shell (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Wind |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Stop, Sleep, Blind, Silence, Disease, Immobilize, Slow, Libra |
>
> Rocktoise has much stronger physical attacks than magick attacks, but its magick attacks can multi-target. Then again, so can some of his physical attacks, like Sonic Turn. He can also remove bad statuses with Purify, so be sure to have some weapons that inflict status ailments like Silence so that it wastes turns clearing them. While it has a few other skills, they all do about the same damage. It also has a chance to inflict Slow, so having some Chronos Tears handy might be in order. What can make this fight way tougher is that Skull Defenders and Skeletons can spawn from the ground in the room with it. To counter this, only enter the room far enough to get Rocktoise to target you and then back away. Try to pull Rocktoise as far from the center of the room as possible before engaging. Again, one melee and two ranged characters is probably the way to go. If you do happen to spawn an undead, let your ranged characters take it down while your melee character keeps Rocktoise's attention.
>
> If you are taking too much physical damage, you may be able to time backing away with your melee character to entice Rocktoise to use the magick spells that don't do quite as much damage. Once its health drops below 20%, it will begin attacking incessantly, just like some of the other hunts. As long as your melee character has the best shield available and high HP, you should still be able to keep up the pressure and finish it off. While the hunts are getting tougher and more dangerous, they still don't need too much of a specialized strategy yet. Don't worry, they'll get more exciting...

| **Hunt Completed** | Rocktoise |
| **Rewards** | 1200 Gil<br>Hi-Potion (x2)<br>Survival Vest (x1) |

Head back to Pilika in **Bhujerba / Skygrounds** to claim your reward. Then immediately speak with her again to start another errand.

#### Pilika's Diary

| **Notable Loot** | Merchant's Armband, Pilika's Diary |

Tell Pilika you'll help her and she'll hand over a Merchant's Armband to get you into the back area of Clio's Technicks. Head east of of the **Skygrounds**, and head southeast to reach **Miner's End**. Clio's Technicks is the first shop on the left. Head inside and go to the elevated area on the left. On the bookshelf with a ladder in front you will see Pilika's Diary at eye level. Pick it up and read it (your choice doesn't affect the rewards, so you might as well). Return to Pilika. If you tell her you read the diary, she'll give you the Bangle accessory that gives the wearer the Libra status. If you tell her you've done nothing, she'll trust you and give you a Jade Collar which improves evasion. I prefer the Jade Collar. Your answer does not have to be honest to get the item.

### A New Hope

When you're all ready to proceed, head to the north end of **Travica Way** and speak to the Bhujerban Sainikah. Tell him to take you to the Marquis. Reassure him you're ready once you have your snacks for several upcoming scenes. When you eventually regain control, use the Save Crystal.

#### The Dreadnought Leviathan

| **Treasures** | 4 ( **Port Section**), 6 ( **Large Freight Stores**), 6 ( **Starboard Section**), 4 ( **Sub-control Room**) |
| **Notable Loot** | map of the Dreadnought Leviathan, Sleep (magick), Infuse (technick), No. 1 Brig Key, System Access Key |
| **Enemies** | Imperial Swordsman, Mastiff, Imperial Magus, Imperial Hoplite, Imperial Gunner, Judge, Judge Ghis (boss) |

After saving, exit the door to the east. As you move forward, you'll be warned about the security doors. This is another good chance to get a high level chain as long as you don't run into any Mastiff enemies. Everything else chains together. Trigger as many alarms as you'd like to gain LP, but the steals and drops are lousy (usually just Potions, with the occasional Antidote or Phoenix Down). There can be up to four chests in the **Port Section**, but nothing very valuable. The first may be just to the right after the scene introducing you to the alarm webs. The second can be found by heading east until you are forced south. The third is found in the dead end coming of the southwestern chamber (it may hold a partisan if you have a Uhlan). Getting this chest will cause an alarm web to turn on behind you, forcing you to trigger the alarm. The alarms shut off after about two or three imperials find you. The last chest is on the eastern most path leading to the next room to the south.

When you enter the next room ( **Large Freight Stores**), you will be on an upper walkway. There may be a couple of patrols up top, and several more on the lower level where all 6 chests can be found. There may be a group of four chests in the middle of the room. Two are guaranteed one-time spawns that both contain Reflectga Motes. The other two have healing or status removing items. There may be one chest to the east of these four that might hold Ringmail if you don't already have that on all of your characters. The last chest would be in the southeastern corner of the lower level, and it might contain a magick users hat Shepherd's Bolero. You can't use the Security Stations yet. From here, there are two possible routes. One has all the treasure, the other is more direct (and you have less control over the mobs you face).

The shorter, non-treasure route (that you shouldn't take) is accessed via the north door on the lower level. This puts you in the southern portion of the **Airship Berth Access**. You will need to make your way around the central loop until you reach the north intersection. You would head south from there. This is where the other path joins you. This shorter route will have you facing many chain-breaking Mastiff. If you take the left route around the loop, you will trigger two alarms. If you take the right route, you will pass through two one-way gates that activate alarm webs behind you, and you will trigger one alarm near the north end. But it doesn't matter since you aren't going this way, right?

The longer route requires you to head east through one of the doors on the upper level of the **Large Freight Stores** into the **Starboard Section**. First, head to the southeastern chamber for a one-time chest with the Sleep magick. This is a guaranteed chest that is linked to one that shows up later in the game with the same item. If you grab it here, you won't see it later (since it won't do you any good). Head north to the next chamber. If you go north from the chamber, you will trigger two alarms on your way to the next chamber. If you instead took the west path, however, you'll fight a Mastiff but won't trigger any alarms. The alarms might draw the Mastiff anyway, but this should be the only Mastiff you'll face for a while. From this third chamber, head north until you hit a wall, then head all the way east, then south for a chest that may have an Ether or Iron Armor. You also passed through a one-way door, so you will have to trigger an alarm now. Since you must, just follow the path north as it curves around, and when given the opportunity head north and immediately west to find possibly two more treasures. One might hold an Ether or an Iron Helm, the other a Blue Fang or Hi-Potion. From here, your next destination is the two dead ends to the southwest of your current location. If you don't want to trigger any alarms, follow the path around to the north, then west. When forced to turn south, immediately turn west again. Turn south at the end of that path and follow it all the way until it turns back east where you can find a chest that may contain a Reflectga Mote. On you way, you just passed the dead end to the west that may have a chest behind an alarm wall containing a Hi-Potion or Iron Sword. Not too thrilling, unless you just want to trigger another alarm. Now, directly to the north is a Bulkhead Release that you can only pull while the alarm is off, but if you head straight to it you will trigger an alarm. You can do that and wait for the alarm to shut off, or you can loop around to the west and avoid all alarm webs. Once the Bulkhead Release has been activated, you can use the door a little to the east of the Bulkhead Release to enter the room to the north, the **Sub-control Room**.

Just to the right when you enter, two chests could spawn, one of which might hold a Vega if you need it. The other options are Potions or Phoenix Down. Take out the enemies and head down the stairs to the lower level. There is another Bulkhead Release to pull. There is also a pathway to the west that is not on your map that contains a guaranteed one-time chest with the technick Infuse (also linked to a later chest) and another chest that may contain a Zwill Blade. This pathway is also behind a one-way door that spawns an alarm web, so expect to have a few enemies to take care of before proceeding through the door at the south of the lower level into the **Airship Berth Access**.

When you enter the **Airship Berth Access**, you are now at the northernmost point and just need to head straight south, rejoining the other route you may have taken. The moment you open the door to the south, you are thrown into a fixed encounter with three Imperial Swordsmen, one Imperial Magus, and two Judges. The battle ends when the Judges are felled. You receive the No. 1 Brig Key as a reward, allowing you to open the door directly in front of you after the Congratulations scene.

After the scene, you can choose a job for your new party member. If you picked up Partisan, Uhlan might be nice. Regardless of what you pick, you can chat with the moogle merchant to finish equipping once you spend your LP. Be sure to save again in case you boosted your new companion's HP on the license board.

When you exit **Brig No. 1**, a permanent alarm will trigger. You may attempt to fight, but you could get overwhelmed. Instead, hold R2 and sprint back toward where you first arrived in the Leviathan. Run north through this room into **Airship Berth Access**, then make your way around the loop to the south. This will put you in the **Large Freight Stores** where you can put that Systems Access Key to use to shut off the alarm for 60 seconds. Run up to the upper level and take the north exit back into the **Port Section**. Continue to make your way back to the beginning. You'll get cut off for a scene and get your full party. Be sure to spend LP and equip, if you plan on using your new party member in the upcoming battle. Head straight north after the scene, and then west. When you pass through the door in **Port Launch**, you will trigger a scene followed by a boss battle.

> ##### Boss: Judge Ghis
>
>
> | **Level** | 14 | **LP** | 22 | **CP** | 530 |
> | **HP** | 4120 | **Steal** | Potion (55/80%), Dark Mote (10/30%), <br>Nishijin Belt (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | N/A |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | N/A |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Stop, Sleep, Blind, Sap, Slow |
>
> Ghis is accompanied by three Imperial Swordsmen. If you are quick, you can take them out before Ghis has a chance to get into the battle. Ghis can cast Protect and Aero. If you have two ranged characters, you may be able to space everyone out so that Aero only hits one target at a time. Ghis will also use Greater Barrier after you've knocked off 40% of his health, which will help him resist damage. If you took down Nidhogg and Rocktoise, Ghis should not pose a threat. He hits hard, but if your melee character has a good shield, you can avoid most of the damage. High HP also helps.
>
> If you want a fast battle, you can use three level 1 quickenings (if you've unlocked them on the License Board) to trigger Inferno, which should wipe him out. If Ashe is in your active party, Ghis will focus attacks on her. He has a small chance to cause blindness on hit, so have your melee character wear an accessory to protect against it or have an Eye Drop gambit set up. The battle ends prematurely when Ghis's health drops below 25%.

After the battle you'll get a scene where Han Sol...err, Balthier...complains about a ship not being as good as the Millennium Falcon and Chewba...I mean Fran...tells Luuu...Vaan...that he can't fly it. Seriously, though. The resemblance to Star Wars stops here, right? I mean, Biggs and Wedge are Imperial soldiers in Nalbina in this game, not resistance fighters. Totally different.

Once you've finished farming, head back to the estate of the Marquis (north exit out of **Travica Way** in **Bhujerba**) to continue the story - and hear Balthier make remarks regarding rewards that are eerily similar to a certain smuggler known for his Kessel Run record.

### Forward Unto Dawn

Real quick, before heading west, head back to Rabanastre. There is one new hunt to grab, and you can answer [Ktjn's next question](#walkthrough-part-1---the-occupied-city). You have two options to get back: hoof it through the Westersand, or use the Teleport Crystal. You probably have plenty of Teleport Stones and just finished farming - but if neither of those statements are true, then you might want to hike. After answering her question, head over to the hunt Notice Board in the Sandsea Tavern to find out about the Wyvern Lord. From there, head over to the Weapon Shop and find an Imperial Soldier named Sherral.

Now, there's nothing else to do unless you want to make sunstones on the **Giza Plains** for very little reward. Head back to the Teleport Crystal in the **Western Divide**. You can purchase new magick, technicks, and gear from the merchant here. If you don't mind trying your luck with the random treasures, most of the weapons the merchant sells can be found in the upcoming area.

#### Ogir-Yensa Sandsea

| **Treasures** | 4 ( **Platform 1 - East Tanks**), 10 ( **Platform 1 - Refinery**), 10 ( **East Junction**), 8 ( **Primary Tank Complex**), 9 ( **Central Junction**), 9 ( **Platform 2 - Refinery**), 6 ( **Platform 1 - South Tanks**) |
| **Notable Loot** | map of the Ogir-Yensa Sandsea, map of the Nam-Yensa Sandsea, Sleep (magick), Infuse (technick), Golden Amulet, Reflect (magick) |
| **Enemies** | Alraune, Pineapple (rare game), Urutan-Yensa, Yense, Speartongue, Wyvern, Urutan Exile (rare game) |

Head west out of the **Western Divide** to enter a new area. Walk forward to trigger a scene. There are two treasures in this area that will not appear if you claimed them on the **Leviathan**. One is to the south of the first platform before heading up the ramp and would contain the technick Infuse. The other would be on the southwest spur of the second circular platform, and would contain the magick Sleep. The other two possible spawns occur just to the north of where you enter and may contain a Bhuj and the west spur of the first platform that may contain a Wizard's Staff. It is also possible to steal a Bowgun from the Alraune. Be sure to grab the two maps from the urns at the top of the ramp leading to the first platform. They are hard to miss, but are in a different location than the PS2 version.

Proceed west from this area to trigger another scene. Ten chests spawn here in the **Platform 1 - Refinery**, one is guaranteed. Six of them have gear of some sort. The guaranteed chest contains a Golden Amulet, which you should always have equipped on one of your non-active party members since it doubles LP earned. It is found at the top of the large central platform that you climb up to from the central sand area. You'll have to move / fight through some Alraunes to get to it. The other weapons and armor you can find include the Ancient Sword (southern most point on the map), Round Shield (on the sand just north of the first platform), Windbreaker (on the large platform that also holds the Golden Amulet), Killer Bow (on the small platform linking the two circular platforms on the west side), and Wizard's Hat (on the east side of the circular platform on the southwest corner of the map). You have a chance to spawn the rare game Pineapple in this area, so you may want to re-enter the area until you find it to get it added to your bestiary.

When you begin to encounter Urutan Yensa, attempt to get a 100-chain. If you have to, turn off gambits or flee when encountering non-Urutan so you don't break the chain accidentally. You will be spawning the rare game Urutan Exile in **Platform 2 - Refinery** later that has a chance to drop a nice katana, but the chances are better the higher your chain level (18% at chain level 3 compared to 5% with no chain). If the Urutan Exile does not drop the katana, you can reload your autosave to trigger it to spawn again, but you will lose your chain. You could at least restore the first level chain quickly by killing a few urutan before engaging the exile, but if you don't get the Magoroku on the first attempt while you have the high-level chain, you'll probably be just wasting your time. Alternatively, you could kill another 100 Urutan.

If you don't want to bother with trying to get this rare drop, there is still the Kogarasumaru katana to be found in a chest in **Platform 1 - South Tanks** or **East Junction**. In the **East Junction**, it can be found in a chest on the small platform that forms the base of the y-pattern in the northern area. In **Platform 1 - South Tanks**, it is on the southeastern most small platform.

Back to the various areas. In **East Junction** you can get a Healing Rod that puts regen on a target and a War Hammer from the two ends of the y-pattern. You can also get a Chainmail in the western sand area and a Barbut in the dead end on the south side of the southwestern platform.

**Primary Tank Complex** has a guaranteed one-time chest with Reflect magick on the platform to the left of where you enter from **East Junction**. You can get a Musk Stick from the top center circular platform, and a Sirius gun from the top left circular platform. Watch out for the Salamand Entite. If it becomes aggressive, it can wipe your party at low levels even if you are using Seitengrats. A Wizar'ds Robe might be found in the chest on the east side of the circular platform south of where you get the Sirius. A Linen Cuirass may be found on the northwest portion of the southwesternmost circular platform.

**Central Junction** may have a Round Shield in the eastern sandy area, and a Paramina Crossbow in the southern portion of the western sandy area. On the southeastern small platform, there can be up to three chests, one of which may hold a Soldier's Hat. On the southernmost circular platform, you may find a Lambent Hat.

In **Platform 2 - Refinery**, a chest that may contain a Gold Shield may appear on the southeastern platform of the triangle pattern in the northwest. A Leather Gorget accessory can appear in a chest on the western platform of the same triangular area.

On **Platform 1 - South Tanks**, in addition to the Kogarasumaru described before, a Bhuj can appear on the southwestern platform of the inverted "y". A musk stick can appear in a chest directly north of the southern exit from this area.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Succulent Fruit (x4) | Tinctures & Tonics | Potion (x5)<br>Handkerchief (x3)<br>Gold Needle (x3) | 700 gil | 10 gil more expensive... |
| Bone Fragment (x5)<br>Succulent Fruit (x3)<br>Earth Stone (x4) | Wooden Pole | Cypress Pole (x1) | 480 gil | 480 gil |

Once you've saved, head back into the **Ogir-Yensa Sandsea** and head back north to **Platform 2 - Refinery** and take the west exit into a new region.

#### Nam-Yensa Sandsea

| **Treasures** | 7 ( **The Urutan-Yensa Sea**), 3 ( **Withering Shores**), 9 ( **Augur Hill**), 9 ( **Yellow Sands**), 9 ( **Demesne of the Sand Queen**), 1 ( **Trail of Fading Warmth**), 11 ( **Simoon Bluff**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Eksir Berries, Balance (magick), Embroidered Tippet |
| **Enemies** | Alraune, Danbania, Urutan-Yensa, Yensa, Axebeak, Bagoly, Bull Danbania, Bull Yensa, Megabomb (rare game), Wyvern, Urutan Eater, Wyvern Lord (hunt) |

When you enter, go talk to the moogle and inquire for more details. This optional little mini-quest will provide a useful item along with some extra scenes and lore. Head west into **The Urutan-Yensa Sea**. There is a small chance that a chest with a lot of gil, Hi-Potion, or a Heavy Lance will spawn in the dead-end in the center of the map. Another chest spawns there as well. All of the other chests in the area only hold basic curative items. Take the bridge in the south to access **Withering Shores**. The chests in here hold Ethers of Phoenix Downs, but the main goal is to defeat the Urutan Eater. Ranged attackers work well, especially if you keep Decoy on one melee fighter. The Urutan Eater will sometimes cast low-level elemental magick, so equipping your melee character with Goddess's Magicite can help reduce that damage. Once you defeat the Urutan Eater, head back to the moogle on **The Sandscale Bank**.Head back into the **Ogir-Yensa Sandsea** and start to climb the platform in **Platform - Refinery 2**. After you get interrupted, head back into **Nam-Yensa Sandeas / The Sandscale Bank**. After the scene, talk to the moogle and interact with the Rogue Urutan Flower to receive Eksir Berries.

With that done, you can begin making your way across the **Nam-Yensa**. After all the gear in the **Ogir-Yensa**, this should be a bit less tedious. The first area with anything of note is the **Demesne of the Sand Queen**. There is a chance to spawn a chest with a Balaclava in the northeast corner of the ground level, and a Wizard's Hat in the southwest area of the ground level. There is also a one-time guaranteed chest containing the Balance magick that shows up at the end of the walkway in the northwest region. If you chain 20 Urutan, you can also spawn the rare game Megabomb.

**Simoon Bluff** is the only other area with gear in chests, most of it may be better than what you currently have. Be careful when you enter as there are many traps that can be difficult to avoid. On the backside of the further circular platform, up to three chests can spawn. One may hold a Chopper (strong dagger that causes Sap on hit), one a Lohengrin (sword), and a guaranteed one-time chest will hold the Embroidered Tippet accessory. On the ground level, the map looks sort of like a 3 with an area attached at the top. At the top left tip of the 3 pattern, 2 chests can spawn with chances to hold a Soldier's Hat (light helmet) and a Chanter's Djebella (mystic armor). In the western hump of the area above the 3 is a chest that may contain a Sirius gun. The furthest area can have up to three chests, among which you may find a Lambent Hat (mystic hat), a Heavy Coat (light armor), and a one-time guaranteed chest with 2918 gil. This is also the area where the Wyvern Lord will appear, if you have that hunt active.

#### A Scream from the Sky

> ##### Hunt: Wyvern Lord
>
>
> | **Level** | 18 | **LP** | 8 | **CP** | 1440 |
> | **HP** | 18669 | **Steal** | Yensa Scale (55/80%), Crooked Fang (10/30%), <br>Wyvern Wing (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Earth |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Wind |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Reverse, Sleep, Blind, Immobilize, Slow, Libra |
>
> The Wyvern Lord is flying, so you cannot attack it with short range weapons. You must use ranged weapons, poles, spears, magick, quickenings, items, or technicks like Telekinesis. The advantage of taking on this hunt now is that you have Vossler in your party, who can use Telekinesis. The disadvantage is that this guy can be tough. You can give yourself an edge by equipping Windbreakers. Its damage output increases when it drops below 50% health. It uses the Breath attack once when its HP drops below 20%. This has the potential of inflicting Sap and can do over 500 damage to everyone it hits.
>
> You also have an opportunity to get a powerful gun fairly early, if you are willing to keep attempting to steal and re-zoning until you get the Wyvern Wing. Two of those along with 1 Salamand Halcyon (from the Salamand Entite) can be combined with 2 Yensa Fin to craft the Arcturus in the Bazaar (called Late-Model Rifle). There are only two guns more powerful, so if you like guns and want a strong one early, now's your chance - if you don't mind a ridiculous amount of grinding to steal).
>
> Other than that, Wyvern Lord doesn't have an immunity to sleep. Putting it to sleep and then using magick to damage it is an easy way to finish it off, but keep in mind that physical damage will wake it up. Or you can just pound away with ranged weapons and keep your health up with Cura or Hi-Potions.

You'll probably want to wait before returning to Sherral. For the time being, collect all the gear you can from **Simoon Bluff** since it probably costs much more than you have. Eventually, enemies will stop spawning as you use the exit to reset the chests. Once you are fully equipped, head back to the **Trail of Fading Warmth** and find the merchant Dyce in the northwest area. Sell any loot you've picked up (without selling Great Serpentskin). This may unlock some new bazaar goods.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Yensa Scale (x1)<br>Green Liquid (x3)<br>Water Stone (x4) | Rain of Tears | Aqua Shot<br>Vega | 800 gil | 1400 gil |
| Bent Staff (x3) | Large Gloves | Blazer Gloves | 2000 gil | 1000 gil |

You can also purchase the best gear yet available from merchants, but most of it you should have already if you farmed the chests. You can also pick up the new magick Stop. If you have the funds, now would be a good time to purchase the Warmage's Monograph for 20,000 gil from the bazaar. Some of the enemies in the upcoming areas can have better drops now, but you may have already noticed that useless pebbles have started dropping from some enemies as well. This is why it's best to wait to purchase these until you first reach the area where the drops improve.

Swap in your best ranged characters and continue west. I'd recommend taking advantage of the save option now.

### Tomb Raider

After the scenic scene, proceed forward toward the tomb until you meet what Dyce has been warning you about.

> ##### Boss: Garuda
>
>
> | **Level** | 16 | **LP** | 11 | **CP** | 1310 |
> | **HP** | 6754 | **Steal** | Gold Needle (55/80%), Giant Feather (10/30%), <br>Lightning Fang (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Dark |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Sleep, Blind, Silence, Disease, Sap, Lure |
>
> If you beat the Wyvern Lord, this battle should be fairly easy. To make it easier, you can toss those Eksir Berries you got from the Urutan Flower. This will cancel the Enrage status, lowering Garuda's attack. It also prevents it from getting to attack without waiting on a charge time. This enemy is ranged, however, so anyone using a short range weapon will not be able to hit it with physical attacks. If you are really struggling, keep in mind its weakness to darkness and throw some dark motes at it from your character doing the least damage. Other than that, just another straightforward boss that shouldn't take too much to knock out of the sky.

After the battle, there will be another scene. You can then do some more shopping (stock up on Handkerchiefs) with Dyce who followed you in and save using the Teleport Crystal. If you're really anxious, you can teleport back to Rabanastre to claim the reward for the Wyvern Lord, but there will be a more convenient time later. Instead, touch the Way Stone to proceed.

#### Hall of the Destroyer

After the scene and learning almost nothing about Way Stones, there is another Way Stone to investigate in front of you. Go down the stairs to the right or left of the second Way Stone. As you begin making your way down the hall, you will be attacked from behind by an optional boss. You can run to the far door and avoid this battle, or you can attempt to fight it.

> ##### Optional Boss: Demon Wall
>
>
> | **Level** | 18 | **LP** | 30 | **CP** | 1350 |
> | **HP** | 22187 | **Steal** | Gold Needle (55/80%), Solid Stone (10/30%), <br>Solid Stone (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | N/A |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Blind, Oil, Slow |
>
> This is designed to be a fight you shouldn't typically win. You must defeat it in a short period of time before it crushes you against the far door. Using my strongest characters (with attack powers of 40, 40, and 42), I still had about 10% of its health left when it crushed me. If I had a quickening on hand, I may have been able to finish it off, but I hadn't spent any LP on quickenings yet. When I swapped out the Lohengrin for the Karkarta, however, I had no problem finishing it off with time to spare. Alternatively, you can manually control Vossler to use the technick Expose, and that should give you time to finish the battle without using any of the overpowered weapons.
>
> This Demon Wall has a slew of nasty attacks, including Telega that simply banishes a player. If it banishes one of your party, you can swap in a new member - but if it banishes Vossler you have to fight down one person. It can also inflict blindness, sleep, doom, and petrify. The battle is too short for doom or petrify to really mean much, but blind and sleep can have the effect of removing someone from battle. The Demon Wall does inflict damage, but so little that a healer won't have much to do. What makes the battle challenging is the time limit, not the threat of being KOed. So use your strongest, fastest attackers and go all out with what little time you have. Your reward for beating this optional boss is access to a hidden area.

You cannot yet access the hidden area, so return to the save crystal and heal up before proceeding. Now use the door that the Demon Wall tried to crush you against.

#### Hall of the Sentinel

The moment you enter the area, regardless of whether you fought the first Demon Wall or fled, you get the surprise of having another Demon Wall bearing down on you. No choice this time, you must fight.

> ##### Boss: Demon Wall
>
>
> | **Level** | 16 | **LP** | 9 | **CP** | 1350 |
> | **HP** | 10332 | **Steal** | Gold Needle (55/80%), Solid Stone (10/30%), <br>Quality Stone (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | N/A |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Blind, Oil, Slow |
>
> This Demon Wall is much more manageable. You have far more time, it has less than half the HP, and its spells are weaker - although it can do pretty much everything the first Demon Wall could. If you are concerned about time, you can attempt to halt its progress by touching the Altars of Contemplation. Start with the altars closest to the Demon Wall. Some stop it temporarily, others speed it up.
>
> Equipping one person with the Nishjin belt to protect against sleep and another with the Argyle Armlet to protect against blind can help reduce the amount of status recovery turns you'll need. Again, the Demon Wall doesn't hit too hard, so focus on using your damage dealers instead of your healers or support characters. If you need to, take advantage of Vossler's Expose technick.

Proceed forward, up the stairs, and use the Ancient Doors to access the next area.

#### Royal Passage

| **Treasures** | 5 |
| **Notable Loot** | Scathe Mote, map of The Tomb of Raithwall, Dispel (magick), Vanish (magick) |
| **Enemies** | Seeker, Tallow, Barmuu (rare game) |

After the scene, go down the stairs and find the Map Urn between the back two Way Stones. After grabbing it, interact with the central Way Stone to return to the entrance. If you need to remove any statuses or heal up, head back outside to the Teleport Crystal. If you did not defeat the Demon Wall, skip down to the next paragraph. If you did defeat it, head down to where the Demon Wall spawned and examine the blue jewel to unlock access to a hidden area in **Royal Passage**. Follow the new path along the lower levels until you reach the **Royal Passage**, and then make you way downstairs until you are against the back wall. You will begin encountering Tallows here which cast fire and can inflict the Oil status (increasing the damage dealt by fire). Remove Oil with Handkerchiefs and work your way to the central area along the east wall. There will be a one time guaranteed chest containing a Scathe Mote as your reward for defeating the optional boss. Return to the platform where you found the Map Urn.

If you get a chain of twelve in this area without leaving, you can spawn the rare game Barmuu - an enemy related to a Ragoh that you may soon encounter. It will appear near the map urn. You can steal a Lamia's Tiara (best mystic helmet available at this point), and it has a very small chance to drop a Betelgeuse (one step up from the Sirius you probably have equipped). You also need the enemy for the bestiary, eventually. It has between 9,000 and 10,000 HP, is weak to wind, and uses some high level magicks, and can petrify. Seekers are probably easiest to chain, if you want to face it now.

If you look at the map of the **Royal Passage**, you'll see 4 possible exits, each with a dead end just past the exit. Each of the dead ends could hold a treasure, and two hold guaranteed one-time chests with magicks. From top to bottom, the Dispel magick, a status recovery item, Vanish magick, and in the southernmost dead end another status recovery item. The chests with status recovery items, of course, could also contain gil - but the magicks are guaranteed. You may get your Seeker chain while grabbing the two magicks. Once you grab them all, it doesn't matter which of the next two rooms you go into first, but I'll describe the north room first.

Make your way toward one of the north exits. They both take you to the same place, but via a circular route. The route from the top exit has more treasures - but no guaranteed gear.

#### Northfall Passage

| **Treasures** | 6 |
| **Enemies** | Zombie, Lesser Chimera, Zombie Mage, Ragoh, |

This map is a convoluted loop, with the key event taking place about halfway around. The route that has the exit further south I call the east route (since it primarily takes you on the east side of the map) which is occupied by Zombies and Lesser Chimeras, while the other route is the west route and has Zombies, Lesser Chimeras, and Ragohs. Gear you could find includes a Chanter's Djellaba at the end of the longer dead end by the east route's exit, a Soldier's Cap in the southwest corner of the large backward L-shaped chamber at the end of the west route, and a Flame Staff in the southeast corner of the fourth room along the west route.

The key event takes place in the weird-shaped room in the center of the map. Make your way there and touch the Northfall Pedestal on the south side of the chamber. This will begin to open a passage and spawn three Zombie Mages. After dispatching them, you can use the Way Stone to return to the **Royal Passage** and make your way to one of the southern exits.

#### Southfall Passage

| **Treasures** | 8 |
| **Notable Loot** | Hastega Mote |
| **Enemies** | Zombie, Lesser Chimera, Ragoh, Lich, Lost Soul, Skull Warrior, Cultsworn Lich (rare game) |

This map mirrors the **NorthFall Passage**. So again, I'll refer to the two routes as eastern and western. At the beginning of the eastern route, you can head west to a dead end with a guaranteed one-time chest with a Hastega Mote. You may also find an Ice Shield in the southeast corner of the third room. If you opt for the western route, you can find a Heavy Coat in the southeast corner of the first room, a Lambent Hat in the northwest corner of the fourth room, and a Paramina Crossbow straight south of the central room.

In terms of foes, the both routes have Ragohs, Liches (which can randomly spawn a Cultsworn Lich if allowed to divide), Lost Souls, Lesser Chimeras, and Skull Warriors. In the central chamber, after touching the Southfall Pedestal, three Liches will spawn. There will now be a new path that heads to the west. Follow it to another Ancient Door.

#### Cloister of Flame

| **Treasures** | 4 |
| **Notable Loot** | Blood Sword, Shades of Black (technick), the Dawn Shard |
| **Enemies** | Belias (esper) |

The **Cloister of Flame** has three levels - an outer ring at the highest level, an inner ring at a mid level, and the central ground area. The two chambers coming off the middle ring contain guaranteed one-time chests with a Phoenix Down (north side) and a Blood Sword (south side). On the ground floor are two guaranteed one-time chests with an Elixir (south wall) and Shades of Black technick (north wall). Before you can grab the last two, however, you'll be thrown into your next boss battle.

> ##### Boss: Belias
>
>
> | **Level** | 20 | **LP** | 15 | **CP** | 1350 |
> | **HP** | 15943 | **Steal** | Aries Gem (55/80%), Ether (10/30%), <br>High Arcana (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Water |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Fire<br>\[Half Damage\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Sap, Slow |
>
> Belias hits a bit harder than what you've seen so far, and has a potentially devastating Firaja attack that deals massive fire damage to everyone nearby. It's a good idea to have someone with a gambit to remove oil with a Handkerchief. You can use "Ally:any" and it will only activate if an ally has the Oil status. For healing, either have someone that has a gambit set up to cast Cura when an ally's health drops below 40% (or 50% if your party can't survive Firaja at 40% health), or have someone set to toss Hi-Potions at an appropriate health level.
>
> At half health, Belias gets a boost. He will cast Greater Barrier, essentially halving your damage, his damage output will increase, his attacks will hit more successfully, and it will not cost him anything to cast his magick. He will use Firaja for the first time immediately following Great Barrier, so manually heal if you need to when you see him cast Great Barrier. He will start using Firaja after every dozen attacks, as well. Be prepared to heal if needed, and keep enduring until he falls.

Congratulations on obtaining your first Esper! You can teach one character the Esper, which may unlock new areas on the license boards (Libra for the Bushi, Horology for the Foebreaker, and Potion Lore 1 for the Knight). You can also now unlock a second job for each of your characters. There's no urgency to do it immediately, so you may want to wait until you are back in Rabanastre and you can use Montblanc to reset things if you pick an option you decide you don't like.

For now, use the Ancient Door in front of you to the west. Skip the Way Stone and head up the stairs for a scene. Now that you got what you came for, you can use the Way Stone you just passed. This takes you all the way back to the first area, where you can use the nearby Way Stone to leave the **Tomb of Raithwall**. Be sure to save at the Teleport Crystal, but you won't be teleporting this time. Head down the stairs toward the **Nam-Yensa Sandsea**.

In the middle of the scenes, you get a boss battle. Can you guess who you get to fight?

> ##### Boss: Vossler
>
> | **Level** | 20 | **LP** | 30 | **CP** | 1260 |
> | **HP** | 9318 | **Steal** | Potion (55/80%), Hi-Potion (10/30%), <br>Black Belt (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | N/A |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | N/A |
> | **Status Susceptibiliities** | Stop, Confuse, Sleep, Sap, Slow, Berserk, Libra |
>
> Vossler is accompanied by three Imperial Swordsmen. He isn't much threat, but he tends to combo more when his health drops. Have Cure or Hi-Potion gambits ready, but this should be easier than Belias even though you have one less party member.

## Walkthrough Part 3 - Forging an Alliance

Now that you are back in Rabanastre and a key event just took place, there is a lot you can do before progressing the story. For starters, here are a few odds and ends you should take care of before running off.

| **Hunt Completed** | Wyvern Lord |
| **Rewards** | 1000 Gil<br>Elfin Bow (x1)<br>Shell Shield (x1) |

First, if you killed the Wyvern Lord, be sure to speak with Sherral in Amal's Weaponry in **East End** to let him know and claim some very nice rewards. You can now buy the Traveler technick for 4800, if you don't plan on getting it from Trial Mode stage 31. You should also sell off your loot (don't sell the Great Serpentskin!). If there's anything you hadn't unlocked before, it may unlock now. I got three items to unlock that probably should have happened earlier, but I was lacking the Foul Flesh. Iron-Forged Blade and Rain of Tears have been mentioned earlier, but a new one was the Light Spear.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Horn (x2)<br>Foul Flesh (x2)<br>Wind Stone (x3) | Light Spear | Javelin | 310 gil | 190 gil |

Now that you've turned in your hunt, you should see Montblanc in the Clan Hall in **North End**. The first thing he'll probably do is offer a reward for reaching the rank Vanguard with _Hi-Potions (x3)_ and _Teleport Stones (x2)._ Then, for defeating the Demon Wall, _1200 gil_ and _a nugget of Electrum_. For the second Demon Wall, you get _600 gil_ and _a Warp Mote_. The rewards are given based on the order you beat them or not - so if you only beat one Demon Wall you will only get the second prize. For gaining control of your first esper, Montblanc hands over _Arcanas (x2)_ and _Teleport Stones (x2)_. Arcana is used to eventually create some of the most powerful weapons in the game so I recommend holding onto it until you are ready to make High Arcana. The Electrum you can sell as it is only used in one bazaar item. After getting all your rewards, pick up the hunt for the Gil Snapper.

|  | **1st Job** | **2nd Job** |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Vaan** | Shikari | Foebreaker |
| **Penelo** | Black Mage | Monk |
| **Balthier** | Machinist | White Mage |
| **Fran** | Archer | Red Battlemage |
| **Basch** | Knight | Bushi |
| **Ashe** | Uhlan | Time Battlemage |

While you are standing next to Montblanc, now's the chance to unlock second boards. There really is no one right way to do this as everything is fairly overpowered. I wanted to use all of the jobs in one playthrough, and I liked trying to match everyone's first job to what they seemed to be in the game. In the end, you can see what I settled on to the right. I had to clear Penelo from White Mage which had only been selected out of necessity earlier. The rest were already ready to select their second job. I also had to immediately spend a lot of LP from Balthier on learning the white magicks to get him up to speed. Also, try to get Charge for any of your magick users, especially your White Mage.

You'll see when you get a second job that many of the licenses are already unlocked. These are licenses shared by the two boards. Obviously, the less two boards have in common, the more benefits you get. Also, giving each of your mages a second job that does good physical attacks and provides a lot of HP really helps them be participants in fights without having to constantly be healed. There are numerous extensive guides on job combinations. Be aware that they don't always use the PS4 release names for the jobs, but use the International (Bushi vs. Samurai). As such, you may have to do some "translation". Most make sense, though.

Finally, a whole bunch of sidequests and hunts have become available. If you want to just get on with it, click [here](#walkthrough-part-3---forging-an-alliance) to jump forward. Otherwise, keep reading if you want to do some sidequests. Be aware that helping July in Nalbina cannot be completed if you progress the story too far, but most of the others can be completed at any time. Even if you don't plan on doing sidequests, though, you probably have a couple of miscellaneous tasks to finish up.

### OPTIONAL Sidequest Central

There are several hunts and sidequests to begin now. Here is a list to jump directly to them.

1. Answer Viera [Ktjn's next question](#walkthrough-part-1---the-occupied-city).
2. [Getting the Thief's Cuffs](#getting-the-thiefs-cuffs)
3. [Picking up hunts in Rabanstre and starting the Rimzat's sandstorm investigation.](#walkthrough-part-3---forging-an-alliance)
4. Starting [July the Streetear](#july-the-streetear).
5. Concluding [Patient in the Desert.](#patient-in-the-desert)
6. Finding and selling [Bhujerban Madhu.](#bhujerban-madhu)
7. Completing the Croakadile hunt, [A Ring in the Rain.](#a-ring-in-the-rain)

#### Getting the Thief's Cuffs

This is a little out of the way and you have to run through some enemies way too tough for you to get them...but if you want to get this accessory as early as possible, here's your first opportunity. Before now, the way was blocked. My recommendation is to turn off any speed boosts and hold down L2 and R2 through this entire section. The enemies get much more difficult very suddenly, and you don't want to get stuck unable to move precisely.

To start, head to **Westgate** and exit into **Dalmasca Westersand**. Head straight across **Galtea Downs** to **The Midfault**, and turn immediately south into **Shimmering Horizons**. In the southwest corner, there is a descent into a new area: **The Zertinan Caverns**. Wind your way southwest through **Invitation to Heresy**. If you've been here before, you may think there is no exit since there has never been one before. At this point, however, the southwest "dead-end" now gives access to the next zone, **Sandfalls**. Head straight south into **Hourglass Basin**. Just in front of you is a boulder you can strike to give you access to the next portion of the zone. Be very careful, however, as this is where the enemy strength goes up suddenly. You need to run past the Buers and take the southwest exit into **The Undershore**. If anybody got knocked out by the Buers, you will at least have a Save Crystal to replenish your health. There are no enemies in this zone, so you can relax.

#### Picking up Hunts, begining Rimzat's Sandstorm Investigation

Head to the Sandsea to pick up new hunts. There should be five to grab, but some may be a bit too tough for us. Grab them all anyway. Speak to the Tavernmaster behind you to kick off the hunt for Marilith.

There are two more petitioners to find while still in Rabanastre before you start questing. Use the moogling outside the Sandsea to reach the **Westgate**. Sorbet, the petitioner for the White Mousse hunt (who says intrigue can't have puns?), is the moogling attendant.

While at **Westgate**, look for a Bangaa named Rimzat standing with his pack, looking forlornly out into the desert. Agree to help him, then head back into the **Southern Plaza** and talk to Cotze, sitting on the fountain. Now, head down to _**Lowtown**_ **/ North Sprawl**. Northon is crouched, talking to a Bangaa, on the east side of the path, south a small distance from the **Garamsythe Waterway** entrance. Before looking for the missing piece, there is a hunt petitioner nearby. Make your way to the west side of **North Sprawl** to find the petitioner Balzac sitting on some crates at the southernmost dead end.

Now that we have a bunch of hunts and tasks, let's completely ignore them - for now. All of them are very high level or are made much easier with magick that isn't yet available. If you want to try your hand now, they are covered [later in the guide](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire). Instead, make your way to **Nalbina** **Fortress** either via **Estersand** or a Teleport Crystal to take care of some other business.

#### July the Streetear

When you get to **Nalbina**, you will see a woman crouched while watching the two Imperials guarding the far exit. Approach the Imperials to listen in less discretely. Head back behind you to the chocobo attendant moogle, Gurdy. Ask to rent a chocobo for 800 gil. Ride the chocobo up to the Imperials. Now that you've ruined some poor guy's life, you can now do two important things. You can pick up a reward later in the game, and more immediately you have access to the **Mosphoran Highwaste**. This gives you a back door to collect the Valeblossom Dew needed by Dantro's Wife.

#### Patient in the Desert

If you haven't gotten this quest started, you should still be able to by following along with the instructions found [earlier](#walkthrough-part-1---the-occupied-city). If you are caught up, it's time to enter the **Mosphoran Highwaste**.

| **Treasures** | 5 ( **Southern Skirts**), 7 ( **Summit Path**) |
| **Enemies** | Worgen, Slaven Wilder, Humbaba, Fire Elemental, Wary Wolf (rare game) |

This area is another terrific farming place for levels and gil, but the enemies may still be tough around level 30. Use the Teleport Crystal to save your game before attempting the Dew recovery. Also have a couple of characters set up with the gambit "Ally: any, Nu Khai Sand" since the Worgens can cause confuse. You also may want "Foe: water-weak, Aqua" to help take the Worgens down more quickly. Move into the **Southern Skirts** from Nalbina. You will want to stay closer to the northern wall so you can avoid most of the enemies in the next area. Both exits lead to **Summit Path**, but the eastern exit is safer.

Once in **Summit Path**, there is a chance to spawn the rare game Wary Wolf. Unless you have been doing some serious grinding, you probably cannot take it down with normal gear. It has a fairly large patrol and will be difficult to avoid. When you come to the first fork, stay to the north. The little alcove to the north of the fork can have two chests, one of which might hold a Kiku-ichimonji, which would be a huge improvement for anyone using katanas. It might be worth zoning in and out until you get it. There is also a chest with a Golden Staff, but you will have to fight Humbabas to get to it. It is on the west side of the "doughnut hole" in the middle of the map. Your ultimate destination is the southwest exit, but try to weave your way so that you don't attract the Humbabas. You always have the option of simply fleeing. This southern exit will take you into the **Estersand**.

| **Treasures** | 13 |
| **Notable Loot** | Valeblossom Dew (x3), Berserker Bracers, Barheim Key |
| **Enemies** | Worgen, Wild Saurian, Dive Talon, Slaven Wilder, Bangaa Pugilist |

While there is some gear here, I don't recommend attempting to grab too much as this area is still fairly high level compared to what you probably are. The nice thing is that almost everything here is docile except the Worgens and the Wild Saurian - so if you feel confident, go for it. The gear may include a Viking Coat, Bangle, Dragon Shield, Diamond Helm, and a guaranteed one-time chest with an Berserker Bracers just a bit southwest of where you came in (the middle dead-end). After grabbing the bracers, head to the westmost dead-end and find a column of Weathered Rocks that you can knock down. Use this new path to bypass the Wild Saurian on the east side and look for what looks like a cherry tree on the cliff to the southeast. When you approach it, you will get the Mysterious Glint notification that will give you a Valeblossom Dew once you interact with it. Just to the east of the tree you can knock over more Weathered Rock to create a shortcut to the other two Valeblossom trees. The other two are growing out of the same crevasse as the first, only on the east side further south. Once you have all three, return to **Nalbina** the same way you came. From there, use the Teleport Crystal to head to Dalmasca Estersand.

Speak to the boy Tchigri on the dock and agree to help travel to the other bank. Talk to Ruksel, now. Talk to Dantro's wife, then check behind the hut to interact with Dran?? Head back to the dock to talk with Tchigri and head to the other side of the river. Approach the cactuar family. You'll be rewarded with _1000 gil_ and _a pouch of Wyrmfire Shot_.

| ##### Cartographer's Guild Moogle - _Dalmasca Estersand: North Bank Village_ |
| **Area** | **Cost** |
| Mosphoran Highwaste | 2400 gil |

Now that that is done, we can finish up the Patient quest. There is a Cartographers' Guild moogle selling a map to the Mosphoran Highwaste. If you can afford it, go ahead and grab it. Step into **The Yoma** just to be sure to trigger it for the cartography title later. If you grabbed the Valeblossom Dew already, you can head back to the South Bank. Otherwise, make your way through **The Yoma** to **Broken Sands**.

Back in the **South Bank Village**, speak to Dantro's wife to deliver the Valeblossom Dew. She'll be snooping while you remove it from inventory and ask for the Great Serpentskin, assuming you haven't accidentally sold it. Hand it over, walk out of the village, and come back in. Speak to Dantro's Wife and then head behind the hut to meet the patient you've been helping. He'll hand over the Barheim Key and a reward that is dependent on how much you helped. If you did everything, this should be your second Golden Amulet - which should always be equipped on one of your non-active party members to accelerate your LP gains. If you did well but not perfect, you'll get Magick Gloves. If you did the minimum or just barely more, you'll receive a Balance Mote.

#### Bhujerban Madhu

The next sidequest will get you a bit of gil and ensure that you've seen every area in Bhujerba. Each region and building has a bottle of Bhujerban Madhu and a person willing to buy it for 1000 gil. You can sell to the same person more than once, so you don't have to utilize all the buyers. There are 14 total bottles - 6 in the streets, 8 in the buildings. You can use this table to find everything:

| **Building Areas** | **Bottle Location** | **Buyer Location** |  | **Street Areas** | **Bottle Location** | **Buyer Location** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Bhujerba Aerodrome | On some crates at the northeast corner of the north/south hall | Sitting on rail in conversation with Imperial to the southwest of where you found the bottle |  | Travica Way | On ground beside a man crouched talking to a boy near the southeast exit | Man standing by wall across the street from Mait's Magicks |
| Rithil's Protectives | Inside partially open cabinet on the left before you get to the counter | Shop Clerk in front of the counter |  | Khus Skygrounds | On crate next to moogle, just north of the merchant. | Bhujerban standing by herself on west side |
| Targe's Arms | On the crates in a corner of the upper level near the polearms | Woman on lower level by crossbow display |  | Miner's End | On crates by Bashketi's Gambits | Lhusu miner Bangaa at the intersection south of Bashketi's Gambits |
| Mait's Magick | Upper level on table with large vases | Bhujerban Guru right next to the table that the bottle was on. |  | Kaff Terrace | On the right side of the bridge leading to the terrace, behind a Seeq | Bhujerban woman looking like she's going to jump from the north wing of the terrace |
| Clio's Technicks | On a stack of books just to the left of the shop counter | Shop clerk standing on the rug in front of the entrance |  | Lhusu Square | At the base of the draining water just before entering the mine | Lhusu miner sitting behind the Seeq merchant |
| Bashketi's Gambits | On a bookshelf in the upper level | Seeq shop clerk on the upper level |  | Cloudborne Row | On crate in the far south dead-end | Cloudborne Patron standing outside the tavern |
| The Cloudborne | On a table with two patrons, one with her head on the table. | The Seeq Magu |  |  |  |  |
| The Staras Residence | Behind a large dresser to the right of the entrance | Niray, but only after completing the Antlion hunt |  |  |  |  |

Amazingly, getting everyone drunk with liquor you just find laying around that many were just too lazy to pick up on their own has absolutely no impact on the story. I wonder if this was meant to impede some Resistance recruiting efforts that got scrapped from the game. Regardless, you are now 14,000 gil richer and have explored every map in Bhujerba. Many people tend to miss **Kaff Terrace** when trying to get the Cartographer trophy. While you're at it, now might be a good time to check to make sure you've set foot in all of the areas in the Sandseas, the Westersand, the Estersand, and Giza Plains. The more careful you are now, the less you'll have to hunt for a missing location later.

#### A Ring in the Rain

Next, let's actually take care of one of the hunts. Head to **Rabanastre / Southgate**, and then move into the **Giza Plains**. It will be laid out a little differently due to the rain.

| **Treasures** | 4 ( **Throne Road**), 1 ( **Nomad Village**), 5 ( **Gizas North Bank**), 6 ( **Gizas South Bank**), 6 ( **Warrior's Wash**), 6 ( **Toam Hills**), 7 ( **Starfall Field**), 7 ( **Tracks of the Beast**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Regen (magick), Ring of the Toad |
| **Enemies** | Hyena, Gigantoad, Ichthon, Great Tortoise, Storm Elemental, Rain Dancer (rare game), Wooly Gator, Croakadile (mark), Mardu Entite, Silicon Tortoise, Gil Snapper (mark) |

Head through **Throne Road** to get to the **Nomad Village**. Open the one-time guaranteed chest in the cockatrice pen to get Regen magick. Speak with Sadeen to initiate the Croakadile hunt.

While there is gear spread through the **Giza Plains** during the wet season, none of it is in the areas we are taking on right now. Head west out of the **Nomad Village** into **Toam Hills**. Make your way to the south exit into **Starfall Fiel** **d**. This area has a river cutting the area in two. The Croakadile is on the south side across the bridge. He'll jump out and surprise you when you get close.

> ##### Hunt: Croakadile
>
>
> | **Level** | 24 | **LP** | 10 | **CP** | 1440 |
> | **HP** | 19449 | **Steal** | Horn (55/80%), Pointed Horn (10/30%), Aries Gem (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Fire |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Water<br>\[Half Damage\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Reverse, Blind, Silence, Disease, Immobilize, Slow |
>
> Bring along someone with white magick Dispel and Cura and this is a breeze, even if you're a few levels below the Croakadile. Since it has Shell and Bravery on and opens with Angel Song, cast Dispel to start the battle. It likes to try to keep Regen status on. As soon as you see it begin to cast Angel Song, have someone cast Dispel. It will waste a bunch of turns reattempting to apply Regen, while your other two characters pound away. It will use Growing Threat to increase its level at 40% HP, and will use Aqua Bubbles at 20% HP - but neither are particularly dangerous. With an average party level of 21 and HP between 1880 and 2500 for everyone, I never even dropped even close to 50% health. I used Fran to cast Dispel, had Basch as a Knight/Bushi with the recently acquired Kiku-ichimonji and Ashe as an Uhlan/Time Battlemage wielding a Heavy Lance.

| **Hunt Completed** | Croakadile |
| **Rewards** | 1200 Gil<br>Serpent Rod (x1)<br>Teleport Stone (x1) |

After the battle, you find the Ring of the Toad. Return to Sadeen in the **Nomad Village**. Sadeen leaves you with the ring and asks you to deliver it. That will have to wait until later.

### Seeking the Garif

Whether you've done the sidequests above or not, the next step in the story is to find the Garif. In general, this means crossing the **Giza Plains** south, and working your way to the southwest corner of the **Ozmone Plains**. If you just finished the sidequest section, you are already in the Giza Plains. If not, teleport to **_Rabanastre_ / Southgate** and head south.

#### Giza Plains - the Rainy Season

| **Treasures** | 4 ( **Throne Road**), 1 ( **Nomad Village**), 5 ( **Gizas North Bank**), 6 ( **Gizas South Bank**), 6 ( **Warrior's Wash**), 6 ( **Toam Hills**), 7 ( **Starfall Field**), 7 ( **Tracks of the Beast**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Regen (magick) |
| **Enemies** | Hyena, Gigantoad, Ichthon, Great Tortoise, Storm Elemental, Rain Dancer (rare game), Wooly Gator, Mardu Entite, Silicon Tortoise, Gil Snapper (mark) |

While exploring **Giza Plains**, look for Withered Trees. Each section (including **Nomad Village** and **Crystal Glade**) has one except **Warrior's Wash** and **Gizas South Bank**:

1. **Throne Road** \- on the west bank near the entrance to **Nomad Village**.
2. **Nomad Village** \- south of the cockatrice pen.
3. **Gizas North Bank** \- north end of the east bank.
4. **Toam Hills** \- northeast corner of the small area with the exit to **Starfall Field**.
5. **Starfall Field** \- just south of the exit to **Toam Hills**.
6. **Crystal Glade** \- north bank.

Once you have knocked all of them over, a tree bridge will form connecting **Gizas South Bank** to the previously inaccessible **Tracks of the Beast**. This is necessary to take on the Gil Snapper hunt - which is probably still a little out of your league.

In terms of treasure, some areas only have basic restorative items, but some have decent finds. First, if you didn't pick up the Regen magick from **Nomad Village** while hunting the Croakadile, be sure to grab that.

In **Gizas North Bank**, the treasure barrels in the two southwest dead-ends have either a weapon (one has Iron Pole, the other Slasher) or an ether if it's not gil. Near the exit to **Throne Road** you might find a Gravity Mote. On the path to the exit to **Gizas South Bank** might be a barrel with a Balance Mote.

In **Gizas South Bank**, five of the six treasure could contain motes (Dark, Bio, Aqua, or Balance). Of the three on the north side, the barrel in the western dead end may contain a Golden Helm. From there, you should be able to see a barrel on the south side that may contain a Leather Gorget, if it appeared.

**Warrior's Wash** contains three chests that may have gear: the Rose Corsage accessory at the end of the north path, a Feathered Cap at the end of the west path, and a Green Beret at the end of the southwest path.

Other than a Bio Mote in **Toam Hills** and a Float Mote and Gravity Mote in **Starfall Field**, there isn't anything worth spending time on unless you want to brave **Tracks of the Beast**. Of the seven barrels, six could have gear and one may have an Aqua or Shock Mote. The possible gear includes Flametongue, Survival Vest, Traveler's Vestment, Magoroku, Main Gauche, and Golden Armor. Unless you found the Gladius in the **Estersand**, the Kiku-ichimonji in the **Mosphoran Highwaste**, and the Karkata in Trial Mode, the weapons should be stronger than what you already have.
