The armors may also be better than what you already have as well, but most can also be found in the upcoming region. The Silicon Tortoises present have levels in the mid-30s, and by the time you are at that level you will probably have gear at least that good.

When ready to move on, find the south exit from **Starfall Field** to enter the

#### Ozmone Plain

| **Treasures** | 9 ( **Field of the Fallen Wings**), 7 ( **Dagan Flats**), 8 ( **The Switchback**), 11 ( **Haulo Green**), 3 ( **The Greensnake**), 2 ( **Field of Light Winds**), 9 ( **The Shred**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Jaya Stick |
| **Enemies** | Mesmenir, Wu, Viper, Hybrid Gator, Ozmone Hare, Red Chocobo, Black Chocobo, Zu, Zaghnal, Sylphi Entite, Garif Adventurer, Bull Croc (rare game), Aeros (rare game), Enkelados (mark) |

If you're obsessive about finding gear and motes, there are a few places in the **Ozmone Plain** to make some acquisitions. In **Field of the Fallen Wings**, a treasure barrel that may contain a Heavy Lance could be found on the south edge on the way to the east exit. On the northwest side of the ring-like portion on the west side of the map, you may find a barrel with a Dispel Mote or Chainmail. You may also find a Dark Mote straight north of the southern exit. **Dagan Flats** and **The Greensnake** only have restorative items. **The Swtichback** may have an Aqua Mote on the west edge, a Chopper on the switchback leading to **Zertinan Caverns**, Poison Bombs on the ledge just above the Chopper, and a Cura mote on the north edge slightly to the east of the middle exit. **Haulo Green** has two chests of interest. One on the west wall a bit south of the entrance may contain Silent Shot. The other chest is on the west side of the second-smallest hole in the map, and may contain Parallel Arrows or a Tourmaline Ring. The two chests in **Field of Light Winds** can contain a Slasher and a Traveler's Vestment. **The Shred** is split into two areas, one of which is only accessible by chocobo. The northern section that you can walk to may have a Dispel Mote on the west wall, Long Bolts on the east wall, and an Aqua Mote in the southwest corner. The chocobo-only region can have a Float Mote in the northwest corner, a Green Beret in the middle of the area, and a Flame Tongue in the southwest corner. You can spawn the rare game Bull Croc by defeating all the enemies in the northern section, and the rare game Aeros by defeating the Bull Croc.

| ##### Cartographer's Guild Moogle - _Jahara: Banks of the Sogoht_ |
| **Area** | **Cost** |
| Ozmone Plain | 1700 gil |
| Jahara | 30 gil |

When ready to move ahead with the story, take the southwest exit from **Haulo Green**. Purchase the maps from the Cartographer's Guild moogle standing by the chocobo stables and save your game (or at least touch the Teleport Crystal to activate a new destination for quick travel). Speak to the Garif Warrior guarding the bridge for a scene. Afterwards, wander around talking to the Garif labeled Village Elder until you are told to speak with High-chief Zayalu who is speaking with Geomancer Yugelu. He will hand over a Jaya Stick. Deliver it to War-chief Supinelu speaking with the bridge guards in the northwest corner. Ask to meet with the Great-chief. Cross the bridge and approach the Garif in front of you. Continue to request a meeting and find that it was not as difficult as everyone made it seem.

After the scenes, you will be given a Recurve Crossbow and a quiver of Onion Bolts by Supinelu. You also can get one free ride on the chocobos - but I would hold off on that for now. Before doing anything else, you've probably accumulated quite a bit of loot. There is a merchant back in **Jahara** that you can sell to, which may unlock some new bazaar goods. Be careful not to sell your Arcana or Teleport Stones, so save in advance if you need to.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Throat Wolf Blood (x1) | Back Harness | Battle Harness | 600 gil | 400 gil |
| Braid Wool (x2)<br>Tanned Hide (x2)<br>Water Stone (x5) | Traveler's Garb | Feathered Cap<br>Traveler's Vestment | 1890 gil | 1910 gil |
| Chocobo Feather (x4) | Eye Openers | Phoenix Down (x5)<br>Prince's Kiss (x5) | 1280 gil | 30 gil more expensive... |
| Iron Carapace (x3)<br>Tanned Hide (x2)<br>Dark Magicite (x3) | Golden Garb | Golden Helm<br>Golden Armor<br>Golden Shield | 3980 gil | 4020 gil |

The merchant also may have some new magick for you, including your first -ra black magick Fira. You may also want to begin buying the remaining monographs. The 19,000 gil one is most handy now, the 25,000 gil least. If you followed my instructions before, the first 22,000 monograph listed is the one to purchase first - Dragoon's Monograph. If you feel the need to beef up your armor, be sure to keep some gil in reserve. You should be better equipped for weapons than anything the merchant has to offer.

Now, you can proceed with the story or you can knock out another hunt while you have a guest party member. There is also a sidequest that will net you a bow that's probably stronger than what you have (other than the Seitengrat). If you want to skip these, click [here](#walkthrough-part-3---forging-an-alliance).

### OPTIONAL Enkelados Hunt, Viera and the Lovestruck Man

#### The Defense of Ozmone Plain

Start off by heading back into **Jahara / The Elderknoll**. You can check your clan primer to find the exact location of the petitioner. He is Low-chief Sugumu, all the way in the north portion of the westernmost region of **Jahara**.

Leave **Jahara** and make your way north out of **Haulo Green**. Take the east exit in **The Switchback**, then turn right in **The Greensnake** to reach **The Shred**. When you come in, the mark will not be there. Kill everything in the northern area, exit, and immediately reenter. Your Mark should now be in the middle of the northern area.

> ##### Hunt: Enkelados
>
>
> | **Level** | 22 | **LP** | 9 | **CP** | 1210 |
> | **HP** | 18709 | **Steal** | Potion (55/80%), Earth Magicite (10/30%), Slaven Harness (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Wind |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Earth<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Stop, Confuse, Sleep, Blind, Silence, Oil, Disease, Disable, Immobilize, Sap, Slow, Berserk, Libra |
>
> As before, Dispel and Cura are a must. Enkelados starts the battle with some protective statuses, like Shell and Protect - and may also reapply them. If you plan on using magick damage, use the gambit "Foe: status = Shell, Dispel". If you plan on using primarily physical damage, use the gambit "Foe: status = Protect" instead. Keeping those off is a huge help. This is also one of the first marks that will heal itself, but it only does so three times. It becomes harder to damage and deals more damage as its HP drops, so be ready with a couple of people with Cura gambits. While Enkelados resists fewer status ailments than most hunts and bosses, it is able to remove them with Purify. Equipping weapons that cause status ailments is a way to do damage and cause Enkelados to waste turns with Purify - but using your highest damage weapons is still probably the way to go.

| **Hunt Completed** | Enkelados |
| **Rewards** | 1100 Gil<br>Ether (x1)<br>Golden Amulet (x1) |

When the battle is finished, you will receive an Ermonea Leaf. Return to Sugumu for your reward. Now that you have three Golden Amulets, you should always keep them equipped on whoever isn't in your active party.

#### The Lovestruck Man

Either hike back to Rabanastre or use the Teleport Crystal. Speak to the Viera looking at the chocobo stables in **Southgate**. Head into the **Southern Plaza** and look for a lovestruck man you may have noticed earlier on the north side of the fountain. He tells you to look for the Viera in the **East End**. Go all the way to **North End** and find her reclining against a pillar in Yamoora's Gambits. She's now called Wandering Viera. Based on the dialogue, you may have already guessed her next destination: the Sandsea in **East End**. Head upstairs to find her sitting on the railing. Speak to her twice and then go find the Lovestruck Man again. Agree to help him and return to the Sandsea to inform the Viera. After telling her, find the two back at the fountain and speak to them again. Now that he's settling down, he gives you his Loxley Bow and two Hi-Potions. Not too bad for a bow upgrade. Now you can teleport back to **Jahara** to continue with the main quest.

### Finding a Path through Golmore

Head out of **Jahara** and through **Haulo Green**. Take either the east of the north central exit from **The Switchback** into **The Greensnake** or **Dagan Flats** respectively. Continue to the northeast to reach **Field of Light Winds** and pass through. After the scene, heal at the Save Crystal and continue east.

#### Golmore Jungle - Closed to Through Traffic

| **Treasures** | 1 ( **Paths of Chained Light**), 4 ( **The Needlbrake**) |
| **Notable Loot** | map of Golmore Jungle |
| **Enemies** | Panther, Gargoyle, Marlboro, Treant, Midgardsormr (rare game) |

It is possible to spawn the rare game Midgardsormr in **Paths of Chained Light**, but it only spawns when you get close. It may take a few rezoning attempts if you are determined to encounter it now. To begin, head forward and take a left at the first intersection after clearing out the panthers. Take your first right down the stairs. After clearing out the Marlboros, there will be a map urn at the next intersection. There may also be a chest slightly to the right containing a Burgonet, which is probably the best heavy helmet you've encountered so far. Head back east and take a right at the end to head toward the next zone.

In **The Needlebrake**, you can advance the story by following the path to the east, but if you want some treasure be sure to go west first. Go south at the first opportunity and turn back east. There will be a small platform that can have up to three treasure barrels holding mainly gil and ether, but a chance to get a Mage's Hat. Continue east and turn right at the end to a nearby dead-end that may host a barrel with a Red Cap. Now head back north to trigger the scene.

#### Eruyt Village

| **Treasures** | 2 ( **The Spiritwood**), 1 ( **Fane of the Path**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Haste (magick) |

| ##### Cartographer's Guild Moogle -   _Eruyt Village: Road of Verdant Praise_ |
| **Area** | **Cost** |
| Eruyt Village | 60 gil |

After receiving instructions from Fran, speak with the moogle Lulluce to purchase a map of the Eruyt Village. The moogle merchant Tristan has some really good gear, but first be sure to purchase the magick Esuna since there are a lot of status inflicting monsters in this area. You may also want to sell loot and see if you can get some of the gear at a discount before upgrading.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Coeurl Pelt (x3)<br>Quality Hide (x2)<br>Dark Magicite (x3) | Matching Reds | Red Cap<br>Brigandine | 2480 gil | 2520 gil |
| Malboro Vine (x4) | Smelling Salts | Hi-Potion (x4)<br>Nu Khai Sand (x2) | 540 gil | 280 gil |
| Lumber (x2)<br>Malboro Vine (x2)<br>Fire Stone (x6) | Burning Blade | Flametongue | 2025 gil | 1975 gil |

Once you're finished spending, be sure to activate the Teleport Crystal to get it added to your list of destinations. Proceed forward into the next zone. You do not need to speak to anyone in this area unless you get enjoyment out of being treated like an annoyance. Make your way to the next zone by always taking the fork that leads higher up the trees. In **The Fane of the Path**, head east across the bridge for a scene. Afterwards, be sure to grab the guaranteed one-time barrel with Haste magick in the dead end east from the scene. Head back to the village entrance.

Head back through **Golmore Jungle** to the **Ozmone Plain**. The shortest path is simply following the right wall. Back in the **Sunlit Path**, there are a couple of new Imperials. Speak to one and give him a Potion. Now you can borrow his Chocobo to reach the **Henne Mines**. Ride the Chocobo out into the **Field of Light Winds** and look for a path to the south taking you to **The Shred**. There are up to four chests that can spawn in the southwest quadrant of **The Shred**. Included among them are a Flametongue, Green Beret, and Float Mote. You will need to dismount to grab any. Head to the mine entrance in the southeast for a scene.

#### The Henne Mines

| **Treasures** | 13 ( **Phase 1 Dig**), 4 ( **Crossover A**), |
| **Notable Loot** | map of Henne Mines, Bonecrusher (technick), Lente's Tear |
| **Enemies** | Seeker, Redmaw, Jelly, Nightmare, Tyranorox, Thunderbug, Tiamat (boss) |

In the **Henne Mines**, there are two colors of gates, only one of which can be open at a time. Gate Switchboards will show which gate is currently closed. In order to progress, you'll have to make use of these switchboards. The path through **North Entrance** is linear, but you have to activate the Gate Swtchboard on the pillar on the left before you get to the fork in order to open up the Blue Gate to the next area. In the next area, you can immediately proceed with the story, but you'd miss the map urn and an area you'll need for the Cartographer trophy. Instead, be sure you have an Esuna gambit set up or a Nu Khai Sand gambit set up, flip the gate switchboard, and defeat the Jellies that drop on you (or run into the next area). Head south or east (the next zone is a loop) and work your way to the southeast corner for the map urn. Continue back to the Gate Switchboard and reopen the blue gates so you can head west. **Phase 1 Shaft** is linear, so move through it to **Phase 1 Dig**. You may have a nice chain built up, which may increase the likelihood of a Redmaw dropping an Ice Shield.

Being a dig site, that means treasure everywhere. There are up to thirteen chests, all with either motes or gear if they hold an item at all. Included is a guaranteed one-time chest right as you leave the area with the Bonecrusher technick. Most chests can contain a Dark Mote or one other kind: Bio, Hastega, Cura, Dispel, Holy, Balance, Aeroga, Gravity, or Aqua. The other three that could contain gear have a Mage's Habit and a Brigandine in a passage in the west not on the map and Shielded Armor in a chest in the very southeast on your way to the next zone. Take your time and explore every path of this area as there is treasure at almost every corner and dead-end. Given how expensive the Shielded Armor is and how close it is to the next zone, it might be worthwhile turning on 4x speed and grabbing this until you have enough for all of your heavy armor wearers.

In **Crossover A**, there may be a chest with a Flame Shield in the dead end south of the entrance. This also may be worth farming a couple for your shield users. The other three chests (at each dead end and the south side of the fork to the north) all contain motes: Dark and either Gravity, Vanishga, or Bio. Head all the way north, then take the east exit on the platform to **Crossover B**. If you look to your right, you'll see two treasures - which are not listed above. That's because they're Thunderbugs. Be sure to take on at least one for your bestiary. This square map cannot be crossed on the south side due to the red gates being closed. That also prevents you from exiting to the north. Instead, follow the north path all the way east and then south to **Pithead Junction** **B**.

Trigger the Gate Switchboard. A possibly overwhelming number of Jellies will drop. You can attempt to hang in there, but you may need two people constantly casting Cura. If you have someone with Fira, be sure to cast that. You can also try a Soleil Fang. You can also try Horology or Traveler technicks. Head back west into **Crossover B** again, making your way north and back to **Pithead Junction B**. Make your way around the square counterclockwise to get to the north exit for a scene.

Use any LP you have and set up your gambits before moving north since you will be thrown into a tough boss battle. Check out the strategy below, and once you're set up save with the Teleport Crystal, then head north to the next zone.

> ##### Boss: Tiamat
>
>
> | **Level** | 25 | **LP** | 15 | **CP** | 4730 |
> | **HP** | 49993 | **Steal** | Hi-Potion (55/80%), Ring Wyrm Scale (10/30%), Leo Gem (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Earth |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Wind<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Slow, Berserk |
>
> It's good to have each character with a gambit to revive an KO'ed party member, and Hi-Potion gambits for low health (maybe below 30%, dependent on the HP of your party). It also might be good to have a shield user with Protect (from Shielded Armor) and Regen (from a gambit) maintained. It's also good to have Larsa with a Stona gambit moved to the top since Tiamat can inflict Petrify on hit. Tiamat will use Aero twice during the battle, will counter magick with Reflect, and will cast Disablega if its preferred target isn't near enough. It will focus typically on one character at a time, thus the shield, protect, regen combo usefulness. It will also use Breath twice once its HP gets low, inflicting Sap. Use a Remedy with someone that has the Remedy Lore 1 license unlocked to deal with Sap and Disable. Tiamat becomes harder to damage at low HP, but doesn't become more dangerous. Keep your health up and you should eventually prevail.

After the scenes, you will receive Lente's Tear, allowing you to continue on your journey to **Bur-Omisace**. Before saving and proceeding, be sure to sell off your loot. If you didn't purchase Esuna before, it is critical to get it now and have the license for it. You may also get new bazaar goods with your loot from **Henne Mines**.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Bat Fang (x5)<br>Yellow Liquid (x1)<br>Water Magicite (x3) | Hollow-Shaft Arrows | Bamboo Arrows<br>Loxley Bow | 3980 gil | 4720 gil |
| Crooked Fang (x2)<br>Fire Stone (x4) | Arrows Alight | Fiery Arrows<br>Longbow | 2800 gil | 2600 gil |

### Arriving at Mt Bur-Omisace

#### Golmore Jungle - Passing Through

| **Treasures** | 1 ( **Paths of Chained Light**), 4 ( **The Needlbrake**), 4 ( **Rustling Chapel**), 1 ( **Parting Glade**), 1 ( **Dell of the Dreamer**), 3 ( **Branchway**), 3 ( **The Greenswathe**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Amber Armlet |
| **Enemies** | Panther, Gargoyle, Marlboro, Treant, Hellhound, Great Marlboro, Diresuaur, Dark Skeleton, Elder Wyrm (boss), Midgardsormr (rare game), Grave Lord (rare game), Phyllo (rare game) |

You are now free to leave the jungle. Head out of Eruyt Village and turn left. You will see the barrier fade away, so you can now exit to the east. Move through the next area, clearing Gargoyles and Marlboros, and save at the Teleport Crystal in The Parting Glade. Pick up the one-time guaranteed chest with an Amber Armlet while here. Check the strategy below to set up your gambits before proceeding north.

> ##### Boss: Elder Wyrm
>
>
> | **Level** | 23 | **LP** | 14 | **CP** | 4200 |
> | **HP** | 71692 | **Steal** | Succulent Fruit (55/80%), Feystone (10/30%), Emperor's Scale (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Wind |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | N/A |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Reverse, Sleep, Silence, Slow |
>
> This may be the first actually challenging boss of the game. It can go very poorly very quickly. The fight starts with both the boss and a couple of Treants. Take out the Treants quickly to reduce the number of foes dealing damage. The problem with this fight, however, isn't the damage. The Elder Wyrm can cast a lot of status ailment magick, including Slow, Break, Blind, Silence, and Confuse. It also has the skill Sporefall that triggers about every 15 moves. This can cause Confuse, Slow, Silence, Blind, Poison, Oil, and Sap. If your entire party gets hit with Confuse, Blind, Sap, Poison, and Oil - expect game over quickly as they will remain confused while their HP drops and they can't hit each other to shake themselves out of it. Oh yeah, Fireball happens pretty regularly, too, which is the biggest damaging skill even without Oil.
>
> So, how to counter all this. First, spread your party out. Sporefall is an AoE, but its range isn't terribly large. If you can manage to only let one person get hit, you should be completely fine. Makes sure that you have someone with an Esuna gambit near or at the top of the gambit queue and that that person isn't the one drawing attention. If your Esuna caster gets hit, be sure to throw a Nu Khai Sand immediately. It's a good idea to have someone with a Nu Khai Sand gambit in place to remove confuse from anyone immediately so that you don't have to wait on Esuna, but still let Esuna take care of the rest. Whoever isn't busy can also use a Handkerchief to remove Oil before Fireball is used. Finally, to really save yourself some headaches, make sure that you have someone with a silence-inflicting weapon - preferably a gun with Silence Shot. If you inflict Silence, you don't have to worry about all the magick the Elder Wyrm can cast that requires extra Esunas.
>
> Of course, be sure to have a couple of Cura gambits set up with Larsa and your Esuna caster, and have Phoenix Down gambits set up as well. As long as you get Silence to stick, you shouldn't have a problem with MP or keeping everyone healed. Keep a melee character with a shield drawing the Elder Wyrm's attention and even Fireball can be blocked occasionally. You will consistently deal damage and the Elder Wyrm can't heal, so as long as you endure and keep Sporefall from afflicting your entire party you will eventually emerge victoriously.

After the battle, there is a one-time guaranteed chest in the room with 9872 gil. You can head back to save and remove Slow, or continue on to the east. Turn the corner and take out the Gargoyles. If you turn right at the intersection, you will come to a dead-end that may have a couple of treasure barrels with Ethers or Hi-Ethers. This is also one of the location where the rare game Phyllo may appear if you defeat the Marlboros in the area. There are two routes to the next area. The northern path has several Marlboros and a Treant. The southern path has a treasure barrel that may contain a Black Belt on the west end and is the other spawn location for Phyllo. The southern path also has Marlboros, Gargoyles, and a Treant. The enemies can respawn for a while, but eventually quit. Once they do, check the two locations for Phyllo. If it doesn't spawn, zone out and back in until it does, checking both areas each time. Phyllo is a bat-like creature and is not any more challenging than the other enemies in the area. Use the south path to proceed to the next area.

There may be a treasure barrel that could contain an Argyle Armlet on the north edge of the southern path, about a third of the way in. At the end of the southern path, head back along the north to face the Diresaur on the platform with a couple of Ether chests. Exit to the east.

#### Paramina Rift

| **Treasures** | 10 ( **Head of the Silverflow**), 5 ( **Freezing Gorge**), 8 ( **Frozen Brook**), 1 ( **Path of Firstfall**), 3 ( **Spine of the Icewyrm**), 4 ( **Silverflow's End** via **Spine of the Icewyrm**), 10 ( **Karydine Glacier**), 7 ( **Silverflow's End** via **Karydine Glacier**), 8 ( **Icebound Flow**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Revive (technick), Death (magick), Baltoro's Seed |
| **Enemies** | White Wolf, Wild Onion, Skull Knight, Lizard, Baritine Croc, Yeti, Garuda-Egi, Slaven Warder, Twintania, Emperor Aevis, Dark Skeleton, Ice Elemental, Leshach Entite, Anchag (rare game) |

Use the save crystal and continue northeast. There is a lot to explore in the Paramina Rift, and there will be time for it later. For now, it makes most sense to head straight to the destination. In **Head of the Silverflow**, you'll follow the wall on the left to **Freezing Gorge**. You'll do the same through **Freezing Gorge** (after a couple of scenes) to arrive at **Mt Bur-Omisace**. By this point, you probably have all the gear available in **Head of the Silverflow**, and it would be out of your way to pick it up. If you must, however, you can find a Mage's Cap, a Burgonet, a Flame Shield, and a Red Cap among the ten treasures scattered across this region. Only the Mage's Cap is on the upper path that takes you straight to **Freezing Gorge**, just past the first Wild Onion on the right. In **Freezing Gorge**, there are five chests on the south portion of the loop, three of which may have gear: Mage's Habit, Brigandine, and Shielded Armor.

#### Mt. Bur-Omisace

| ##### Cartographer's Guild Moogle -   _Mt Bur-Omisace: Sand-strewn Path_ |
| **Area** | **Cost** |
| Paramina Rift | 3200 gil |
| Mt Bur-Omisace | 15 gil |

Now that you've finally arrived, you have the normal checklist of tasks.

- Sell loot
- Buy new magicks
- Upgrade gear
- Buy maps of **Mt. Bur-Omisace** and **Paramina Rift**
- Activate Teleport Crystal and save

Here are a few new Bazaar goods that might be available:

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Crooked Fang (x4)<br>Yellow Liquid (x2)<br>Ice Stone (x1) | Ranger's Crossbow | Long Bolts<br>Crossbow | 1080 gil | 1220 gil |
| Sturdy Bone (x5)<br>Demon Eyeball (x3)<br>Fire Magicite (x4) | Iron-forged Pole | Iron Pole | 2115 gil | 2115 gil |

Before you go crazy with purchasing gear upgrades, it may be good to know that almost every weapon and armor the merchant sells can be found in treasure barrels in the Paramina Rift - if you are patient enough to do the farming. So be sure to purchase any new magick and technicks before buying new gear. Once you're satisfied, head north through **Temple Approach** into **Temple Grounds**. Make your way to the temple, climb the steps, and open the Gate of the Holy Circle. Approach the back of **The Hall of Holy Light** to trigger the next scene.

Now that you know the next destination, feel free to explore the **Paramina Rift**. If you wish to get on with it and not grind for treasure, the most direct way is south through **Freezing Gorge**, take the southwest exit in **Frozen Brook** into the **Path of Firstfall**. Save and prepare for flying foes before proceeding into **Spine of the Iceworm**, and then take the south exit out of **Silverflow's End**. Now click [here](#walkthrough-part-3---forging-an-alliance) to skip to walkthrough of the **Stilshrine**.

If you're still reading, you want loot. I've already described what's available in the regions you've passed through, so I'll start here with a nice route that will cause enemies to continuously spawn while chest farming so you can gain some levels. Most of the zones are intertwined, which makes looping through three zones nice and easy. In particular, the loop between **Frozen Brook**, **Karydine Glacier**, and **Icebound Flow** requires you to go through almost the entirety of each region so you don't have to go far out of your way to find any treasure barrels.

To kick off the loop, head south out of **Mt Bur-Omisace** straight through **Freezing Gorge**. When you reach **Frozen Brook**, travel all the way to the southeast to reach **Karydine Glacier**. Work your way through **Karydine Glacier** to the northwest exit into **Icebound Flow**. Scout out the southern region of **Icebound Flow** and then work your way back to the northeast exit back where you started in **Frozen Brook** to repeat. Here's the list of key loot to look for in these areas:

- **Frozen Brook**
  - Bangle on the north wall near to the east of first entrance
  - Elfin Bow on the frozen river directly south of where the bangle might appear
  - Long Bolts in the eastern region near the northern edge of the river
  - Parallel Arrows on eastern wall north of exit
- **Karydine Glacier**
  - Power Rod about halfway down eastern wall south of the entrance
  - Six-fluted Pole, Murasame, and Betelgeuse in the south east alcove
  - Gaia Rod in the southern-most point on the map near the center
  - Thorned Mace on western wall almost directly north of Gaia Rod location
  - Death magick in a one-time guaranteed chest at the end of the long dead-end path in the north
  - Baltoro's Seed in a one-time chest with only 1% appearance rate where the Death magick appears
  - Ice Shield by large rock just north of ice along river on west edge.
- **Icebound Flow**
  - Lamia's Tiara in southeast alcove
  - Diamond Shield near the center of the south wall
  - Close Helmet almost directly north of the Diamond Shield
  - Recurve Crossbow on south wall about halfway between Diamond Shield and exit to **Feywood**
  - Headband in the southwestern corner of the slope on the west edge of the river in the middle of the region.
  - Demonsbane in the northern corner of the same slope as the Headband, where the slope meets the water.
  - Storm Spear on eastern wall directly in front of you when walking along the ice edge from the Demonsbane barrel
  - Jujitsu Gi in front of rocks on your way back to **Frozen Brook**

While you are in **Karydine Glacier**, if you kill all the enemies until they stop respawning, the rare game Anchag should spawn near where the Thorned Mace treasure was. It has around 30,000 HP, but you can steal Damascus Steel from it, which is needed for crafting Gemsteel that is used in the Khumba and Tournesol - some of the most powerful weapons in the game. Anchag also may drop Arcana if you have purchased the Knight's Monograph. Anchag will only spawn once per entry into **Paramina Rift**, so to encounter it again you will need to return to **Mt Bur-Omisace.**

Once you are ready to grab the rest, the next location should be the western half of **Silverflow's End**. It is accessible from the **Karydine Glacier** by using the exit south of the one that leads to **Icebound Flow**. There are 7 chests here, but only 3 have gear and all are clustered in the south tip. You could find a Hunting Crossbow, a Platinum Shield, and a Loxley Bow.

The remainder of the areas are on the way to the **Stilshrine of Miriam**. Start by taking the only exit out of **Frozen Brook** you haven't used yet. This lead to **Path of the Firstfall**. In here is a guaranteed repeatable chest that most likely contains gil. Otherwise it is a Knot of Rust (or potentially a Meteorite, if you have Diamond Armlet equipped). You can also save here. Continuing through takes you to **Spine of the Icewyrm**. Near the southern exit on the east side will be a guaranteed one-time chest with the Revive technick. In **Silverflow's End**, there are four chests, all which might have gear. Once you have reached the large open area in the south, a Sledgehammer can be found just to the east behind a rock near the wall. South of that you may find a Gladius perched on another big rock. There are two small alcoves on the west edge. The northern may contain an Icebrand, and the southern a Glacial Staff. Once you've gotten as much of each gear as you need, you can exit south into the **Stilshrine of Miriam**. Here was my weapon/armor set up at this point (not using the Seitengrat or Karkata):

|  | **Vaan** Lv 28, HP 2818 | **Balthier** Lv 29, HP 2806 | **Fran**<br>Lv 29, HP 2747 | **Basch**<br>Lv 27, HP 2251 | **Ashe**<br>Lv 28, HP 2704 | **Penelo**<br>Lv 26, HP 2243 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| _Shikari_<br>_Foebreaker_ | _Machinist_<br>_White Mage_ | _Archer_<br>_Red Battlemage_ | _Knight_<br>_Samurai_ | _Uhlan_<br>_Time Battlemage_ | _Monk_<br>_Black Mage_ |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Hand** | Sledgehammer<br>(Axes & Hammers 4) | Betelgeuse<br>(Guns 3) | Loxley Bow<br>(Bows 4) | Kiku-ichimonji <br>(Katana 3) | Hunting Crossbow<br>(Crossbows 3) | Six-fluted Pole<br>(Poles 4) |
| **Off-hand**<br>**Ammo** | Platinum Shield | Silent Shot | Bamboo Arrows | - | Long Bolts | - |
| **Helm** | Close Helmet<br>(Heavy Armor 5) | Headband<br>(Light Armor 6) | Headband<br>(Light Armor 6) | Close Helmet<br>(Heavy Armor 5) | Close Helmet<br>(Heavy Armor 5) | Lamia's Tiara<br>(Mystic Armor 5) |
| **Armor** | Shielded Armor<br>(Heavy Armor 4) | Jujitsu Gi<br>(Light Armor 6) | Jujitsu Gi<br>(Light Armor 6) | Shielded Armor<br>(Heavy Armor 4) | Shielded Armor<br>(Heavy Armor 4) | Mage's Habit<br>(Mystic Armor 5) |

### Stilshrine of Miriam

#### Walk of Sky

| **Treasures** | 3 |
| **Notable Loot** | Tourmaline Ring (x2), Battle Harness |

From the entrance, there are two Tourmaline Rings at the end of the east and west arcs. These are one-time guaranteed chests, as is the one near the southern exit containing a Battle Harness.

#### Walk of Mind

| **Treasures** | 1 |
| **Notable Loot** | Ice Shield |

Grab the Ice Shield in the center of the area, then save your game at the Teleport Crystal. Equip the Dawn Shard accessory on the leader of your active party (make your leader a non-magick user) and continue south.

#### Ward of Measure

| **Enemies** | Redmaw, Zombie |

Upon entry, you should see a pedestal that you have to walk around a gap to get to. If you touch the pedestal without the Dawn Shard equipped on your party leader, you will be forced to face Zombies. With the Dawn Shard equipped, however, it will teleport you to the next zone.

#### Ward of Velitation

| **Treasures** | 3 |
| **Notable Loot** | Countdown (technick) |
| **Enemies** | Miriam Guardian, Dragon Aevis, Miriam Facer |

After defeating the Miriam Guardian upon arrival, there are three Dragon Aevis to take down. If they give you a problem, keep in mind that they are weak to blizzard and susceptible to most status ailments. Afterwards, pick up the one-time guaranteed chest in the center of the room to obtain the Countdown technick. Continue east past the Miriam Facers.

#### Walk of Torn Illusion

| **Enemies** | Miriam Facer, Miriam Guardian, Oiling (secret passage) |

Head up the stairs in front of you and take out the Miriam Facer. Then head up to the left or right and make your way down the upper hallway back east, taking down Miriam Guardians on the way. In the back hallway in the center is another Pedestal of the Dawn. Activate it with the party member wearing the Dawn Shard. Head back to the **Ward of Velitation** and use either set of newly revealed staircases to reach the next zone.

#### Walk of Revelation

| **Enemies** | Crystalbug |

Head down the stairs until you reach a green Life Crystal. Interact with it to find that it was actually a Crystalbug monster that likes to cast Aquara. Once it is defeated, you can save your game. Continue down the central stairs to the next zone.

#### Ward of the Sword-King / Cold Distance secret area

| **Treasures** | 1 |
| **Notable Loot** | Shell Shield |
| **Enemies** | Blood Gigas, Miriam Guardian, Darkmare, Oiling, Ghast, Matriarch Bomb (rare game), Negalmuur (rare game) |

Proceed west down the hall, facing two Blood Gigas and a Miriam Guardan. Take a left and take on the two Blood Gigas hidden in small alcoves. As you approach the Darkmare, you can reveal a hidden passage on the west wall. Continue south, take on two more Blood Gigas and interact with the Sword of Judgment. Before heading to the newly opened door, head into the secret area to the west of where you faced the Darkmare. This is technically part of **Walk of Torn Illusion**. You may face an Oiling on the way up the stairs. At the top, before moving into the next area, be sure to equip Steel Poleyns on anyone you can. You will trigger traps as soon as you step in that inflict confuse and berserk. At the top of the steps, you will face several more Oilings, then you can grab the Shell Shield from the treasure chest.

While here, exit and re-enter, clearing out the Oilings each time, until the Matriarch Bomb appears. If you don't have at least two Steel Poleyns, you may want to wait to do this. The Matriarch Bomb begins with Shell, but if you dispel it she will counter with Greater Barrier, applying Shell and Protect. She may also apply Oil to one of your characters when striking, so be sure to remove that with a Handkerchief before she casts Fira.

Return back to the **Ward of the Sword-King**, but head down the stairs slowly as you will have frequent ambushes by Oilings. Given that they can cause Confuse, you don't want to face too many at a time, even if you are over-leveled. Once back in the **Ward of the Sword-King**, you may want to go and use the Save Crystal again if you are weak from the Oilings - especially if you happen to spawn Negalmuur at the intersection. Negalmuur spawns after the Darkmare has been killed and you have spent 30 minutes in the **Stilshrine**.

If you choose to fight Negalmuur, it's best to do it near the exit to the save sphere in case things get out of hand. Negalmuur doesn't hit particularly hard, but regularly raises undead Ghast to fight alongside. If you are killing the Ghast slightly faster than they are being summoned, you will periodically get hits in on Negalmuur and take it down slowly. You may even gain a few levels in the process. Negalmuur can cause confuse, so have couple of characters set up with Nu Khai Sand gambits. Negalmuur can also cast Doom, but it takes so long to actually kill a character you can prbably ignore it and deal with the KO once it happens. The last frustrating attack is Annul, which wiped out one character's MP. Use charge, if it was your healer. Otherwise, you can ignore it.

Head to the north end of **Ward of the Sword-King** and interact with the Way Stone to return to the **Ward of Measure**. Head up to the northwest corner and down the stairs to the newly unlocked door. You can also step outside and save, first, if you wish.

#### Cold Distance - West

| **Enemies** | Miriam Guardian |

There are two Miriam Guardians you can face, but you only are required to trigger the first. Take the exit on the west wall.

#### Walk of Prescience

| **Treasures** | 1 |
| **Notable Loot** | Ruby Ring |
| **Enemies** | Balloon, Facer, Zombie Warrior, |

Head straight down the path in front of you to reach a one-time guaranteed chest with a Ruby Ring. Head back to the entrance and take the northern path. When you come to the room with Facers and Zombie Warriors, clear them out and examine the statue. Rotate it clockwise so that it faces the center of the shrine. Continue south.

#### Walk of Reason

| **Treasures** | 10 |
| **Notable Loot** | Ashura |
| **Enemies** | Zombie Warrior, Ghoul, Ghast, Balloon, Facer |

This area is a bit of a maze, with treasures hidden down various paths. All of them can contain gear: Ashura (one-time guaranteed), Platinum Sword, Obelisk, and Hunting Crossbow in the southeast dead-end; Power Rod, Kiku-Ichimonji, and Golden Staff in a dead-end near the center of the map; Loxley Bow north of the statue that is your destination; Hammerhead right near the exit; and a Ras Algethi south of the exit in the short hallway to the east. If you've been keeping up with gear, you may have the Power Rod, the Hunting Crossbow, the Loxley Bow, and the Kiku-Ichimonji. The rest may be new, so you are welcome to farm and gain some levels here.

Begin by heading south, and follow the path east to the Ancient Door. This room will have several undead ambushes as you make your way across to another Ancient Door. Follow the wall on your right until you come to a dead end filled with undead spawns. There can be up to four chests, one of them only appearing once with the Ashura. If you were hoping for the Platinum Sword for your Knight or Obelisk for your Uhlan and didn't get them, head back to the previous zone to reset the chests. Ashura is the first Ninja Sword available and is only available to a Shikari. It requires access via the Quickening Red Spiral on the left side of the license board. If you plan on using it, go ahead and unlock it, but you may want to reset the board later when better Ninja Swords are available and not bothering unlocking Ninja Swords 1.

When you have all the gear you wanted from that dead-end, head back around and take the stairs to the north at the first interesction you reach. At the top of the stairs, turn east, then south. When you reach the next intersection, turn north for more gear. You will have to face two balloons and two Facers, but there can be up to three chests behind them. The Golden Staff in the right-most chest may be the only item you don't have of these three, so zone until you get what you want.

Your next target is the statue, marked on your map. Head south until you are forced to turn east, and you should see the Stone Brave. You will have to knock off a Facer and three Ballons, but once you do you can rotate the statue counterclockwise to have it face towrd the center of the shrine. Directly north (without going down the stairs), there may be a chest that could hold a Loxley Bow if you still need one.

From the Stone Brave, head east and then north. At the first intersection, there may be a treasure in the short west hallway containing the Ras Algethi. If you don't get it, you can use the exit to the north to zone until you obtain this gun as it probably is the best available so far. There is also a chest right by the exit that may contain the best Axe available so far, the Hammerhead. Now you can proceed out the north exit to continue.

#### Cold Distance - East

| **Enemies** | Miriam Guardian |

As with the west hallway, there are two Miriam Guardians. Before taking the east exit, you should probably head out and save your game unless you feel comfortable depending on autosaves. You've done a lot and you are getting close to a boss battle. When ready, take the east exit.

#### Ward of Steel

| **Treasures** | 1 |
| **Notable Loot** | Sage's Ring, map of the Stilshrine of Miriam |
| **Enemies** | Vinuskar (boss) |

Before proceeding, it may be useful to swap party members so that you have a bow user, a gun user, and nobody wearing heavy armor. The upcoming boss battle has a permanent field that slows down any metal bearers (including swords, axes, and hammers). Head down the stairs, and be ready for the battle that ensues immediately upon opening the Ancient Door.

> ##### Boss: Vinuskar
>
>
> | **Level** | 27 | **LP** | 19 | **CP** | 2480 |
> | **HP** | 15138 | **Steal** | Knot of Rust (55/80%), Thief's Cuffs (10/30%), Damascus Steel (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | N/A |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | N/A |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Blind, Slow |
>
> With the low HP total, this boss isn't challenging unless you are going in with heavy armor and swords. Not including Trial Mode, this is the second-earliest chance you will have to get the Thief's Cuffs, in case you chose not to [run for the ones in **Zertinan Caverns**](#walkthrough-part-3---forging-an-alliance). So you may want to restart the battle until you get it. He can cast Slow and Break, but the battle should be fairly quick without dealing with them. An Esuna gambit can take care of Break, however, if you are worried. The technick Crown has a 50% chance to cause Confuse, so be ready with Esuna or a Nu Khai Sand. Sword Dance can cause some damage, but if you are using mostly ranged characters then it may only hit one party member. It can use these skills more than once, but it is unlikely it will get the chance. You can also chain Quickenings, if you have any, to make the fight go quickly.

After the battle, grab the Sage's Ring from the chest up ahead before opening the Ancient Door. In the next room, be sure to grab the map from the urn on the south side, then rotate the Stone Brave clockwise to face west. Exit the zone to see what changed.

Make your way back to the **Ward of the Sword-King**. The easiest route is by using the Way Stone in the south end of the **Ward of Measure**. Head to the south end of the **Ward of the Sword-King** and prepare for a tougher fight than the previous.

#### Hall of Worth

| **Treasures** | 2 |
| **Notable Loot** | Elixir, Bubble Mote |
| **Enemies** | Ice Azer, Mateus (Esper) |

> ##### Boss: Mateus
>
>
> | **Level** | 30 | **LP** | 27 | **CP** | 2480 |
> | **HP** | 34259 | **Steal** | Pisces Gem (55/80%), Ether (10/30%), High Arcana (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Lightning |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Ice<br>\[Nullify\] Water, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Oil |
>
> Kick off the battle with Dispel to nullify the Protect and Reflect status on the boss, then immediately follow up with Thundara to knock out the Ice Azers. The boss will reapply Reflect, so it's best not to have any magick casting gambits. This boss casts lots of ice-elemental magick, so Ice Shields and Lamia's Tiaras can come in handy. She can also cast slow. Make sure you have someone dedicated to keeping everyone's health up and dispelling slow.
>
> Once the boss gets below 50% health, she will use Chain Magick which reduces the charge time on spells. The damage output will also increase. Below 20% health, all actions will have zero charge time. If you have any quickenings, that would be the time to use them.

After the battle, check the east and west edges of the room for a Bubble Mote and an Elixir, respectively. Then open the Ancient Door to the south.

#### Vault of the Champion

| **Notable Loot** | The Sword of Kings |

Proceed forward for a scene, at the end of which you gain the Sword of Kings. This is one of the few weapons that doesn't require a license, but unless you are just into Greatswords, it isn't that strong. Exit the **Stilshrine of Miriam**. As you approach the Teleport Crystal, there will be a scene. You can teleport directly back to **Mt Bur-Omisace**, or hike back through the **Paramina Rift**. If you choose to hike, might as well try to nab another Damascus Steel from the rare game Anchag in the **Karydine Glacier**. Either way, go check in on the Gran Kiltias.

### Return to Mt Bur-Omisace

| **Notable Loot** | Stone of the Condemner |
| **Enemies** | Imperial Judge, Judge Bergan (boss) |

Once you arrive in **Mt Bur-Omisace**, if you missed any of the gear in the **Stilshrine** you may be able to buy it from the Traveling Merchant sitting near the chocobo stable. He also likely has armor upgrades for you. If you need gil, you can sell loot, but be sure to not sell the following pieces yet:

- Teleport Stone
- Arcana
- Damascus Steel

You may unlock some items listed previously if you hadn't already, along with a couple of new ones.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Bundle of Needles (x1)<br>Festering Flesh (x2)<br>Ice Magicite (x5) | Hunstman's Crossbow | Stone Bolts<br>Recurve Bow | 3500 gil | 3700 gil |
| Festering Flesh (x4) | Magick Shards | Aquara Mote (x4) | 1480 gil | 330 gil more expensive... |

If you are using a Crossbow, the Recurve Bow is probably not an upgrade, but the Stone Bolts can cause Disable on hit, which can be fun. Your next boss is susceptible to Slow, however, so you may want to stick with the Long Bolts for now. This also may be a good time to pick up another Forgotten Grimoire, if you have enough gil left over. It's late enough in the game that all of them are useful.

Once you've upgraded any gear, make you way back to the **Hall of Light**. If you want to feel the full weight of the moment, be sure to talk to the NPCs as you pass through each zone. Enter the **Hall of Light** to trigger a scene and a boss fight.

> ##### Boss: Judge Bergan
>
>
> | **Level** | 30 | **LP** | 19 | **CP** | 1980 |
> | **HP** | 17200 | **Steal** | Hi-Potion (55/80%), Ruby Ring (10/30%), Ether (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | N/A |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | N/A |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Stop, Sleep, Blind, Sap, Slow, Berserk |
>
> Judge Bergan is a pure physical attacker in the Zodiac Age version (the Japanese version had him using magick which was easily reflected). This means, as usual, a good shield-using tank can help tremendously. He will be accompanied by three Imperial Judges, so try to take them out quickly to minimize the amount of damage you are receiving. His low HP relative to other bosses you've been facing also makes gun users and quickenings effective. If you are level 30, this should be another quick battle. If you are struggling to keep your health up due to too many judges, quickenings or an esper can help. You can also try to use his lack of resistance to Blind status (Blind magick, requiring Black Magick 2 license). Otherwise, since this is primarily a physical duel, just keep your health up and his health will whittle down over time. He does hit hard, so be prepared to heal.

## Walkthrough Part 4 - Infiltrating the Empire

Now that another story arc has completed and you are required to head back to Dalmasca, you can finish up some hunts and progress some sidequests. All of them will be available at this point until the end of the game, so you can do them now while the rewards are more worthwhile or save them for later when you will be overpowered. Read on if you want to do them now. Otherwise, continue with the story by clicking [here](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire).

### OPTIONAL Hunt and Sidequest Continuations

If you plan on tying up some loose ends, teleport back to **Rabanastre**. First things first, find Ktjn to [answer her last question.](#walkthrough-part-1---the-occupied-city) If you missed answering one earlier, she will continue to ask follow-up questions, but you have to wait for a little bit. So do a couple of sidequests or hunts and check back, or just teleport back every time you unlock a new teleport destination. Here's what else you can do:

- [White Mousse hunt](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire)
- [Ring Wyrm hunt](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire)
- [Completing Rimzat's sandstorm task](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire)
- [Marilith hunt](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire)
- [Picking up new hunts and claiming rewards from Montblanc](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire)

#### Lost in the Pudding

| **Notable Loot** | Broken Key, Sluice Gate Key |
| **Enemies** | Lizard, Malboro Overking, White Mousse (mark) |

Make your way to the **Garamsythe Waterway** from **Lowtown / North Sprawl**. Take the south exit from **Overflow Cloaca** into **Central Waterway Control**. Head west from there using one of the middle of the four west exits into the mazelike **No. 10 Channel**. Your destination is the southwest exit to **West Sluice Control**. Clear out all the Malboro Overkings on the upper path and be healed up before descending the stairs to the lower level.

> ##### Hunt: White Mousse
>
>
> | **Level** | 15 | **LP** | 34 | **CP** | 3420 |
> | **HP** | 69469 | **Steal** | Potion (55/80%), Echo Herbs (10/30%), Hi-Potion (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Fire |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Water<br>\[Half Damage\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Sleep, Blind, Berserk |
>
> The reason to wait on taking on a level 15 boss is that it does some major damage and isn't trivial to take down when you first receive the task. By this point in the game, however, it shouldn't be too bad. If you take in two characters that can have the gambit "Foe: fire-weak, Fira" set up, that helps. Also, a Viking Coat (Light Armor 6 License) will nullify the Aquaga damage. If you don't use Light Armor characters, be sure to have a gambit that maintains Shell on your allies, such as "Ally: any, Shell". Maintaining Regen can also be beneficial generally and to mitigate Toxify which inflicts Poison status. With these gambits set up along with a Cura gambit, the battle should go pretty smoothly.
>
> You could try to get fancy by inflicting White Mousse with Blind and Berserk to eliminate the damage dealt, but if you are doing this after the events in **Mt Bur-Omisace**, you are probably strong enough to do the battle this way.

| **Hunt Completed** | White Mousse |
| **Rewards** | 2800 Gil<br>Burning Bow (x1) |

After the battle, you will obtain a Broken Key. Head back to **Rabanastre / Westgate** and find Sorbet, the Moogling Attendant. After receiving the rewards, you'll also receive the Sluice Gate Key, which will allow you to control the water levels in the **Garamsythe Waterway**.

#### For Whom the Wyrm Tolls

| **Enemies** | Dive Talon, Wolf, Alraune, Ring Wyrm (mark) |

To find this foe, begin by teleporting to **Dalmasca Westersand**. The enemy only appears during a sandstorm, so use the east exit into the **Westersand**, but if it's not a storm then rezone. Take out the nearby creatures and find the Ring Wyrm due east of the entrance.

> ##### Hunt: Ring Wyrm
>
>
> | **Level** | 32 | **LP** | 16 | **CP** | 6300 |
> | **HP** | 128648 | **Steal** | Phoenix Down (55/80%), Hi-Potion (10/30%), Water Magicite (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Fire |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Water<br>\[Half Damage\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Blind, Immobilize, Slow |
>
> The Ring Wyrm has several magicks it can cast, including fire-based magick which it is weak to. Therefore, if you equip some Ruby Rings, you will reflect that damage. It also can inflict many status ailments, such as Sap, Disable, and Immobilize. Be sure to have some party members that have Remedy Lore 1 unlocked with a Remedy gambit set up. The Ring Wyrm will heal up 30% health after it drops below 50%. At that point, shields will no longer be effective at blocking, but the flame shield will still reduce the fire damage.
>
> Dispel the mark at the beginning of battle. It may be worth inflicting Blind as well so that it will rely on its magick attacks. If you have Ruby Rings equipped, this is definitely to your advantage. If you have anyone with the Belias license, summoning this esper would also be useful since most of the attacks would heal Belias. Otherwise, try to use two ranged or magick-casting characters (with gambits setup for Fira), while the other character attempts to tank. If you do have Ruby Rings equipped, be sure to change your healing gambits to Hi-Potions and not Cura, and don't have any other ally-targeting magick gambits active either. Between Reflect and Blind, the Ring Wyrm is fairly powerless. You may end up using a few Hi-Potions as it will eventually get some damage through, and you'll use a few Remedies - but otherwise you will eventually emerge victorious without much danger.

| **Hunt Completed** | Ring Wyrm |
| **Rewards** | 200 Gil<br>Moon Ring (x1)<br>Icebrand (x1) |

Head back to **Rabanastre** and then to **Lowtown / North Sprawl** to check in with Balzac in the southwestern dead end. You can hike back through the Westersand or teleport back using the crystal that brought you here.

#### Source of the Sandstorms

| **Notable Loot** | Wind Globe, Windvane |
| **Enemies** | Wolf, Cactoid, Urstrix, Sleipnir, Dive Talon, Alraune, Alpha Wolf, Cockatrice, Earth Tyrant (boss) |

From **Rabanastre / Westgate**, head west into the **Dalmasca Westersand**. Head directly through **Galtea Downs** into **The Midfault**. Turn south immediately to take the exit into **Shimmering Horizons**. Follow the north wall to the west until you find the exit into the cut off portion of **Windtrace Dunes**. There are two large Dynast Cactoids in the area. One of them has the Wind Globe buried in with its roots. Head back to **Rabanastre / Westgate**. Northon, Rimzat, and Cotze are gathered together. Speak to any of them. Your Wind Globe will be converted to a Windvane. Now you can find the Earth Tyrant, which you overheard the group talking about when you approached. Doing this now unlocks dialogue in an area you will be encountering soon.

To find the Earth Tyrant, head back out into the **Westersand**, but this time head north to **Corridor of Sand**. Make your way around to the east exit into **Wyrm's Nest**.

> ##### Boss: Earth Tyrant
>
>
> | **Level** | 30 | **LP** | 21 | **CP** | 4500 |
> | **HP** | 73982 | **Steal** | Hi-Potion (55/80%), Tyrant Hide (10/30%), Tyrant Bone (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Wind |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Earth<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Reverse, Sleep, Blind, Silence, Slow, Berserk |
>
> This is another boss that is helpful to blind. If possible, it may be a good idea to inflict it with Berserk as well so that it will not use Screwtail, one of its stronger attacks. As with many bosses, it's a good idea to have two ranged characters and one tank with a shield and Protect status. Decoy might be called for as well. Be ready with Cura, Curaga, and Hi-Potions either manually or with gambits. Regen is also useful again, particularly since the Earth Tyrant can inflict Poison on hit. It only uses physical attacks, however, so Reflect and Shell are useless. Below 50% health, it will use Screwtail more frequently, it is harder to evade, and takes reduced damage. Below 20%, its attack rate will increase.
>
> Since I couldn't get a party of three that had all the skills I needed, I began by getting Protect and Regen on Vaan (my shield-bearing tank), Balthier (my white magick support character), Ashe (my Decoy and Berserk caster), and Penelo (my Blind caster). I also had Decoy on Vaan before entering the area. I went in with Vaan, Penelo, and Balthier. As soon as Blind stuck, I swapped Penelo with Ashe. All of the casting was set up with gambits. Baltheir's gun helped with damage whenever he wasn't casting Cura, Regen, or Protect. The Earth Tyrant's health dropped fairly quickly, but once it got low things slowed down tremendously. I even had some attacks going for 0 damage. Most were in the 300-400 range. Additionally, I began taking much more damage, even though Blind and Berserk were applied. Toward the very end, Vaan was very low on HP as the attacks were coming in faster than Cura and Hi-Potions could be tossed. In retrospect, a Curaga gambit would probably have been good to switch to about halfway through battle once Decoy was being maintained well. If you have to swap in a fresh melee character, be ready and remember that you can use Phoenix Downs and Hi-Potions and other items on characters not currently in your party by selecting the item and then R1 to target your reserve characters. Keep it up and the Earth Tyrant will eventually fall.

#### Tingling Toast

The next hunt is fast and powerful. If it is too challenging right now, don't feel bad about waiting until later. If you want to try it out, head out of the **Rabanastre / Westgate** into the **Dalmasca Westersand**. Head straight across the **Galtea Downs** into **The Midfault**. Take the exit immediately south into **Shimmering Horizons**. In the western region of this zone is access to a cavern. This is **Zertinan Caverns**. Head inside.

| **Treasures** | 9 |
| **Notable Loot** | Firefly, Serpentwyne Must |
| **Enemies** | Slime, Speartongue, Marilith (mark) |

Once inside, clear the first zone ( **Invitation to** **Heresy**) of enemies. In the first dead end to the north, there is a guaranteed one-time chest with a Firefly. The rest of the chests that appear only hold restorative items or gil. Once you've been in the map five minutes (even in menus), Marilith will appear near the southern portion of the zone at the small southeastern alcove where sun is shining in on a grove of trees and cacti, just past two Speartongues.

> ##### Hunt: Marilith
>
>
> | **Level** | 38 | **LP** | 22 | **CP** | 3510 |
> | **HP** | 54921 | **Steal** | Fire Stone (55/80%), Prime Tanned Hide (10/30%), Fire Crystal (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Water |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Fire<br>\[Nullify\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Blind, Silence, Disease, Slow, Lure, Libra |
>
> Marilith is easier to dodge when its health is below 50%, but it is is harder to hit and does more damage. Just like many of the others, it also attacks much more rapidly when its health is below 20%. It can inflict Slow and Sap with its hits, so having some Chronos Tear and Remedy or Esuna gambits set up. If you can cast Haste, have it set up in a gambit to counter Slow. Decoy on a shield-bearing tank with high evasion is particularly useful again, especially during the latter half of the battle. Inflicting Blind and Slow also helps diminish the damage.
>
> With this gambit setup at level 30, I had no problem:
>
> |  | **Vaan**<br>Foebraker / Shikari |  | **Fran** Red Battlemage / Archer |  | **Penelo**<br>Monk / Black Mage |
> | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
> | **Slot** | **Condition** | **Action** |  | **Condition** | **Action** |  | **Condition** | **Action** |
> | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
> | 1 | Ally: any | Remedy |  | Ally: HP < 30% | Curaga |  | Ally: HP <40% | Hi-Potion |
> | 2 | Ally: any | Phoenix Down |  | Ally: HP < 60% | Cura |  | Foe: any | Blind |
> | 3 | Foe: HP ≥ 70% | Steal |  | Ally: any | Raise |  | Ally: any | Phoenix Down |
> | 4 | Foe: Highest Max HP | Attack |  | Ally: Vaan | Decoy |  | Ally: any | Chronos Tear |
> | 5 | Self | Libra |  | Ally: any | Protect |  | Foe: water-weak | Aqua |
> | 6 |  |  |  | Ally: any | Regen |  | Foe: Highest Mx HP | Attack |
> | 7 |  |  |  | Foe: any | Slow |  | Self | Libra |
> | 8 |  |  |  | Foe: Highest Max HP | Attack |  |  |  |
>
> Given all the casting Fran had to do, I gave her a Sage's Ring to help with MP management. Vaan had a Hammerhead and Platinum Shield, which gave him the best evasion I could at this point in the game. I forgot to cast Dispel, forgot to turn off the Steal gambit after stealing for several rounds, and never got a mist charge with my single character with a quickening (Vaan). I still had no problem and was never in any danger.

| **Hunt Completed** | Marilith |
| **Rewards** | 2200 Gil<br>Serpent Eye (x1)<br>Teleport Stone (x1) |

After the battle, you will receive a cask of Serpentwyne Must. Take this back to the Tavernmaster in the Sandsea tavern in **Rabanastre / East End**. The Serpent Eye that you get as a reward is needed for crafting the Serpentarius, of which you need 3 to craft a Tournesol. In other words, don't sell it until you have everything you need for a Serpentarius and can craft them one at a time with complete sets. If you sell extras, they get wasted. At this point, other than Teleport Stones you probably have 3 types of loot you should hang on to: Arcana, Damascus Steel, and Serpent Eye. You may also want to hang on to the Moon Ring if you earned it from slaying the Earth Tyrant. It's only used in one item, but it is the powerful Silver Bow, so you may want to not forget that you had one and end up farming the Ash Wyrm longer later on.

#### Picking up New Hunts and Getting Clan Rewards

If you've been following along, you only have one active hunt still - the Gil Snapper. The Gile Snapper may still be a bit on the challenging side. If you check out a Notice Board like the one in the Sandsea, you'll see seven new hunts available. Might as well grab them all. Most of them can be done now. Some will be done while progressing the story. None of them have petitioners here in **Rabanastre**, so we will need to do some travelling.

Before that, however, go vist Montblan in Clan HQ. He should have quite a few rewards for you. What I receive was for reaching Headhunter rank: _Ethers (x2)_ and _Teleport Stones (x2)_; Ward of Justice rank: _X-Potions (x2)_ and _Teleport Stones (x2)_; defeating the Earth Tyrant: _1200 gil_; defeating the Elder Wyrm: _800 gil_; defeating Tiamat: _900 gil_; and defeating Vinuskar: _1100 gil_. Montblanc will also have three new hunts for you. Be sure to accept them all. You may get interrupted after accepting the hunt for Trickster. Just talk to Montblanc again.

If you want to keep hunting, keep reading. If you want to skip on to the main story and do these hunts later, click [here](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire).

### OPTIONAL More hunts

For now, I'd wait on attempting Trickster and Antlion, although the reward for Antlion (a Bubble Belt) makes it very tempting to try now. I will be presenting these in the order I recommend doing them just because it seems logical. There is no need to do them in this order, however, and you can take these on whenever you feel like it. For convenience, here is a list of links to each of the hunts covered in this section:

- Orthros - [Crime and Punishment](#crime-and-punishment)
- Gil Snapper - [Paradise Risen](#paradise-risen)
- Ixtab - [The Dead Ought Sleep Forever](#the-dead-ought-sleep-forever)
- Mindflayer - [The Mine Flayer](#the-mine-flayer)
- Vorpal Bunny - [A Chase Through the Woods](#a-chase-through-the-woods)
- Feral Retriever - [Befoulment of the Beast](#befoulment-of-the-beast)
- Atomos and Carrot - [Trouble in the Hills and Carrot Stalk - Part 1](#trouble-in-the-hills-and-carrot-stalk---part-1)

#### Crime and Punishment

Make your way to **Lowtown / North Sprawl** to find the Contrite Thief near the **Garamsythe Waterway**. He is a Seeq laying on the ground surrounded by children. Listen to her story and then head use the nearby entrance to the **Garamsythe Waterway**.

| **Notable Loot** | Garamsythe Candle, Stolen Articles, Blackened Fragment |
| **Enemies** | Lizard, Malboro Overking, Water Elemental, Orthros (mark) |

If you recently took on the White Mousse hunt, the directions will sound familiar. Take the south exit from **Overflow Cloaca** into **Central Waterway Control**. Head west from there using one of the middle of the four west exits into the mazelike **No. 10 Channel**. Work your way to the west edge and head south into **West Sluice Control**. Make your way to where you fought the White Mousse. If you want to use the Save Crystal in **East Sluice Control**, make sure you have at least one male in your party when you head into **Southern Sluiceway**. Be sure to grab the Garamsythe Candle in the urn by the Save Crystal. This will reveal more of the map. When you are ready to face Orthros, put Fran, Ashe, and Penelo in your party and enter **Southern Sluiceway**. Approach the southern edge to get it to spawn. You can swap out any character you wish once it has spawned. You may want to prepare regen, protect, and shell on your non-active party members so that they will have those statuses when swapped in.

> ##### Hunt: Orthros
>
>
> | **Level** | 34 | **LP** | 25 | **CP** | 3600 |
> | **HP** | 87141 | **Steal** | Phoenix Down(55/80%), Caramel (10/30%), Slime Oil (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Fire |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Water<br>\[Half Damage\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Sleep, Blind, Diasble |
>
> Orthros can cause quite a number of status ailments, including Slow, Disable, Immobilize, Petrify, Sap, and Confuse. It also can cast Darkra magick. Without being able to Berserk or Silence Orthros, getting Blind on it can at least help reduce the number of physical attacks that hit. Like all flans, it is weak to Fire. Important support gambits will include Protect, Shell, Regen, Remedy, Nu Khai Sand, Esuna, Cura and/or Curaga, and Hi-Potion in case of an emergency. High magick defense is a bonus. Offensively, Fira is your best bet.
>
> Start the battle with Dispel and Blind. Then try to maintain Sleep. Turn off all Attack gambits so that only Fira will be used while Orthros is asleep. Try to keep your party spread apart in case Orthros wakes up and you can't get Sleep reapplied quickly. If possible, have two party members dedicated to casting Sleep and Fira, while the other steals and is ready to use healing and status recovery items. Once Orthros's HP reaches critical, it will become harder to damage and will start dishing out a lot more damage. Quickenings or Belial might be helpful to finish the battle once it reaches this point. The Sleep + Fira gambits, however, made this battle trivial and quick for me.

| **Hunt Completed** | Orthros |
| **Rewards** | 3800 Gil<br>Horakhty's Flame (x1)<br>Unpurified Ether (x1) |

After the battle, you'll receive the bundle of Stolen Articles. Head back out of the **Garamsythe Waterway** and speak to the Contrite Thief. After collecting the rewards, Samal will hand over a Blackened Fragment to a medallion after giving you some backstory. This is an important item for a sidequest that you may not have started yet.

If you are doing all the hunts as I recommend them, you could head over to Montblanc and possibly get rewards for reaching the next clan rank: Brave Companion. If so, he'll hand over _Hi-Ethers (x2)_ and _Teleport Stones (x3)_. You can also accept the Carrot hunt.

The Clan Provisioner in Muthru Bazaar will carry the Cat-ear Hood now, and accessory that converts LP to gil - which may be useful eventually. If you haven't already, you should also pick up the magick Drain. You can also take this opportunity to sell loot. In addition to the key loot from before, you now have a Horakhty's Flame, which is used in crafting the key item Caopic Jar. You only get one of these in the game and only one is needed for synthesis. You can hang onto it until you get the other items, or sell it now. I like holding onto loot used for synthesis of major items, but that's probably just my paranoia. For sure, however, keep the Arcana, Damascus Steel, Serpent Eye, and Moon Ring. You'll probably unlock a few more bazaar items. You probably have enough gil to finish buying any Forgotten Grimoires that remain.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Pointed Horn (x2) | Burning Fangs | Soliel Fang (x5) | 980 gil | 20 gil |
| Malboro Fruit (x4) | Survival Set | Antidote (x12)<br>Eye Drops (x12)<br>Echo Herbs (x12) | 1500 gil | 300 gil |
| Malboro Flower (x3) | Phials & Philtres | Serum (x8)<br>Nu Khai Sand (x16) | 1980 gil | 4420 gil |
| Malboro Fruit (x4)<br>Pointed Horn (x2)<br>Wind Magicite (x6) | War Axe | Francisca | 4680 gil | 4820 gil |

#### Paradise Risen

| **Notable Loot** | Silent Urn, Feather of the Flock |
| **Enemies** | Hyena, Ichthon, Great Tortoise, STorm Elemental, Gigantoad, Rain Dancer, Silicon Tortoise, Gil Snapper (mark) |

Head out of **Rabanstre / Southgate** into the **Giza Plains** during the rainy season. If you haven't knocked down all the Withered Trees, check [here](#walkthrough-part-3---forging-an-alliance) to find their locations. If you have, head east into **Gizas North Bank** and then south into **Gizas South Bank**. The tree bridge exit into **Tracks of the Beast** is just across the bridge. First, you have to speak to the petitioner in the **Crystal Glade** to the west of **Gizas South Bank**. Speak to Nanau there, replenish and lost MP, and set up your gambits.

Now head over to **Tracks of the Beast**. The Gil Snapper only appears in heavy rain, so keep exiting and re-entering until you see heavy rain and lightning. Bansat will let you know when it has appeared, so if he doesn't say anything immediately you can rezone without trying to figure it out.

> ##### Hunt: Gil Snapper
>
>
> | **Level** | 37 | **LP** | 20 | **CP** | 3830 |
> | **HP** | 86956 | **Steal** | 1000 gil (55/80%), 2000 gil (10/30%), 4000 gil (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Lightning |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Ice<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Water, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Blind, Silence, Disease, Immobilize, Slow |
>
> The Gil Snapper has Ice magicks it can cast, as well as Disablega. It also has a technick that can cause the Blind status. Protect as you can against these, but the most important thing is to have a gambits that casts Slow and Blind. If you are worried about Disablega, you can also inflict it with Silence, but be ready to toss a Remedy. The Gil Snapper is able to remove the status using Purify, so you want to be able to reapply it as needed. Keep one character distracting it, while the others pound away using Thundara. It gets increasingly tougher as its HP drops. Be sure to start the battle with Dispel, and maintain Protect and Regen at least on your shield-bearer.

Once you are finished, you'll be given notice that the Gil Snapper was drawn into the urn. Before leaving the area, be sure to head to the southeastern alcove and open the Urn there for the Feather of the Flock. Now head back to the **Crystal Glade** and read the Soggy Letter. You won't be able to wrap up this hunt, apparently, until the Dry Season.

#### The Dead Ought Sleep Forever

Hike or teleport to Jahara. The petitioner is in the back section, **Elderknoll**. Speak to High-chief Zayalu, standing near the Great Chief in the center of the area.

This hunt may be done at the same time as [The Mine Flayer](#the-mine-flayer) since the two petitioners are close to one another, as are the marks themselves. After accepting the hunt, speak to Gurdy the moogle in **Banks of the Sogoht** to claim your free chocobo ride to Henne Mines. If you had already claimed it before, you may have to pay.

| **Enemies** | Seeker, Redmaw, Ixtab (mark) |

Ride through **Haulo Green** along the south edge until you spot chocobo tracks and a hidden path on the eastern edge of the area. Follow this path to the southern section of **The Shred**. This will put you right by the entrance to **Henne Mines**. Dismount and enter the mines.

As you make your way south, make sure the Gate Switchboard is red so you can move into the next zone. In **Pithead Junction A**, change the switch back to blue so you can access the southeastern section of **Phase 1 Shaft**. You can fight off the Jellies that drop or proceed. Head to the southeastern corner to find the mark.

> ##### Hunt: Ixtab
>
>
> | **Level** | 24 | **LP** | 12 | **CP** | 1760 |
> | **HP** | 22562 | **Steal** | Dark Crystal (55/80%), Book of Orgain-Mille (10/30%), Flame Shield (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Dark |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Stop, Confuse, Reverse, Blind, Silence, Immobilize, Sap, Slow, Berserk, Libra |
>
> Ixtab likes to inflict status ailments, but notice that it is not immune to Blind and Berserk - the early boss hack that makes it a breeze. Be sure to have someone ready to cast Esuna if necessary. Otherwise, this is probably easier than the elite marks you may have tackled already.

| **Hunt Completed** | Ixtab |
| **Rewards** | 1300 Gil<br>Ether (x1)<br>Soul Powder (x1) |

After the fight, head back to the **Pithead Junction A** and flip the Gate Swith. Fight the Jellies if you wish, and then head north and exit the mine. There should be a Wayward Chocobo nearby that you can hop on and ride back to **Jahara**. Speak with High-cheif Zayalu to receive your reward.

#### The Mine Flayer

If you just picked up or finished the Ixtab hunt, you are very close to the petitioner for the Mindflayer. If not, teleport to **Jahara** and head to the middle section, **Lull of the Land**. Warrior Guromu is standing at the front of the southern section. Speak with him to get the details.

| **Enemies** | Seeker, Redmaw, Nightmare, Mindflayer (mark) |

Just like with Ixtab, ride through **Haulo Green** along the south edge until you spot chocobo tracks and a hidden path on the eastern edge of the area. Follow this path to the southern section of **The Shred**. This will put you right by the entrance to **Henne Mines**. Dismount and enter the mines.

As you make your way south, make sure the Gate Switchboard is red so you can move into the next zone. In **Pithead Junction A**, keep the Gate Switchboard red and head west into **Phase 1 Shaft**. Follow it around into **Phase 1 Dig**. When you leave the tiled area, head straight south to the southernmost intersection of the zone. This is where the Mindflayer will spawn. The Mindflayer will only spawn, however, if your party has full MP. You can use items, or once you've cleared the area you can turn off gambits and run around.

> ##### Hunt: Mindflayer
>
>
> | **Level** | 35 | **LP** | 18 | **CP** | 3150 |
> | **HP** | 31161 | **Steal** | Pebble (55/80%), Float Mote (10/30%), Vanishga Mote (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | N/A |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Blind, Sap, Slow, Berserk |
>
> The Mindflayer is susceptible to Berserk, Blind, and Slow - which will greatly limit its ability to cast magick. Plan to start the battle with those. Unfortunately, its most dangerous magick can still be used: Invert. This swaps your HP and MP, so be ready to heal up with Cura, Curaga, or Hi-Potions. It can buff itself with Hero's March, so be sure to Dispel it. If you use a Dispel gambit, be sure to give it a specific criteria other than just "Enemy:any" since Slow will trigger Dispel. Shell status is probably the best to tie a Dispel gambit to. Mindflayer can also use a Time Requiem technick that causes Stop, so protect yourself with Shell and be ready to use a Chronos Tear. Once it's HP reaches 20%, it can fully heal itself once with Soul Etude. The other technicks it can use cause Blind and Sap (Dark Shock) or raise its level (Mystery Waltz). The Mindflayer is resistant to all magick elements, so focus on physical attacks. This also conserves your MP in case Invert is cast. This should still be easier than the Elite Marks, given its lower HP.

| **Hunt Completed** | Mindflayer |
| **Rewards** | 2200 Gil<br>Carmagnole (x1) |

Return the way you came to leave the **Henne Mines**. There should be a Wayward Chocobo nearby that you can hop on and ride back to **Jahara**. Speak with Warrior Guromu to receive your reward. He will also tell you that Geomancer Yugelo wants a word with you. He is sitting in front of a tent a bit to the north of where you are standing. If you haven't unlocked 10 Espers, however, he won't be willing to set you on his task. If you do, click [here](#lists-for-trophies) to go to the part of the walkthrough where his task is covered.

#### A Chase Through the Woods

Teleport or hike to **Eruyt Village** in the **Golmore Jungle**. Nera is sitting on the wall on the north side of the gazebo in the round section of **The Spiritwood**.

| **Treasures** | 5 ( **The Needlebrake**), 4 ( **The Rustling Chapel**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Rabbit's Tail |
| **Enemies** | Treant, Malboro, Panther, Great Malboro, Hellhound, Coeurl, Diresaur, Dark Skeleton, Grave Lord (rare game), Vorpal Bunny (mark) |

To reach the Vorpal Bunny, head back into **Golmore Jungle / The Needlebrake**. You should now be able to reach the southern portion of this area, accessible via a path on the southwest area of the northern loop. The entrance to **The Rustling Chapel** where the Vorpal Bunny lives is on the southeast corner of the southern loop. The Vorpal Bunny will appear on the second square platform amidst several Coeurls and a Hellhound...and run off. Take out the enemies here. If it ran back to the first platform, prepare for the fight. If it ran deeper in, follow it and keep taking out enemies it runs past.

> ##### Hunt: Vorpal Bunny
>
>
> | **Level** | 31 | **LP** | 16 | **CP** | 1440 |
> | **HP** | 20010 | **Steal** | Drab Wool (55/80%), Hi-Potion (10/30%), Blood Wool (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
