> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Dark<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Blind, Silence, Disease, Slow, Berserk, Libra |
>
> The Vorpal Bunny's main strategy is to make you fight a bunch of dangerous enemies while it runs away. The best way to deal with this is to cast Berserk. If you attack it with melee while it is running away, you will likely miss. You can also nail it with Blind as it can start doing quite a bit of damage, but its low health should make it easy to take down.

| **Hunt Completed** | Vorpal Bunny |
| **Rewards** | 2000 Gil<br>Lightning Arrows (x1)<br>Gillie Boots (x1) |

After you finish the Vorpal Bunny, you will receive a Rabbit's Tail. While here, you can spawn the rare game Grave Lord by defeating all the enemies in the area and then fending off Dark Skeletons for a while. If you do this, be prepared to run out of the area if you spawn too many at once. The best way is to spawn two or three by stepping into one of the hallways just off the first platform, then head to a different hallway. Keep alternating until no more spawn. The Grave Lord should then be on the center platform. He has some major area-of-effect elemental skills, but shouldn't be too difficult if you could handle the Dark Skeletons. Head back to Nera once you are done to claim your reward.

#### Befoulment of the Beast

Teleport or hike to **Mt Bur-Omisace**. Hymms, the petitioner, is standing by the chocobo stables.

| **Enemies** | White Wold, Lizard, Skull Knight, Ice Elemental, Feral Retriever (mark) |

Head south through **The Freezing Gorge** into **Frozen Brook**. Take the southwestern exit into **Path of the Firstfall** where the Save Crystal is. From there you will find the Feral Retriever just inside the Spine of the Icewyrm.

> ##### Hunt: Feral Retriever
>
>
> | **Level** | 28 | **LP** | 14 | **CP** | 1820 |
> | **HP** | 22559 | **Steal** | Pebble (55/80%), Quality Pelt (10/30%), Prime Pelt (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Earth |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Wind<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Reverse, Blind, Silence, Disease, Immobilize, Slow, Libra |
>
> The Feral Retriever uses Blaster, which can cause a variety of status ailments. Try to have at least one character with Esuna equipped with a Nishijin Belt so as to be protected from Sleep. While you can inflict Silence, the Feral Retriever will use Purify to remove any status effects once its health is below 50% - and the only magick it uses is when its health is below 20%. Unfortunately, this magick is Balance, which can do quite a bit of damage. Expect someone to get wiped out. Your best bet is to finish the creature quickly by using Berserk or a Bacchus's Wine on your strongest melee character. Keep your other characters as ranged acting as support to maintain Protect, Decoy, and Regen on your shield-bearing melee character. If you have them, use Quickenings as soon as the Feral Retriever hits HP critical and you may be able to knock it out before Balance triggers.

| **Hunt Completed** | Feral Retriever |
| **Rewards** | 1500 Gil<br>Hunting Crossbow (x1)<br>Teleport Stone (x2) |

Head back to Hymms for your reward. Now that you've finished several more hunts, it may be worthwhile to check in with Montblanc back in **Rabanastre**. Your hunt rank should be higher, netting you some more rewards. If you've kept up with this guide, you should have reached rank Riskbreaker, earing _Elixir (x2)_ and _Teleport Stone (x3)_. This should allow you to purchase Bubble from the Clan Provisioner in the **Muthru Bazaar**. It also may be a good time to sell off any new loot you've acquired. Oddly, this was when I first sold Tyrant Hide, so many of the things listed below you may have already encountered. I also found myself in possession of a Feystone and a Soul Powder. Add these to your list of loot to not sell, as they are ingredients used in crafting High Arcana and Empyreal Souls. Regarding the list below, I did not have any unlock at this point, but I had encountered at least one of each of the ingredients by now - just not enough of all of them. At this stage, however, the only worthwhile set would be the Gigas Gear.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Tyrant Hide (x2) | Sipping Wine | Bacchus's Wine (x3) | 240 gil | 120 gil |
| Fine Wool (x3)<br>Ice Magicite (x4)<br>Tyrant Hide (x1) | Alluring Finery | Lamia's Tiara<br>Enchanter's Habit | 3180 gil | 3220 gil |
| Coeurl Pelt (x4)<br>Ice Magicite (x4)<br>Tyrant Hide (x2) | Monk's Garb | Headband<br>Jujistu Gi | 3180 gil | 3220 gil |

#### Trouble in the Hills and Carrot Stalk - Part 1

Teleport or hike to **Nalbina Town**. If you are low on Remedies, the Portentous Provisions merchant near the Teleport Crystal should sell them now, along with several other merchants. Head into **Jajim Bazaar** to find Burrogh, a Bangaa sitting just south of the Gambit store.

If you haven't seen Jovy yet, head further into town into **West Ward**. Jovy should be running up from the northwest. Talk to him and follow him. Talk to Jovy again once he falls down.

Now head up to the **Aerodrome** and speak to the Imperial standing by the door. Agree to have him escort you in. Once inside, turn right in the seating area to find the uppity woman Zammadria sitting on one of the benches. Speak to her to initiate the hunt for Carrot.

Now you can speak with the Imperial by the exit to leave the **Aerodrome**. These marks are in areas we haven't quite reached, so it's time to get back onto the main storyline. If you want to jump ahead, however, use these links to jump to the completion of [Atomos](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire) and [Carrot](#optional---endgame-sidequests).

### Reaching the Archadian Border

#### Mosphoran Highwaste

| **Treasures** | 5 ( **Southern Skirts**), 7 ( **Summit Path**), 5 ( **Empyrean Way**), 5 ( **Rays of Ashen Light**), 4 ( **Babbling Vale**), 5 ( **Trail of Sky-flung Stone**), 9 ( **Northern Skirts**), 2 ( **Halny Crossing**), 4 ( **Skyreach Ridge**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Thief's Cuffs, Turtleshell Choker, Gillie Boots, Elixir, Dispel Mote, Reflectga Mote |
| **Enemies** | Worgern, Slaven Wilder, Humbaba, Seeq Cateran, Clay Golem, Python, Vulture, Fire Elemental, Alpha Worgen,Wary Wolf (rare game), Atomos (mark) |

If you have been following along with the optional sections, you should be in **Nalbina Town**. If not, hike or teleport there and head into the **Mosphoran Highwaste** using the northwest exit. If you did the [Patient in the Desert](#patient-in-the-desert) quest earlier, you have probably explored the first couple of areas. If you didn't, check back there for descriptions of loot in **Southern Skirts** and **Summit Path**.

From **Summit Path**, take the more eastern north exit into **Empyrean Way**. The treasures only hold items here. You may notice, however, that you cannot access the whole area yet - including the region where the rare game Grey Molter appears. For now, take the north exit to **Rays of Ashen Light**. Again, you can't reach all of the areas here. Head into **Babbling Vale** by taking either of the two west exits. There are four treasure barrels, all guaranteed to appear, but only one reoccurs and contains a trifling amount of gil. The others include one by the northern exit that contains Thief's Cuffs, and two that aren't immediately accessible: one with 6636 gil and one with a Turtleshell Choker. Be sure to speak with Va'Kansa, the Bangaa standing by the merchant Luccio in the center of the area. This will kick of the hunt for Braegh.

While here, might as well improve your gear and buy new magicks from Luccio. If your curious about what gear you can loot ahead so as not to spend money, here's the list:

- Mosphoran Highwaste
  - Metal Jerkin
  - Black Garb
  - Diamond Armor
  - Dragon Shield
  - Giant Stonebow
  - Avenger
- Salikawood
  - Gokuu Pole
  - Chaos Mace
  - Bastard Sword

As you can see, headwear won't be found, and weapons you don't already have mostly won't be available until **Salikawood**. Purchase what you need or don't feel like farming. When ready, head north into **Trail of Sky-flung Stone**.

This zone has two parallel paths. The northern path has a guaranteed one-time chest with Gillie Boots near the center. The southern path has two treasures that might appear containing Diamond Armor and Black Garb. This area is filled with pop-up Pythons and flying Vultures. Be sure to have some long-range weapons equipped to handle the Vultures. Poles are considered long-range melee. On the east edge of the zone, strike the Weathered Rock to make a new path to **Empyrean Way**. There's no need to head that way, though, unless you need a quick exit for farming armor. Once you are ready, head north into **Northern Skirts**.

The Metal Jerkin can be found in a treasure all the way to the west end of the southern path. If you need multiple of those, it's best to farm them now before moving to the northern section. Before farming the gear in that area, however, you need to deal with your mark: Atomos.

> ##### Hunt: Atomos
>
>
> | **Level** | 33 | **LP** | 16 | **CP** | 2070 |
> | **HP** | 40020 | **Steal** | Potion (55/80%), Tanned Giantskin (10/30%), Gemini Gem (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Dark<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Sleep, Blind, Silence, Disease, Disable, Immobilize, Sap, Slow, Libra |
>
> Atomos may have some Vultures around. If possible, take them down separately. Atomos hits hard and ignores evasion (sorry, shield-users). He will also remove status effects with Purify and then use White Wind to give itself immunity for a couple of minutes. Obviously, however, Sleep can avoid that since it won't be awake to cast Purify. The tricky thing about Sleep, however, is that physical attacks will wake it up and this thing is fairly magick resistant.
>
> Obviously, start the battle off with Dispel to remove the Protect, Shell, and Regen. After that, Blind can still help a bit as it will cause it to waste a couple of turns to cast Purify and White Wind, but the main goal is to damage it quickly so don't tie Blind to a gambit. It has some multi-target attacks, so using two ranged characters can be helpful. Between 20% and 50% health, it can heal itself, so try to push through that quickly. If you are struggling, consider using a Quickening. Once it is below 20%, it will begin to attack very quickly, so keep up Protect and Regen. It does not use any magick that targets you, so no need for Shell. If you have Haste and Bravery, those are obvious boons for this fight as well.

With Atomos out of the way, you can try for the rest of the gear in the area. A Dragon Shield may be found in the southeast corner of the area with Atomos, and a Giant Stonebow can be found on the ledge just north of where Atomos appears. Move north to **Halny Crossing**, where two chests can appear. One will not respawn once claimed and holds an Elixir. Cross the bridge to the northwest.

#### The Salikawood

| **Treasures** | 2 ( **The Omen-Spur**), 8 ( **Trunkwall Road**), 12 ( **Diverging Way**), 4 ( **Sun-dappled Path**), 6 ( **Garden of Decay**), 4 ( **Quietened Trace**), 4 ( **Grand Bower**), 7 ( **Corridor of Ages**), 3 ( **Piebald Path**) |
| **Notable Loot** | map of The Salikawood, Charm (technick), Sash |
| **Enemies** | Pumpkin Head, Wyrdhare, Sprinter, Malboro King, Baknamy, Antares, Bomb, Brown Chocobo, Green Chocobo, Spee (rare game), Wood Toad (rare game), Carrot (mark), Braegh (mark), King Bomb (optional boss) |

Proceed through **The Omen-Spur** into **Path of Hours** where you can save your game. Continue north into **Trunkwall Road**. There are two pieces of gear here. To get to the Chaos Mace, head straight along the path until you have to turn. Head left (north) and follow the path until the end to find the treasure barrel that might hold the Chaos Mace. Head south along that same path until you curves east and take the next turn south into a short dead end with the map urn for the area. Then head across the path to the north and take the next turn northwest into a short dead end that has a barrel that can contain the Gokuu Pole. There are several other treasure barrels at almost every dead end, but they contain items or gil only, no gear.

Unless you are extremely over-levelled, you should not go west into the **Sun-dappled Path**. The Carrot mark is there, and it is likely out of your league. It's 110,000+ HP and major status-inflicting skills are the reason this is a Rank VII Elite Mark. You'll take it down eventually, but it helps to have Ribbons first. Instead, head east into **Diverging Way**. For now, stick to the southern path and head straight through this area to **Living Chasm**. You'll pick up gear later.

Speak to the Moogle Boss to learn your next task. If you want to hear more story, speak to him a second time. If you check your map, you can see all your targets. Save the **Path of Hours** for last and instead head toward **Garden of Decay**.

Back in **Diverging Way**, there are several treasures, but only two pieces of gear - one of which you likely already have. To find the Bastard Sword, look for the upside down "y" on the west side of the zone. Both dead ends can contain a treasure, but the Bastard Sword should be in the eastern of the two. The Ras Algethi can be found on the western end of the large platform on the northeast corner of the map. Take the northwest exit into **Quietened Trace**. Here you can save your game at the Teleport Crystal, and in the southernmost point is a guaranteed one-time chest with the Charm technick. You could head north to unlock **Nabreus Deadlands** and the **Necrohol of Nabudis**, but you'd have to fight the King Bomb optional boss. It isn't necessary now, unless you just want the challenge. You also will not be able to complete the Braegh hunt until you do however. Continue west into **Garden of Decay**.

No gear here, just moogles. They are marked on your map, so head to them to round up your first four. If you feel like it, you can take on the rare game Spee by heading south from here to the **Sun-dappled Path**. Kill all the Wyrdhares you can find and then head to the southwest corner to spawn Spee. If you run into Carrot, leave the area and try again. If you don't run into Carrot, continue east back to **Trunkwall Road** after you killed Spee. If you don't want to bother with Spee, still make your way around to **Trunkwall Road**. Follow the marker to the moogles on break. 6 down, 3 to go.

Head back south to **Path of Hours** and find your last moogles on break. If you got them all, they will give you the option of returning to the gate with them. There isn't any good reason to not go with them because you can return to the **Salikawood** at any time. If you talk to the Craftsmoogles, they all have differing ideas of where their next job will be. Speak the the Moogle Boss to be rewards with a Sash. Continue forward and save in the next zone, **Phon Coast / Kaukula Pass**, after the scene.

#### Phon Coast

- **The Reseta Strand**
  - Poison Bombs \- in the sand just north of the southeastern exit
  - Bowline Sash \- northwest corner behind Iguion

  - Magick Gloves \- entering from Cape Tialan, by the tree just north of the large rock straight in front of you

- **Caima Hills**
  - Steel Mask \- along the southern wall by the 8-foot tall rock to the east of where you enter
  - Diamond Sword \- north of the 9-foot tall rock west of the exit to **Limatra Hills**

  - Blazer Gloves \- if you enter from **Limatra Hills**, you will be staring right at this barrel on the sand

  - Francisca \- Directly north of the hidden cave exit, near the trap, west of the Archaeosaur spawn

  - Adamant Vest \- at the apparent dead-end of the southern upper path (west of the hidden cave path to **The Vaddu Strand**)

| ##### Cartographer's Guild Moogle - _Phon Coast / Hunters' Camp_ |
| **Area** | **Cost** |
| Phon Coast | 1800 gil |

To reach **Hunter's Camp**, follow the wall on your left (north) through **The Reseta Strand**, **Pora-Pora Sands**, and **Cape Uahuk**. Once you reach the camp, there will be a scene as you attempt to enter. Once you regain control, be sure to activate the Teleport Crystal and find the Cartographers' Guild Moogle just to the west of the north exit. While here, take a break to sell loot that you aren't saving.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Eye of the Hawk (x1) | Chronos Tear Pack | Chronos Tear (x10) | 333 gil | 267 gil |
| Stardust (x2) | Wing Cord | Pheasant Natsuke | 2000 gil | N/A |
| Screamroot (x3) | Potion Crate | Potion (x30)<br>Hi-Potion (x20)<br>X-Potion (x10) | 7480 gil | 3120 gil |
| Bundle of Feathers (x3) | Soul of the Fire Bird | Phoenix Down (x25) | 5980 gil | 980 gil more expensive... |
| Giant Feather (x3) | Triage Kit | Phoenix Down (x12)<br>Hi-Potion (x3) | 2980 gil | 40 gil more expensive... |
| Dark Magicite (x5)<br>Festering Flesh (x4)<br>Giant Feather (x5) | Jag-tooth Ninja Sword | Kagenui | 3800 gil | N/A |

If you have the money, please buy your gear and don't waste time farming it. Everything is available in treasure barrels - but it's a huge waste of time. Make note of the Shady Bangaas southwest of the merchant. This is where you will join the Hunt Club later in the game. Follow the zone around southeast and look for a Bangaa looking out to the ocean. If you did the Earth Tyrant earlier, Rimzat will be here. Now you can begin exploring and treasure hunting if you want.

The rare game Apsara will spawn once you've killed around 10 Piranhas. Enter **The Mauleia Strand** from the south and then hook around to the south and check out the beach with a couple of Iguions. If it isn't there, go kill more Piranhas and come back. Once you've taken care of all the business you have, head north from **Hunter's Camp**, northeast through **Caima Hills**, and pass through **Limatra Hills** to reach **Rava's Pass** \- your gateway to **Tchita Uplands**. Save your game and continue through.

### Accessing the Capital

#### Tchita Uplands

| **Treasures** | 10 ( **Realm of the Elder Dream**), 10 ( **The Skytrail**), 11 ( **Oliphzak Rise**), 8 ( **Garden of Life's Circle**), 10 ( **The Highlands**), 9 ( **Field's of Eternity**), 12 ( **Sundered Earth**), 9 ( **The Lost Way**), 11 ( **Uazcuff Hills**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Float (magick), Blindga (magick), Regen (magick) if not already found in **Giza Plains (Rains)**, Soul Ward Key |
| **Enemies** | Malboro Overking, Coeurl, Serpent, Bellwyvern, Lizard, Earth Elemental, Feral Croc, Tarasque (rare game) |

Gear hunters, I have some bad news. First, there is no way to get a map for this area until later in the game, so expect to uncover all of each map if you want to understand the directions. Second, if you thought gear drop rates were bad in **Phon Coast**, they become much worse in **Tchita Uplands**. The barrel spawn rates drop to 50% or below for most barrels and the gil appearance rate raises to 70%. And new gear barrels are almost always 20% spawn rate, 70% gil - making your odds of getting the gear you want only 3%. So while there is far more gear here than anything we've seen previously, I'm only going to mention the gear that couldn't be obtained elsewhere by now. My assumption is that if you are farming gear, you would have already gotten those that were already available, and if you aren't farming gear why would you start now?

In **Realm of the Elder Dream**, there is an Officer's Hat that you have a better chance getting as the ultra rare steal from a Silicon Tortoise in the **Giza Plains (Rains)** (6% with Thief's Cuffs). You can head east into **The Skytrail**, where there is a Penetrator Crossbow on the east edge south of the east exit, but only a 3% chance of finding it each time you enter the area. Other than for map completion, it's probably better to ignore **The Skytrail** and instead head southeast to **Oliphzak Rise**. There is a 3% chance of getting a Doom Mace just north of the eastern exit (you can buy this later if you like maces), but there is a 100% chance of getting the important Float magick. It is found behind a large rock in the northeast corner of the southern half of the zone.

From here, proceed east into **The Nameless Spring**. Save your game and speak with the Archadian Wayfarer. He will ask you to carry out a hunt for you. Ask him about the dire threat. He will hand over the Soul Ward Key, which you need to find the creatures (and continue with the story, conveniently).

While in **Garden of Life's Circle**, there is a 3% chance to find the Burning Bow in a barrel on the north wall just east of the fork leading to the north exit. There is also a 6.75% chance of finding Aqua Shot at the southernmost point of the zone. This is a little bit better than the steal rate with the Thief's Cuff from the Croakadile in Trial Mode Stage 11, but you won't be able to buy this anytime soon without skipping ahead to some later optional locations. If you are determined to loot farm, continue east. If you want to progress the story, head north through **The** **Highlands** to the northeast exit and skip down to A New Hunt.

Really? You're still reading? Alright, well, I suppose you're in the aptly-named zone **The Lost Way**. In the northernmost region of the zone, there is a 3% chance for the mystic armor Maduin Gear. If you're reading this, you must have picked up Regen already - but just in case you didn't, it should be directly south of the Maduin Gear. There is also a 3% chance for the katana Yakei in the southeastern dead-end.

Continuing east into **Uazcuff Hills**, there is a 3% chance for a Runeblade on the east side of the gap directly east from where you entered. There is another 3% chance for a Platinum Helm along the path to the east of the Runeblade. If you want to head east to **The Shaded Path**, you can save your game - but we won't be heading further east yet. Instead, head to the northwest into **Sundered Earth**.

Here you can find a Zephyr Pole and Gaia Hat (both 3% chance). The pole is in the dead end in the southwest, the hat is against the west wall between the two west exits. Both west exits lead to **The Highlands**. In here, you can find a Barrel Coat (3%), Stun Bombs (6.75%), and an Orichalcum Dirk (3%). The Barrel Coat can also be stolen from the Bellwyvern (6% chance with Thief's Cuffs). The Stun Bombs can also be obtained by completing the Bloodwing hunt that you should have active. All of these can be purchased later - so only bother if you need them now. If you imagine **The Highlands** as a face looking to the west, the Barrel Coat is at the tip of the lower lip, the Stun Bombs are on the lower jaw in front of the cheek, and the Orichalcum Dirk is in the dead end between the two east exits.

Now are you ready to advance the story? Head northwest.

#### A New Hunt

Ah, the father's missing child. You can't reunite them, but you can activate the Teleport Crystal.

Head west into **The Highlands**. If you skipped ahead to here and are interested in loot, check the last paragraph in the section above. Otherwise, head to the north exit and into **Fields of Eternity**. I apologize for bringing up loot if you skipped ahead, but there is at least one very important item here and one new piece of gear (that is purchasable later). If you hug the wall on your left all the way until you get behind the ruins on the hill, there is a guaranteed one-time chest with the Blindga magick. There is also a 3% chance for platinum armor in the northwest area of the zone on the north side of the "hole" in the map. Use the north exit to reach the **Sochen Cave Palace**.

| **Treasures** | 9 ( **Doubt Abandoned**), 4 ( **Hall of Lambent Darkness**), 2 ( **Acolyte's Burden**), 8 ( **Mirror of the Soul**), 8 ( **Fall of Time**), 7 ( **Destiny's March**), 5 ( **Hall of Shadowlight**), 4 ( **Temptation Eluded**), 11 ( **Uazcuff Hills**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Hastega Mote, Shock Mote, Megalixir, map of Sochen Cave Palace, Sakura-saezuri, 1000 Needles (technick), Cura Mote, Elixir, Bubble Mote, X-Potion |
| **Enemies** | Zombie Knight, Gorgimera, Imp, Iguion, Pit Fiend, Wendigo, Striker, Focalor, Crystalbug, Abysteel, Zombie Warlock, Cubus (rare game), Alraune King (boss), Onion Queen (boss), Mandragora Prince (boss), Pumpkin Star (boss), Topstalk (boss), Ahriman (boss) |

No gear loot in this region, but several one-time guaranteed chests with some nice items. For now, work your way to the north end of **Doubt Abandoned**, stealing from the Zombie Knights as you go. Through the door at the north end, you will face 5 bosses - the unofficial mark.

> ##### Boss: Mandragors - Alraune King, Onion Queen, Mandragora Prince, Pumpkin Star, Topstalk
>
>
> | **Level** | 37 (all) | **LP** | 5 (each) | **CP** | 1200 (each) |
> | **HP** | 9069 (al) | **Steal** | King - Gimble Stalk (10/30%)<br>Queen - Onion (10/30%)<br>Prince - Four-leaf Clover (10/30%)<br>Star - Jack-o'-Lantern<br>Topstalk - |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | King - Wind<br>Queen - Earth<br>Prince - Fire<br>Star - Ice<br>Topstalk - Water |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | N/A |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | King - Stop, Oil, Disease, Slow, Berserk, Libra<br>Queen - Confuse, Silence, Disease, Berserk, Libra<br>Prince - Blind, Disease, Slow, Berserk, Libra<br>Star - Sleep, Disease, Slow, Berserk, Libra<br>Topstalk - Blind, Disease, Sap, Berserk, Libra |
>
> The difficulty with this battle is a special skill that the Alraune King, Onion Queen, and Pumpkin Star have called Mystery Waltz. They will use this when away from your party to increase their level by 10, making them incredibly tough the longer the battle rages on. Additionally, some can use Pollen which can afflict any characters in range with numerous status ailments including the very dangerous Confuse. The queen and Mandragora Prince have healing and protective magicks. Topstalk can inflict a few status ailments with magick (Blind, Silence, Sleep) and a few others with technicks. Pumpkin Star can cast those and a few others.
>
> So...how do you deal with this? The best way is to have all six characters with at least one quickening unlocked. Run around the map until all five Mandragoras are chasing you then target the middle one with a quickening. Try to get a nice chain. As soon as the concurrence finishes, swap out all party members and do it again.
>
> If that won't work for you, notice how each boss has a different elemental weakness. Use that to your advantage in setting up gambits. Start by focusing on the Mandragora Prince since he heals and has an easy weakness to target. For example, have gambits like "Foe: fire weak, Fira" and "Foe: fire weak, Attack". Once it falls, jump into your gambit menu and pick the next in the list. If you can knock out the prince and queen quickly, you won't have to deal with as much healing. Be sure to have Remedy gambits set up and high priority to deal with all the status ailments.

You can return to the Archadian Wayfarer to receive a prize for killing these. He'll hand over 1000 gil and 3 Remedies.

After the battle, take the east door into **The Acolyte's Burden**. If you turn south, there is a one-time guaranteed chest with 4936 gil. Head back north, avoiding the traps. When you come to an intersection, look south for another one-time guaranteed chest holding a Hastega Mote, then head north. Use the Save Crystal...after you kill it. Head east through the Ancient Door.

Head down the steps and open the door to take on some Zombie Knights. Proceed through the door to the north into a more cavern-like area. At the fork, head either direction for now as you are heading for the opposite side of the small loop to grab a one-time guaranteed chest with a Shock Mote by the glowing wall. Now follow the path east, ignoring forks to the north, until you reach the Urn to claim the map of Sochen Cave Palace.

In order to claim some important loot, there is something called the Pilgrim's Path that will require you to walk in an ever-tightening spiral that spans this zone and the next too. To begin, continue north from the map urn and step into the next zone, **Falls of Time**. Immediately turn around and walk back into **Mirror of the Soul**. Follow the path around to the far west exit back into **Falls of Time**. You should get a message about the waterfall changing course. Head to the nearest north exit into **Destiny's March**. Of the five exits leading back south, you want to use the next to last one (the last being the furthest east and the first being where you are standing). Head as directly to that exit as possible, but this will require you to pass through a couple of Ancient Doors. When you take that exit back into **Falls of Time**, you will get another message about the waterfalls. Take the exit almost directly south of you back into **Mirror of the Soul**. Follow the path south and head west again. This time take the first fork to the north you come to (by the Zombie Knights) instead of going all the way west. This is the second exit north, counting from the west. When you take this exit into **Falls of Time**, another waterfall message. This time, take the second north exit from the west (last time you took the first). Now your target is the south exit to the east of where you stand, so head up to the Ancient Door, pass through to the east, and turn immediately south. You'll get a new message this time. Cross the bridge immediately in front of you and search the circular platform for two one-time guaranteed chests. One contains the 1000 Needles technick, the other a Sakura-saezuri - possibly your first Ninja Sword. Continue south and investigate the Pilgrim's Door. It will open now, revealing a one-time guaranteed chest with a Megalixir. You can also open the door to the south to create a nice shortcut through the zones.

Head back north to **Destiny's March** and head for the far east pathway leading north. Use that to approach the diamond-shaped section from the east. Travel around the diamond in a clockwise path by heading southwest at the first fork, then northwest at the next, northeast after that, and then southeast to arrive back where you began. When you open the 8th Door of Hours, you will get another message about a door opening up. While the Ascetic's door is opened on the west side, it only leads to a door for which you do not yet have the key - leading to a boss you probably aren't ready to face. We'll deal with that later. For now, head to the north exit and prepare for a boss fight more your speed as soon as you move into the next zone.

> ##### Boss: Ahriman
>
>
> | **Level** | 38 | **LP** | 28 | **CP** | 3000 |
> | **HP** | 62149 | **Steal** | Sky Jewel (55/80%), Death Powder (10/30%), Maduin Gear (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Dark |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Slow |
>
> As you may notice, Ahriman is immune to almost all status ailments - the only exception being Slow. Interestingly, that ninja sword you just picked up can cause Slow on hit, if you'd like to try it out. Ahriman can cast -ra level elemental spells, as well as Immobilizega and Toxify (inflicts Poison). It can also attempt to Confuse with Phantasmal Gaze and can inflict Doom. A Remedy gambit is pretty much a necessity. At 60%, 40%, and 20%, Ahriman will use divide, spawning a replica with only 700 HP. Since the replicas can still attack and cast magick, it's probably wise to take them out with a hit or two. If you don't and you end up with them all together, you can cast Cura on one while they are bunched up to eliminate them all. If you don't want to bother with the replicas at all, be sure that your attack gambit is set to target "Foe: highest max HP". As long as the replicas are still up, Ahriman won't use Maser Eye, which can deal a bit of damage - possibly KOing someone that was low on HP.
>
> Another trick to take advantage of utilizes the undead status of Ahriman. Casting Regen on it will cause its HP to drop the entire battle. Healing magick and items should also damage it effectively. Regardless, as with most of these boss battles, as long as you survive and keep the status ailments at bay, you'll eventually win. These suggestions just make it faster.

After Ahriman goes down, be sure to grab the five one-time chests scattered around the room. Your prizes should include a Phoenix Down, a Cura Mote, an Elixir, a Bubble Mote, and an X-Potion. Take the door heading east. There are numerous traps in this area, so either tread carefully or cast Float on your party. While in **Temptation Eluded**, you may spawn the rare game Cubus if the enemies in the large chamber are Wendigo and Pit Fiends. If they aren't, you'll need to reload or zone two areas away to respawn. Once you get them, clear the enemies and Cubus should drop down on you when you kill the last enemy in the zone.

To continue, head north into **Chamber of the Chosen**, and loop counterclockwise around to the lift. Activate the Disused Pedestal. Once you arrive at the top, you can use the Teleport Crystal to save your game. Head north through the Stone Door and into **Archades**.

### Climbing the Social Ladder

#### Old Archades

| **Treasures** | 11 ( **Alley of Low Whispers**), 9 ( **Alley of Muted Sighs**) |

First off, you may want to sell off some loot. There is a merchant ahead on the right. Additionally, you can purchase Bacchus's Wine here, which may be the first vendor to do so. Here's the list of items you may have unlocked now.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

It's a good idea to talk to the NPCs. In Archades, knowing who is who and who knows what turns out to be pretty useful. Once you pass into the **Alley of Muted Sighs**, be sure to talk to everyone around the entrance until you find a Lucky Man and Beasley. Continue on until you reach the exit to **Archades** and speak to the Imperials. At the end of the scene, agree to pay Jules 1500 gil and go back to find Beasley. Head back to Jules who should be sitting on the crates on the northeast corner of the large loop. Talk to him for another scene.

#### The Imperial City of Archades

Again, it's a good idea to talk to all the NPCs to get their identities - even more so than back in **Old Archades**. Make your way west until you get another scene. Continue west into **Molberry**. If you helped July back in **Nalbina**, you can find her again in **Charlotte's Magickery** where she'll reward you with a _Salamand Halcyon_. Talk to her a second time to be reminded of a scene from the beginning of the game. Be sure to buy any new magick available.

| ##### Cartographer's Guild Moogle - _Archades / Molberry_ |
| **Area** | **Cost** |
| Tchita Uplands | 3300 gil |
| Archades | 230 gil |

Continue west to **Bluward's Technicks**. On the second floor is a moogle to purchase maps from. There probably will not be any new technicks to purchase. South in **Nibasse**, you can head into **Vint's Armaments** to check out gear. At this point, purchasing is much more reasonable than farming, but keep 2500 gil on hand. After gearing up, talk to the Cab Guide just south of **Vint's Armaments**. Give Jules the 2500 gil to learn your next objective.

If you plan on getting the **Cartographer** trophy, you need to earn every chop. If you just want to progree the story, you only need 3. Each area has chops available. **Molberry** has 9, **Nilbasse** has 6, **Rienna** has 7, and **Trant** has 6. When you find someone with info, it will appear in gold letters and you are given the option to memorize. If you talk to the right person while that phrase is memorized, you will earn a chop. If you went around and spoke with people already, this task is much easier. Use this table to help you find the matches.
