Once you have 28 Pinewood Chops, you can trade them for a Sandalwood Chop with any Chopmaster. Chopmasters are located on the second floor of each store. Head back to the Cab Guide in **Nilbasse**, and have him take you to **Tsenoble**. Activate the Teleport Crystal to the southwest. Continue down the one-way path until triggering another scene. Backtrack to trigger another scene. Talk to the Cab Guide again, but this time tell him "You know where to go." Reassure him you're serious.

### Like Lab Rats in a Maze

#### Draklor Laboratory / 66th Floor

| **Treasures** | 3 |
| **Enemies** | Mastiff, Imperial Swordsman, Imperial Pilot |

While there are three chests in a room in the northeast corner with motes in them, you can't get there yet. Instead, your goal is the lift in the north central area. Head west and turn north for a scene. Continue going north at every opportunity until you find the North Lift Terminal. Set the destination as 67F.

#### Draklor Laboratory / 67th Floor

| **Treasures** | 3 |
| **Notable Loot** | Lab Access Card, map of Draklor Laboratory, Claymore |
| **Enemies** | Imperial Swordsman, Imperial Hoplite |

Head east and turn north at the split. At the end of the hall is a door labeled C.D.B. Use this and watch the scene. You'll be rewarded with a Lab Access Card and the area map. You can now enter side rooms and can return to **66th Floor** for those motes if you wish. The first room you come to ( **Rm 6704 East**) has a Save Crystal inside. The next room ( **Rm 6703 East**) has Bulkhead Controls that swap which color gates are open. Use it to open your way back to the lift. Imperial Swordsmen and Imperial Hoplites will periodically spawn from the elevator. Continue west past the elevator and turn north. Enter the first room on the right ( **Rm 6703 West**). Use the Bulkhead Controls to open the red doors. Leave the room, head south until forced west and then turn south again as soon as you are able. Directly in front of you is the door to **Rm 6711 West**. Check in there for a guaranteed one-time chest with a Claymore and up to two other barrels with motes. Now go back to **Rm 6703 West** to swap the gates and get back to the Lift.

If you want the motes from **66th Floor**, be aware that it does have foes now. When you arrive, head east until you are forced to turn. Follow the path south, then east, and open the door on your left to **Rm 6613 East**. Head back to the lift and set your destination for 68F.

#### Draklor Laboratory / 68th Floor

| **Treasures** | 4 |
| **Enemies** | Imperial Swordsman, Imperial Pilot, Rat, Judge, Imperial Hoplite |

This floor is a bit larger and trickier than the previous floors. When you arrive, blue doors are open, so head east. Turn north when forced, and enter the room on your left ( **Rm 6803 East**) to change the Bulkhead Controls to open red. If you go all the way north and turn east to **Rm 6814 East**, you can fight some Rats. It's completely unnecessary, so instead head west down the northernmost passage and enter **Rm 6804 West** after the path turns south. Open the blue bulkheads, then continue south again. Once you are forced to head west, you can go into **Rm 6813 West** to fight a Judge for no good reason. Or you could skip the Judge and go south and make your way around the loop counterclockwise until you get to **Rm 6811 West** on the south side. Open the red bulkheads, and continue around the loop to the east. After turning north, there is **Rm 6801** on the right that may hold up to four motes in barrels. Keep heading north and turn east at the first opportunity. This leads you to the South Lift Terminal which you can use to reach the **70th Floor**.

#### Draklor Laboratory / 70th Floor

| **Treasures** | 2 |
| **Notable Loot** | Gil Toss (technick) |

After a fun scene, you may be tempted to run up the stairs in front of you. Hold off. There is a Save Crystal just to your right, if you care to use it. Also, you should head down the hall to the east and look inside **Rm 7002 East** for the Gil Toss technick. You might also find a Reverse or Aeroga Mote. Now you can save and head up the stairs.

#### Draklor Laboratory / Energy Transitarium

| **Treasures** | 2 |
| **Notable Loot** | Holy Mote, Hastega Mote |
| **Enemies** | Rook, Dr. Cid (boss) |

The two one-time guaranteed treasures are only available during the upcoming battle. If you want the Holy and Hastega Mote, grab them during the fight or you'll never get another chance. They aren't unique items, though, so unless you are obsessive it isn't a big deal if you don't bother.

> ##### Boss: Dr. Cid
>
>
> | **Level** | 40 | **LP** | 29 | **CP** | 3240 |
> | **HP** | 72989 | **Steal** | Knot of Rust (55/80%), Hi-Potion (10/30%), <br>X-Potion (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Immune\] Dark<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Silence, Berserk |
>
> Until the Rooks are defeated, you shouldn't bother attacking Dr. Cid. If you try to attack him while the Rooks are alive, he'll cast Greater Barrier, giving himself numerous buffs while the Rooks keep him alive. Avoid casting magick as the Rooks will cast Reflect. A nice Quickening chain with a strong Concurrence may help knock out the Rooks, which have just under 10,000 HP.
>
> After the Rooks are defeated, Dr. Cid will cast Haste on himself and then use a special attack called S-27 Tokamak. His gun ignores defense, and he ignores evade. As such, heavy armor and shields don't help much. Just outdamage him and keep your health up. Bubble and Regen are helpful. Protect and Shell not as much.

#### Balfonheim Port

| **Treasures** | 1 ( **Quayside Court** \- inside Whitecap), 2 ( **Canal Lane**\- 1 in each Port Villa), 2 ( **Sea Breeze Lane**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Muramata |

When you regain control after the scenes, you'll be in dialogue with some of Reddas's crew. There are a few treasures in Balfonheim, but only one guaranteed one-time chest with 5840 gil in **Sea Breeze Lane**. Be sure to activate the Teleport Crystal near the 5840 gil.

| ##### Cartographer's Guild Moogle - _Balfonheim Port / Sea Breeze Lane_ |
| **Area** | **Cost** |
| Cerobi Steppe | 4100 gil |
| Balfonheim Port | 80 gil |

You can do some shopping here, but the gear is rather expensive and you may need to save up a bit. Check out the now available quest [Great Cockatrice Escape](#great-cockatrice-escape) to see if you may be able to get any of the desired weapons without purchasing them. There is also new magick, four spells each costing between 7000-8000 gil. No new Gambits or Technicks. Dyce is here near the Teleport Crystal, and now you can buy Gysahl Greens and Teleport Stones. Near him is a Cartographer's Guild Moogle from whom you can buy maps of the Cerobi Steppe and Balfonheim Port.

In **Chivany Breakwater**, talk to the Fishing Enthusiast twice and ask him about his rod. Walk over to the Aerodrome, but don't go in. Instead, head back to the Fishing Enthusiast and pick up the abandoned Fishing Rod: Muramata.This opens up the [Fishing sidequest](#fishing-sidequest) \- and what is a Final Fantasy game without fishing or frog catching? We've had fishing ever since Celes was trying to save Cid on the Solitary Island in Final Fantasy VI - why stop now? It's better to wait until after the hunt for Gilgamesh is completed, however.

There are a lot of other new quests available now, too. You can work through [some or all of them](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts), or you can [skip down to chasing Dr. Cid](#walkthrough-part-5---doctor-in-the-house) by teleporting to **Eruyt Village**.

## OPTIONAL - post-Draklor Sidequests and Hunts

More sidequest content opens up now than probably any other time. Some are so big that I have given them their own sections. Others can be covered here. Here's a list of what you might want to do:

01. Checking in with [Ktjn](#walkthrough-part-1---the-occupied-city) to claim reward if not already done so
02. [Claiming rewards from Montblanc](#claiming-rewards-from-montblanc)
03. [Finding Ann's Sisters](#finding-anns-sisters)
04. [Finishing Trouble in the Hills](#finishing-trouble-in-the-hills)
05. [Finishing Paradise Risen](#finishing-paradise-risen)
06. [Great Cockatrice Escape](#great-cockatrice-escape)
07. [Finishing Rodeo to the Death](#finishing-rodeo-to-the-death)
08. [The Medallions](#the-medallions)
09. [Explore Cerobi Steppe](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts)
10. Beginning [new Clan Centurio Hunts](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts)
11. Starting the [Hunt Club](#optional---endgame-sidequests)
12. Gaining some [optional Espers](#lists-for-trophies) (still needed for Platinum Trophy)

### Miscellaneous Quests and Completing Hunts

#### Claiming Rewards from Montblanc

For defeating Ahriman, you'll receive 1600 gil. For the Mandragors, he'll hand over 1600 gil. That's it for now.

#### Finding Ann's Sisters

To start this quest, book passage on a flight, but choose Leisure Craft. Move into the Sky Saloon and speak to the Chief Steward. Ask about Rande and agree to help with his challenge. You need to show the letter to all of Ann's sisters, each one being a Chief Steward on a different flight. It shouldn't matter whether you are departing from or arriving at the locations listed, as long as you are traveling between the two locations. Here are the routes to find them and the names you'll encounter if you take the same order:

| **Sister** | **Location 1** | **Location 2** | **Flight Cost** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Ann | Nalbina | Rabanastre | 210 gil |
| Liddy | Rabanastre | Bhujerba | 200 gil |
| Twarra | Bhujerba | Balfonheim | 250 gil |
| Carryl | Balfonheim | Archades | 180 gil |
| Sanne | Archades | Nalbina | 200 gil |
| Lyseth | Rabanastre | Archades | 200 gil |
| Cisset | Balfonheim | Nalbina | 180 gil |

After speaking to the last sister, you'll be rewarded with a Ring of Renewal, an accessory that isn't easy to come by.

#### Finishing Trouble in the Hills

| **Hunt Completed** | Atomos |
| **Rewards** | 1800 Gil<br>Gaia Rod (x1)<br>Platinum Shield (x1) |

If you just finished finding Ann's sisters using the list above, you should be back in **Nalbina**. Perfect! Find Burrogh in **Jajim Bazaar** to claim your reward.

#### Finishing Paradise Risen

Hike or teleport to **Rabanastre / Southgate**. You need it to be the dry season in Giza Plains. You can typically tell by looking south. If it's cloudy and green, it's rainy. If it's sunny and yellow, it's dry. There is also a Seeq nearby that can tell you. He'll start going by the name Weather Eye after you talk to him once. After that, he'll tell you which season it is. If it's rainy season, wait for a bit. You'll know it's less than twelve minutes to dry when Weather Eye says "the Rains have almost run their course." If he says, "It's only just become the Rains", you have about an hour until the Dry season and you can do something else. The dry season will last two hours once they start.

| **Hunt Completed** | Gil Snapper |
| **Rewards** | 3000 Gil<br>Phobos Glaze (x1) |

Head for the **Nomad Village**. If you did the [Croakadile hunt](#walkthrough-part-3---forging-an-alliance), speak to Elder Brunoa. Now talk to Nanau, behind Brunoa's tent. The Phobos Glaze is one of the three items needed to craft a Morbid Urn, which will net you a trophy. You probably already have Horakhty's Flame from the Othros hunt. The only item still needed is the Deimos Clay, which you get from the Trickster hunt.

#### Great Cockatrice Escape

| **Rewards** | Yoichi Bow, Deathbringer, Platinum Dagger, Koga Blade, Tumulus, Defender, Hi-Ether (x2) |

Before running off and doing more hunts, while you're here in **Nomad Village** you can start the very profitable Great Cockatrice Escape quest. Talk to the child Terra inside the empty cockatrice pen. Hopefully you picked up the key item Feather of the Flock while doing the [Paradise Risen hunt](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire). If not, you'll have to wait until the Rains to go find it. This item is necessary to talk to each cockatrice and learn how to send them home. The prodigal cockatrices are spread out all over Ivalice. Some of them won't return home until you complete some mini-game or have already sent others back.

To get started, head to **Eruyt Village / Fane of the Path**. There will be a cockatrice next to Mjrn, close to where the Haste magick was obtained way in the back. Speak to the cockatrice Chit. Turn around and look for the red Sparkling Light on the ground. Pick it up and give it to Mjrn. There are eight more to find:

Head back to **Rabanastre / North End**. Head to the far north part of the loop and look for a cockatrice standing in the tip of the alcove. Speak to it and it will run off. This part is tricky. If you try to catch it, it will run off. If you walk up behind it, it will still run when you get close. If you can approach it slowly from the side, however, you will be able to speak to it. Use the pillars around The Clan Hall and Yamoora's Gambits to get your chance. When you speak to it, tell it "Erm...no?" Afterward, find Renn near where you first encountered him and tell him that Chit has returned. He'll reward you with a Deathbringer.

Your next destination is **Jahara / Lull of the Land**. There is a large group of Garif standing around the cockatrice directly west from the entrance. Speak to the Garif Herder, then to the cockatrice. Afterwards, speak to the Garif Herder again. The correct answer is to tell the herder where Shurry came from, but feel free to tell him Shurry's opinion of the mask first if you like. Once you've informed the herder of Shurry's origin, he'll have you speak to the Great-chief. Head to **The Elderknoll** and speak to Great-chief Uball-Ka sitting by the bonfire. He'll hand you the Gift of the Great-chief to pass to Shurry. Speak to Shurry, then to the Garif Herder again. The herder hands over a Platinum Dagger.

Next stop: **Dalmasca Estersand / South Bank Village**. If you teleport there, you should see a small cockatrice right in front of you. Speak to it. Go speak to Tchigri, sitting by the ferry. Tell him you want to cross. Ask to take Nathyl along. Now ask to cross again, but don't take anyone. Sassan will be running around now, but you need to take Arryl across first. After moving Arryl across, head to the South Bank again and finally take Sassan across. Follow and speak to Sassan to receive a Koga Blade.

Teleport to **Archades**. You'll arrive in **Tsenoble**, but you should be able to access the two areas you couldn't reach previously, assuming you got the [Sandalwood Chop previously](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire). If you didn't, you'll need to earn it before you can proceed. If you didn't speak to the cockatrice in **Old Archades**, head there first. He'll be in the northeast dead end of **Alley of Low Whispers**. Afterward, speak to the Docent at the north end of **Tsenoble** to gain access to **Grand Arcade**. The large cockatrice won't stay still for you, but should be easier to catch than Renn. If you spoke with the cockatrice in **Old Archades / Alley of Low Whispers**, then you can tell Agytha. She'll run off after giving you a Tumulus.

Make your way to **Balfonheim Port / Sea Breeze Lane**. Speak to the Chocobo Wrangler by the pen. Let her see the feather, when asked. About that time, Miffed Moogle should walk up. Talk to him, then to Gurdy (the flying moogle in pink that rents out chocobos). The Miffed Moogle will hand over a Defender as thanks.

Now return to **Giza Plains /** **Nomad Village** during the Dry season. You can speak to each of the cockatrices (Agytha and Renn are most amusing), but speaking to Terra finishes the quest and nets you two Hi-Ethers.

#### Finishing Rodeo to the Death

First up, Braegh. Teleport to **The Salikawood / Quietened Trace**. The area to the northwest **Grand Bower** has a boss you must defeat before accessing the zone with Braegh.

> ##### Boss: King Bomb
>
>
> | **Level** | 34 | **LP** | 22 | **CP** | 2050 |
> | **HP** | 37596 | **Steal** | Bomb Shell (55/80%), Fire Crystal (10/30%), Bomb Fragment (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Water |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Fire<br>\[Half Damage\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Reverse, Silence, Oil, Disease, Immobilize, Sap, Slow |
>
> Unsurprisingly, King Bomb can cast Fira. It can also summon other Bombs and have them explode on you. If you manage to silence King Bomb and Immobilize it, the battle can be pretty easy by using ranged attacks (a gun with Aqua Shot, if you have it) and casting water-based magick or motes. If you don't silence it, be sure to have gambits set up to remove Oil with Handkerchiefs. The most irritating aspect of the battle is King Bomb can use Renew, completely restoring its health. It's guaranteed to attempt it at least once when its health drops below 20%. If possible, use Quickenings to finish it off before Renew occurs, otherwise you will have to deal with the foe while it also has Reduced Damage active. Of course, any protective statuses like Protect and Regen are a big help. Shell is only useful if you don't Silence the boss.

Now you can access the rest of **Salikawood** as well as reach the **Nabreus Deadlands** and **Necrohol of Nabudis**. To find Braegh, take the western exit from **Grand Bower** into **Corridor of Ages**. Follow the path north and then back south toward the southwest exit.

> ##### Hunt: Braegh
>
>
> | **Level** | 35 | **LP** | 17 | **CP** | 2340 |
> | **HP** | 43283 | **Steal** | Storm Magicite (55/80%), Storm Crystal (10/30%), Grimoire Aidhed (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Ice |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Lightning<br>\[Immune\] Fire, Water, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Reverse, Sleep, Silence, Disease, Disable, Immobilize, Sap |
>
> Braegh reminds me of Ixion from Final Fantasy X, so be prepared to resist Lightning. If you have Diamond Armlets (obtainable from a chest in Trial Mode level 1), those nullify lightning damage. That doesn't solve all your problems, however. Braegh can inflict Bleed and Immobilize. It can also cast Invert, which swaps HP and MP for the target. This doesn't happen often, but is guaranteed to happen at least once. If you plan on healing with items, you could go in wearing Mirror Mail to ensure that Invert gets reflected. Ice magick and Icebrands can make this battle go quickly.

| **Hunt Completed** | Braegh |
| **Rewards** | 1700 Gil<br>Caliper (x1)<br>Hi-Ether (x1) |

While here, now's your chance to take out the rare game Wood Toad. South of the north exit is a staircase leading down to the water's edge. On the platform where the stairs turn, the Wood Toad may spawn, but it is invisible and docile. You can get it to attack by reflecting an offensive spell in its vicinity. You will know you have it close when you see a distortion shaped like a toad.

Return to Va'Kansa in **Mosphoran Highwaste / Babbling Vale** to claim your reward.

#### The Medallions

| **Notable Loot** | map of Nabreus Deadlands |

To initiate this quest, you need to speak to Ma'kleou in **Nabreus Deadlands** / **The Muted Scarp**. To reach him, you will have to have already defeated King Bomb in **Salikawood / Grand Bower**. From there, take the north exit to **Piebald Path** and the north exit from there into **Nabreus Deadlands / Greencrag**. The Baknamy here hit hard, so be prepared to heal up, cast quickenings, or have decoy on shield user. This zone is very short and only has the three Baknamy (two begin invisible), so you could also just flee into the next zone if you aren't high enough level yet. Once you reach **The Muted Scarp**, you find Ma'kleou overlooking the ruins of Nabudis. He will tell you of his followers. You have two options now. You can peak further into **Nabreus Deadlands** to grab the map urn in the northwest corner of **Vale of Lingering Sorrow** (just follow the shoreline on the left) if you feel strong enough, or you can proceed.

| **Notable Loot** | Dusty Letter |

When ready, head back to **Rabanastre** and to **Old Dalan's Place**. Speak to Roh'kenmou. He'll give you some instructions. If you completed the [Orthros](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire) hunt, you should already have one medallion that you can hand over immediately. Now leave and find the residence in **Lowton North Sprawl** by heading straight north from Dalan's place as far as you can go. Go into the Private Residence there. This is only accessible if you took care of the [Wraith](#walkthrough-part-1---the-occupied-city), one of the early hunts. Speak to Deeg and then read the Letter on the table. If you haven't completed the hunt for [White Mousse](#walkthrough-part-4---infiltrating-the-empire), you'll need to complete it to get the Sluice Gate Key needed to solve the letter's riddle. If you have, continue on.

| **Notable Loot** | Dull Fragment |

Make your way to **Garamsythe Waterway / Central Waterway Control**. The floor is a map of sorts, and the letter tells you the order to shut the gates. Start by opening them all up by turning off any that have a light on (if this is your first time coming in, it should only be #10 on the west). If you don't want to solve the puzzle on your own, here is the order:

1. East: Close #11
2. Southeast: Close #4
3. East: Open #11
4. Southwest: Close #3
5. Southeast: Open #4

After that, you should hear a faint sound. Walk to to outer perimeter to find a shiny object at the southernmost point. Pick it up to claim the Dull Fragment.

| **Notable Loot** | Grimy Fragment |

Leave **Garamsythe Waterway** and look for Kytes hanging out near the exit. Speak to him. His clue doesn't narrow it down too much, so if you want some help, speak to Curious Woman by the fountain in **Southern Plaza**. So that doesn't sound like a clue, but it is. Again, you can try to sort it out on your own, or find the Bangaa merchant up the stairs from the Clan Provisioner in **Muthru Bazaar**. Your next lead is the Sotted Imperial crouched next to a glass of ale in Yugri's Magicks. This should be straightforward \- but isn't. The woman isn't at the fountain when you look for her. Now, when you don't know where else to turn, look for your friends. Talk to Kyte in **Lowtown / North Sprawl** where you spoke to her earlier. Filo can be found in **Lowtown / South Sprawl** just around the corner north and east from Old Dalan's Place. Let her know what you need. Finally, you don't have to walk, Filo will escort you if you agree to it. Speak to the person you came to see. After the conversation, you'll receive the Grimy Fragment. Take your recent acquisitions back to Roh'kenmou in **Old Dalan's Place**. He'll reforge the Medallion of Bravery and head off.

| **Notable Loot** | Moonsilver Medallion |

Now that you found one of Ma'kleou's followers and helped him obtain a medallion, time to help the other. Make your way to **Archades / Molberry** and head into Charlotte's Magickery. Speak to Roh'kenmu to find out who has the Moonsilver Medallion. Otto is sitting with a woman, overlooking the channel in the northernmost path in **Old Archades / Alley of Muted Sighs**. Return the new and improved medallion to a relieved Roh'kenmu.

| **Treasures** | 2 ( **Greencrag**), 4 ( **Vale of Lingering Sorrow**), 6 ( **Echoes of the Past**), 12 ( **The Slumbermead**), 3 ( **The Fog Mutters**) |
| **Notable Loot** | map of Nabreus Deadlands, Mythril Blade, Medallion of Bravery, Medallion of Love, Lusterless Medallion |
| **Enemies** | Baknamy, Crusader, Banshee, Emeralditan, Dead Bones, Leamonde Enntite, Foobar, Focalor, Crystalbug, Velelu (rare game) |

Now you can return to Ma'kleou in **Nabreus Deadlands / The Muted Scarp**. The fastest way is to telport to **Salikawood** and retrace your earlier steps. Ma'kleou will tell you where the next medallion is - and what they are for. Just to be clear, you should probably be fairly high-level (maybe 60?) before attempting to finish this quest. You may be able to survive much of it at level 40, but the bosses at the end (especially Chaos) may prove too much.

If you're ready to head for the medallion, save your game and head into **Vale of Lingering Sorrow**. If you didn't grab it earlier, be sure to pick up the map from the urn in the northwest corner. From here you can head north through **Hope's Reach**(which has no treasure) or the long way through **Echoes of the Past**. There is a one-time chest with a Holy Mote in **Echoes of the Past** as well as some other treasure. Stun Bombs can show up along the north edge, if you don't have those and like hand bombs. Whichever route you take, be sure to pop your head into the other for the Cartographer trophy.

**The Slumbermead** has a one-time guaranteed chest with a Mythril Blade in the northeast corner. You can also find Fiery Arrows in the southeast corner, Stone Bolts where the map narrows on the north side, and Maduin Gear a short distance to the west of the place where you can find the Stone Bolts. Your destination here is the northwest corner. There is a path not on the map, but watch out for a hidden Foobar that can confuse the entire party. Follow this around to **The Fog Mutters**. There are three guaranteed one-time chests. The first is to the left and holds an Elixir. The second is at the north side of the loop and has 4416 gil. The last is in the westernmost point with a phial of Serum. You can also cause the Velelu rare game to spawn near the entrance by having one of your party below 20% health. It has a very low chance to drop the Durandal sword.

Continue to **Overlooking Eternity**. Work your way carefully through the hordes of undead to the top of the area. Approach the temple to trigger a scene. Work your way back down and out through **The Fog Mutters** and into **The Slumbermead**. This time, you will take the northeast exit into **Succor Midst Sorrow**. At level 40, this Crystalbug is very challenging, but beatable. Manually control your healer and keep him/her back. Shell is almost a must in this fight. Protect does nothing. Once it falls, you have a new Teleport Crystal.

Continuing with this quest will pit you against some very high-level foes, bosses, and eventually one of the toughest Espers in the game. Check out the section on obtaining [Chaos](#lists-for-trophies) for the remainder of the quest when you feel ready. I'll recommend a point later in the walkthrough when it may be more feasible.

#### Exploring Cerobi Steppe

| **Treasures** | 15 ( **South Liavell Hills**), 13 ( **North Liavell Hills**), 14 ( **The Terraced Bank**), 15 ( **Feddik River**), 14 ( **The Northsward**), 14 ( **Crossfield**), 16 ( **Old Elanise Road**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Cleanse (magick), Telekinesis (technick) |
| **Enemies** | Bandercoeurl, Shield Wyrm, Adamantitan, Charybterix, Bellwyvern, Silver Lobo, Bangaa Thief, Seeq Explorer, Undin Entite, Etherian (rare game) |

Some of the absolute best loot in the game is in **Cerobi Steppe**. Almost every chest can contain a piece of gear or a very powerful item. Two caveats, however. First, unless you have a Diamond Armlet equipped, you will only get Knots of Rust. Second, the odds of getting the gear or item is extremely small. **South Liavell Hills** has the best odds at 1.6% chance per item per zone entry (20% chest spawn, 80% chance for item, 10% chance of rare instead of Knot of Rust). No other zone comes close to those odds, with most zones suffering from a 0.2% - 0.4% chance for the item you want. The only realistic way to get all the gear you want hear is to use RNG manipulation tricks with a "Reks save". Even so, it's not easy to get it to work all the time. There are guides available on how to get the gear you want, but I'd rather use the bazaar to get anything I really want whenever possible. Even so, there is a magick and technick only available here, and you need to go through the area for your Cartographer trophy. As such, you might as well keep a Diamond Armlet equipped and hope for the best. Cleanse magick is in the center of **The Terraced Bank**, but only has a 20% spawn chance. It spawns on a trap by a rock that you see ahead of you as you walk northwest between the windmills. Telekenisis technick is a guaranteed one-time treasure by the exit from **Old Elanise Road** to **Tchita Uplands**. The rare game Etherian will drop in on you immediately when you kill all enemies in **The Northsward**.

### Clan Hunts

Head to any Notice Board. You can find them in many places throughout the game, but the easy ones to find are taverns in cities like **Rabanastre**, **Bhujerba**, **Archades**, and **Balfonheim**. If you've been keeping up, here's the list of hunts that should now be available:

- Roblon
- [Darksteel](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts)
- [Vyraal](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts)
- [Lindwyrm](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts)
- [Overlord](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts)
- Goliath
- Deathscythe

In addition to these, you may still have [Bloodwing](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts), [Trickster](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts), and [Antlion](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts) hunts for which you haven't yet spoken to the petitioners. Grab all of the posts and let's get started!

Three petitioners are located in **Nalbina Town**. Head there first. In **Jajim Bazaar**, you can find Popol sitting on the right just before you reach the Technick shop.

Barrong is in **West Ward** down an alley beside the entrance to the Aerodrome.

The last petitioner, Morgen is also in **West Ward**, up in the north corner.

Deathscythe, Goliath, and Roblon are not going to be covered here. There is a more convenient time later in the game when you have gained more levels, as they pair well with gaining one of the tougher espers. If you want to jump ahead and do them now, they are covered [in a later Clan Centurio section](#optional---endgame-sidequests) after the storyline in Pharos.

Now that we have those, let's go ahead and speak to Bloodwing's petitioner. Head to the **Dalmasca Estersand / South Bank Village**. There are a couple of Bangaas by the shore, one collapsed. Speak to No. 381 who is standing.

The next several are in **Archades**, so teleport over. Head to **Nilbasse** and go into Vint's Armaments. Just inside on the right you will see a Homesick Man. Speak to him.

Now head to Bulward's Technicks in **Molberry**. Check upstairs for the Insecure Seeq browsing the bookshelf.

Make your way down into **Old Archades / Alley of Muted Sighs**. Fermon, the next petitioner, is found talking with a group of people sitting on the ground on the northern edge of the center of the map.

I'll continue listing the petitioner locations here, but you may want to take on Darksteel and Overlord now since you are right by an entrance to **Sochen Cave Palace**. If you wish, you may even want to head on into **Tchita Uplands** to take down Lindwyrm too. Otherwise, make your way to **Balfonheim Port** and speak to the Viera Wayfarer sitting on a window ledge in The Whitecap in **Quayside Court**.

The last petitioners are Gurdy, the Chocobo handler, in **Mt Bur Omisace / Sand-strewn Pass** and the weeping Niray in the Staras Residence in **Bhujerba / Cloudborne Row**. She will hand you the Site 3 Key for **Lhusu Mines**.

Now that you have them all, the order you take them on is up to you. I'll start with the lowest rank and the path out of **Archades**.

#### Shelled Obstruction

Either teleport to **Sochen Cave Palace**, or enter from **Old Archades**. Take the lift down to **Chamber of the Chosen**. As soon as you see **Temptation Eluded**, you should see Darksteel.

> ##### Hunt: Darksteel
>
>
> | **Level** | 38 | **LP** | 21 | **CP** | 4080 |
> | **HP** | 111331 | **Steal** | Eye Drops (55/80%), Aged Turtle Shell (10/30%), Ancient Turtle Shell (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Dark<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Blind, Poison, Disease, Slow, Libra |
>
> By now, you probably have some experience with these turtle-like creatures. They have Flatten and Stomp and Spin skills that can do hefty damage, so having a couple of ranged characters is beneficial. This one also likes to cast Dark magick, and at high levels frequently when its HP is low. Having some gear that reduces, negates, or absorbs dark magick can be very helpful (Bone Mail, Bone Helm, Argyle Armlet). As the battle goes on, a shield user will be able to block more. Inflicting Poison while having a melee character can help speed the battle along, but if you use all ranged attackers then Darksteel will remove any status ailments and start healing. Be sure to start the battle with Dispel. Overall, this isn't much tougher than any other turtle creature, only just a little longer.

| **Hunt Completed** | Darksteel |
| **Rewards** | 3000 Gil<br>Lead Bolts (x1)<br>Adamantite (x1) |

You can head back to **Archades** and speak to the Homesick Man in Vint's Armaments in **Nilbasse** for your reward, or keep going further into **Sochen Cave Palace** to take on some more hunts.

#### The Things We Do...

Overlord is in **Sochen Cave Palace / Doubt Abandoned**. The fastest route is probably from the **Tchita Uplands** teleport crystal, but you can also make your way through **Sochen Cave Palace** from **Archades**. The moment you walk into **Doubt Abandoned**, you'll be met by the Insecure Seeq who's eager to get this done. Your mark is southwest of the exit to **Hall of Lambent Darkness** in the small alcove.

> ##### Hunt: Overlord
>
>
> | **Level** | 42 | **LP** | 24 | **CP** | 3420 |
> | **HP** | 64325 | **Steal** | Forbidden Flesh (55/80%), Fire Crystal (10/30%), Gemini Gem (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Water |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Fire<br>\[Half Damage\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Blind, Silence, Disease, Slow, Libra |
>
> Overlord's most dangerous skill is Pyromania. This will likely KO the Insecure Seeq and can do a number on you. If you wear gear that protects against fire, it should help: Adamant Hat, Adamant Vest, Sash. Also, notice that Overlord is susceptible to Blind. Your best bet going in is to get Protect, Shell, and Haste on your party before going into battle, and lead of with Dispel and Blind. Use one or two ranged characters (Aqua Shot is useful) to minimize the number of people being hit by Pyromania and his other area attacks.
>
> Once his HP drops below 50%, this battle gets very tough. Overlord takes less damage and begins using Pyromania much more. This can become very dangerous, especially if your ranged party members get too close. It can likely cause a party-wipe and you'll spend the next several minutes trying to resurrect reserve party members while keeping active party alive. Whenever you get a breather, throw out an Aquara mote or Aqua spell, and if things get very dire and you think his health is depleted enough, get some Quickening chains in.

| **Hunt Completed** | Overlord |
| **Rewards** | 3500 Gil<br>Hi-Ether (x2)<br>Teleport Stone (x1) |

Again, you can go claim your reward immediately from the Insecure Seeq in Bulward's Technicks in **Archades / Molberry**, or continue on to do a hunt in **Tchita Uplands**.

#### Old Legends, Decaying Bonds

Your target is **Garden of Life's Circle** in **Tchita Uplands**. It is one of the southern zones. You need the weather to be Cloudy, but not Rainy. It will be the only foe in the zone, if the conditions are correct.

> ##### Hunt: Lindwyrm
>
>
> | **Level** | 39 | **LP** | 22 | **CP** | 9450 |
> | **HP** | 228468 | **Steal** | Pebble (55/80%), Ring Wyrm Scale (10/30%), Ring Wyrm Liver (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Wind |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Sleep, Blind, Silence, Slow |
>
> Lindwyrm has a plethroa of magicks it can cast, does heavy physical damage, and can cast Level 2 Sleep and Level 4 Break. So if you party is all even number levels...maybe fight a bit first. Not only that, it can use Restore twice and uses Greater Barrier after each time. It also can use Stone Breath, which isn't level-dependent. What you have going for you, however, is that it lacks immunity to Blind, Silence, Sleep, and Slow. Be sure to equip gear to protect against Sleep and Stone: Nishijin Belt, Fuzzy Miter.
>
> Send in a shield-user, Dispel its starting status bonuses (and again whenever it uses Greater Barrier), get Blind and Silence on it ASAP (these make the fight a breeze), and trigger Quickenings when its health gets low after the second time it uses Restore. Keep up Bubble, Regen, and have Cura gambits ready since Stone Breath still does quite a bit of damage. Keep your party spread out so Stone Breath doesn't hit them all. It has a lot of HP, so this is really just an endurance battle.

| **Hunt Completed** | Lindwyrm |
| **Rewards** | 4200 Gil<br>Minerva Bustier (x1)<br>Hi-Ether (x1) |

After the battle, you will receive a Rusted Scrap of Armor. Head back to Fermon in **Old Archades / Alley of Muted Sighs** to claim your prize. The Minerva Bustier is likely a major upgrade for one of your Light Armor wearers, and Hi-Ethers are always great to stock up on.

#### Paramina Run

From **Mt Bur Omisace / Sand-strewn Pass** head south through **Freezing Gorge** into **Frozen Brook**. Monid will meet you at the entrance. Watch out for gambits that target allies that don't affect him (like Bubble) as your caster will burn through MP. If Trickster isn't present, clear out enemies and use the exit into **Path of the Firstfall** to replenish MP. Every time you re-enter, if Monid doesn't say that Trickster has appeared, clear out any enemies you see. Having an empty zone can make this battle a little easier as Trickster likes to run around quite a bit. Once Monid says Trickster has arrived, be ready as it will probably rush up to you.

> ##### Hunt: Trickster
>
>
> | **Level** | 44 | **LP** | 25 | **CP** | 3240 |
> | **HP** | 61321 | **Steal** | Chocobo Feather (55/80%), Gysahl Greens (10/30%), Hastega Mote (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Varies |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Varies |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Sleep, Blind, Silence |
>
> For this hunt, you'll want to have a Black Mage. For as many slots as you can afford, set up gambits targeting each elemental weakness with the highest level corresponding black magick you have. Trickster's weakness varies throughout the battle, and when it puts up a paling it is immune to all damage except one element. This happens when its HP drops below 20%. Along with the paling, it starts using Choco-comet much more frequently, and it will deal damage far faster than Cura can heal. It may be best to have two healers and your Black Mage as your party at that point. If anyone falls, be sure to swap them out with a new party member quickly and use a phoenix down on them while they are in reserve.
>
> Start the battle with Dispel to remove and Protect or Haste on Trickster. Don't try to chase it too much as it will keep coming back to you or running past you. You can try to Blind it if you want, but that won't protect you against the worst of its attacks: Choco-comet. If you want to make things a little easier, try to put it to sleep after Monid falls and then let your caster do all the work. It probably won't stay asleep too long, though. Once it is getting close to 20% health, use your Quickenings and hope to get enough in to finish it off. If you don't manage to kill it with a Quickening Chain, be prepared for things to get VERY tough. Your best bet is to keep healing as fast as you can and resurrecting fallen members as fast as you can until the paling finally wears off. The moment it does, swap in any characters that have Quickenings available and use them. If that doesn't finish Trickster off, do everything to keep your Black Mage healed. If the Black Mage isn't casting, swap your gambits to any elemental weaknesses not already listed until you start casting again.

| **Hunt Completed** | Trickster |
| **Rewards** | 4800 Gil<br>Deimos Clay (x1) |

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Destrier Mane (x1)<br>Zombie Powder (x1) | Shoes of the Dead | Quasimodo Boots | 450 gil | N/A |
| Book of Orgain (x3)<br>Putrid Liquid (x3)<br>Water Crystal (x10) | Water-drop Munitions | Water Bombs | 2640 gil | N/A |
| Frogspawn (2) | Exquisite Ring | Opal Ring | 7800 gil | N/A |
| Blood-darkened Bone (x8)<br>Demon Feather (x6)<br>Wind Crystal (x7) | Elegant Pole | Ivory Pole | 7980 gil | 5790 gil |
| Fire Crystal (x3)<br>Quality Pelt (x6)<br>Tanned Giantskin (x4) | Emboldening Arms | Chakra Band<br>Power Vest | 7980 gil | 8220 gil |
| Bomb Ashes (x3)<br>Bork of Orgain (x2)<br>Fire Crystal (x3) | Oil-Soaked Incendiaries | Tumulus<br>Oil Bombs | 10625 gil | N/A |
| Fire Crystal (x8)<br>Forbidden Flesh (x7)<br>Prime Pelt (x9) | Nature's Armory | Crown of Laurels<br>Rubber Suit | 13800 gil | N/A |
| Bat Wing (x1)<br>Grimoire Aidhed (x3)<br>Grimoire Togail (x3) | Mysterious Substance | Dark Energy (x1) | 14999 gil | N/A |
| Deimos Clay (x1)<br>Horakhty's Flame (x1)<br>Phobos Glaze (x1) | Morbid Urn | Canopic Jar | 250000 gil | N/A |

#### Antlion Infestation

Head to **Bhujerba** and make your way into **Lhusu Mines**. When you reach **Oltam Span**, you'll get a brief scene. Continue forward all the way to **Site 2** where you faced Rocktoise and ran from Ba'Gamnan. Approach the Wrought Iron Gate in the northwest corner of the room where Rocktoise was and use the Site 3 Key to access a new area of the mines.

| **Treasures** | 8 ( **Site 3**), 7 ( **Site 9**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Multiscale, Koga Blade, Tumulus, Indigo Pendant, Iga Blade, Expose (technick) |
| **Enemies** | Headless, Dullahan, Gazer, Vampyr, Bug, Killer Mantis, Antlion (mark) |

Proceed into **Site 3**. Keep Float active, as there are a lot of unavoidable traps. There are two dead ends, both of which contain guaranteed one-time chests. The northern dead end holds a Koga Blade and a Tumulus, the southewestern dead end holds a Multiscale. The rest of the treasure barrels hold motes or gil or are Bugs. Once you've raided this zone, take the more southern exit back into **Site 2**. Before taking the nearby exit to the western part of **Shunia Twinspan**, make your way east until you find a set of rails. On the platform is a Gate Switchboard that opens a quicker route through **Site 2**, bypassing **Site 3**. Now take the southwestern exit and traverse the **Shunia Twinspan** down into a new section of **Transitway 1**. Make your way south until you reach the rails, and find the Gate Switchboard on the southern platform. Now you have another quick way out of the mines. Head west and activate the Lhusu teleport crystal and speak with Kait.

Head across **Tasche Span** into **Site 9**. Directly forward behind a mine cart should be a guaranteed one-time treasure with an Indigo Pendant. Head south and follow the path around. In the chamber with the first Killer Mantis where the path splits west and north, there is a guaranteed one-time treasure holding the Iga Blade. Continue west and then up and around, but be careful approaching the dead end chamber. You will find your mark with several Killer Mantises around.

> ##### Hunt: Antlion
>
>
> | **Level** | 37 | **LP** | 25 | **CP** | 3750 |
> | **HP** | 106499 | **Steal** | Potion (55/80%), Charger Barding (10/30%), Cancer Gem (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Wind |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Earth<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Reverse, Silence, Disease, Slow |
>
> Antlion itself is not too much of a challenge. The main concern is that it can Cannibalize the Killer Mantises to regain health and boost its level. If you can take out the Killer Mantises quickly, however, that negates that ability. The other concern is that it has a small chance to disable a character on hit. Black Belts can protect against that, if it's a concern.
>
> There are a few ways to rid yourself of the Killer Mantises. Possibly the most effective would be to use a Quickening Chain. If that doesn't take care of them, Aero might finish them off or another Quickening Chain by switching to your other three characters. Once they are gone you can Dispel Antlion and go to work.

After you defeat it, you will get another scene. Don't go running off yet, though. Head back to where you encountered the Antlion. In the east side of the chamber, you can gain the Expose technick.

| **Hunt Completed** | Antlion |
| **Rewards** | 4300 Gil<br>Bubble Belt (x1)<br>Sickle-Blade (x1) |

Leave the **Lhusu Mines** and head for The Staras Residence in **Cloudborne Row**. Speak to Niray who still appears to be weeping. The Bubble Belt is a nice accessory. One more thing to do, however, before treating this hunt as over. Make your way to **Phon Coast / Hunters' Camp**. On the northwest side of the loop near the exit to **Cape Uahuk**, look for a man sitting down called The Man Who Fell or Fallen Bhujerban. On the ground by him should be something shining called Small Key. Grab that to receive the Site 11 Key.

#### Get My Stuff Back

The Vyraal hunt takes place in **Cerobi Steppe** **/ Northsward**. Refer back to [Exploring the Cerobi Steppe](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts) for a list of treasures. Once you reach **Northsward**, there will be no other enemies present and you will be joined by the Viera Wayfarer. Vyraal can pace around the zone, but I found it in the northwest.

> ##### Hunt: Vyraal
>
>
> | **Level** | 41 | **LP** | 22 | **CP** | 5670 |
> | **HP** | 106616 | **Steal** | Spiral Incisor (55/80%), Charger Barding (10/30%), Leo Gem (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Wind |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Earth<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Resistances** | Confuse, Sleep, Blind, Silence, Slow, Libra |
>
> This fight isn't too difficult, but it can be frustrating. Things will be going nice and smoothly and then suddenly everyone is immobilized and the beast runs off and heals itself fully. You can equip your party with Black Belts to protect from Immobilize. It's also a good idea to apply Slow and Silence. Other than that, good healing gambits and high combo rates are a huge help. If Vyraal manages to get any buffs, be sure to Dispel them. If it manages to run off, be sure that all of your buffs are reapplied before continuing the fight. Aeroga can deal nice damage, but at low HP Vyraal will become immune to magick. You can still use a Quickening chain to finish it off.

After the battle, you'll receive a Viera Rucksack. You can also explore **Northsward** with a Diamond Armlet equipped to find any potential treasure without having to deal with foes. Unfortunately, killing Vyraal does not meet the conditions to get the rare game Etherian to spawn (killing all enemies in **Northsward**).

| **Hunt Completed** | Vyraal |
| **Rewards** | 3500 Gil<br>Halberd (x1)<br>Aegis Shield (x1) |

Return the Viera Rucksack to the Viera Wayfarer in The Whitecap in **Balfonheim Port / Quayside Court** to get your reward. She'll hand over a Dragon Scale that fell from the beast into her bag.

#### The Deserter's Revenge

This hunt can be combined with tracking down the Esper Zalera. From **Dalmasca Estersand / South Bank Village**, head south through **Banks of the Nebra** to the southeast exit into **Murmuring Defile**. Interact with the Weather-beaten Door and use the Barheim Key.

| **Treasures** | 2 ( **Greencrag**), 4 ( **Vale of Lingering Sorrow**), 6 ( **Echoes of the Past**), 12 ( **The Slumbermead**), 3 ( **The Fog Mutters**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Arc Scale, Toxify (magick), Shear (technick), Barheim Candle |
| **Enemies** | Suriander, Adamantitan, Bomb, Mimeo, Dead Bones, Specter, Ithuno (rare game), Mini Bug (rare game) |

From **The Zeviah Subterrane**, head north through **Great Central Passage** into **North-South Junction** to activate the Barheim Teleport Crystal. Head back to **Great Central** **Passage**. If you take the southern west exit, you'll be in a dead-end passage **Special Op Sector 5**. You can face the rare game Ithuno in here (a mimic-type fiend) near the back. Also in the back is a guaranteed one-time chest with an Arc Scale. Other gear you might find include a Thorned Mace, Enchanter's Habit, Diamond Shield, Headband, and Glacial Staff.

Now take the northern east exit from **Great Central Passage**. There is another mimic-type rare game in the dead end north of the entrance. This is where you'd find the Minibug. You can find a Gladius and Jujitsu Gi in the treasure barrels, along with a one-time guaranteed barrel with the Toxify magick in the dead end where Minibug might appear. Dangerous traps may make Float a necessity. Take the west exit into **The Zeviah Span**.

There are a number of barrels through here, including potential gear loot including Icebrand, Sledgehammer, Lamia's Tiara, Close Helmet, and Demon Mail - which you should have all of these by now. At the first intersection, head down the stairs and up the east branch to find a map urn to reveal the rest of the area. There are three one-time guaranteed chests near the south end. One contains a Scathe Mote, one a Kagenui, and the last the very important Shear technick that you can't reach without getting to the next zone first. To get there, look for a crashed mine cart on the west side of the southern tracks. Interact with it to push it over and access the west path south. Head south into **West Annex**, but loop back around to the exit just to your east to get to the Shear technick treasure.

There is a guaranteed one-time chest with a Fumarol in the dead-end pointing south on the east side of the map. Other gear you can find through here includes Murasame, Fuzzy Miter, Six-fluted Pole, Power Armlet, Bowline Sash, and an Agate Ring. Your mark is in the southern walkway running east-west.

> ##### Hunt: Bloodwing
>
>
> | **Level** | 37 | **LP** | 24 | **CP** | 2990 |
> | **HP** | 41171 | **Steal** | Pebble (55/80%), Bat Fang (10/30%), Spiral Incisor (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Dark |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Reverse, Blind, Silence, Disease, Immobilize |
>
> Bloodwing is a typical flying bat-type enemy. It likes to use Gnaw, Leech, and Eerie Soundwave. It is susceptible to Blind and Immobilize - both of which can make this battle much easier. With Immobilize on, using two or three ranged characters keeps you completely safe while you take it down. Even without using status ailments, however, it should go down fairly easy as long as you didn't aggro several Dead Bones and Specters at the same time.

| **Hunt Completed** | Bloodwing |
| **Rewards** | 2400 Gil<br>Stun Bombs (x1)<br>Vampyr Fang (x1) |

From here, you can continue deeper to face the [Esper Zalera](#lists-for-trophies). You can use the link to jump to her section to get details on how to take her on. If you continue on to fight Zalera, you can exit by continuing forward and exiting into **Garamsythe Waterway**. Otherwise, you will need to return the way you came. Head back to No. 381 in **Dalmasca Estersand / South Bank Village** to claim your reward.

#### Checking in with Montblanc

At this point, you should have 4 active hunts remaining: Roblon, Goliath, Deathscythe, and Carrot - all which take place in the northern **Salikawood**, **Nabreus Deadlands**, and the **Necrohol of Nabudis**. These hunts are all a step above what you've just completed, so there isn't any harm in waiting on them.

For now, you should have gained a few ranks in the Clan Centurio. If you visit Montblanc, for reaching Paragon of Justice he'll reward you with _High Arcana_ and _Teleport Stones (x3)_. For High Guardian, _Empyreal Soul_ and _Teleport Stones (x3)_. For defeating King Bomb, you'll receive _1300 gil_ and a _Mallet_. For commanding four Espers you get _High Aracana_ and _Teleport Stones (x2)_. He also should have two new Elite Marks: Ancient Man of Mystery and Belito. Grab them both. Montblanc is the petitioner for both, as well. Go ahead and get the details and sign on for both.

Completing Ancient Man of Mystery is required to make progress with the [Fishing Sidequest](#fishing-sidequest). While both of these may be doable now, depending on your level, I'm going to cover them [later in the guide](#optional---endgame-sidequests). It's advisable to be level 60+, but I had an average party level of 44 at this point. For now, however, go see the Clan Provisioner and pick up Syphon for your Green Magick users. Also, sell off the non-rare loot. Don't sell:

- Arcana
- Damascus Steel
- Moon Ring
- Serpent Eye
- Feystone
- Soul Powder
- Scarletite
- Adamantite
- Sickle-Blade
- High Arcana
- Empyreal Soul
- Mallet
- Gysahl Greens
- Teleport Stones

It's useful to go into your menu and sort your loot using Auto-sort after selling. This puts Sandalwood Chop at the bottom of the list, and it can't be sold. That way, from now on when you go to sell loot you know not to sell anything above the grayed out Sandalwood Chop.

If you've scoured all the area I did, you may have unlocked the following items in the bazaar:

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Dark Crystal (x15)<br>Solid Stone (x2)<br>Vampyr Fang (x3) | Crimson Blade | Kakarta | 4444 gil | N/A |
| Malboro Flower (x7)<br>Windslicer Pinion (x5)<br>Water Crystal (x9) | Double-bladed Knife | Zwill Crossblade | 7500 gil | N/A |
| Iron Ore (x5)<br>Screamroot (x7)<br>Water Crystal (x9) | Samurai's Katana | Ame-no-Murakumo | 8400 gil | 3200 gil |
| Windslicer Pinion (x5) | Phoenix Flight | Phoenix Down (x50) | 8750 gil | 1250 gil |
