Now's a good time to continue with the story so that you can finally get everything available and gain a few more levels to help take on more Espers, Marks, and upper level Hunt Club targets.

## Walkthrough Part 5 - Doctor in the House?

.

### Finding Giruvegan

Start from **Eruyt Village** and exit into **Golmore Jungle**. Make your way south through **The Needlebrake**, and continue through **The Rustling Chapel**. Take the southern exit in **The Feywood**.

| **Treasures** | 11 ( **Walk of Flitting Rifts**), 10 ( **Walk of Stolen Truths**), 11 ( **Walk of Dancing Shadow**), 1 ( **Antiquity's End**), 2 ( **Redolent Glade**), 9 ( **White Magick's Embrace**), 12 ( **Ice Field of Clearsight**), 11 ( **The Edge of Reason**) |
| **Notable Loot** | map of The Feywood, Embroidered Tippet, Domaine Calvados, X-Potion, Dispelga (magick), Feywood Candle, Golden Amulet, Bubble Belt, Defender, Reflectga (magick) |
| **Enemies** | Deadly Nightshade, Golem, Mu, Tartarus, Cerberus, Mirrorknight, Holy Elemental, Crypt Bunny (rare game), Rafflesia (boss), Preying Mantis, Basilisk, Juggernaut (rare game), Behemoth, Giruveganus, Mandragora Prince, Alraune King, Onion Queen, Pumpkin Star, Topstalk |

There won't be any important gear treasures until after you reach the Save Crystal. Our first goals are to open up the exits to other areas, get the map, and track down the rare game Crypt Bunny. Start by heading east along the north wall until you find the exit into **Paramina Rift**. Cross through so that maps will be easier to navigate, then make your way southeast into **Walk of Stolen Truths**. The Crypt Bunny will appear here, but only after all of its predators (the Tartarus) are dealt with and at least a handful of Cerberus. Go through and keep killing off the dog-like creatures until it spawns. It will only spawn when you approach it and could be in a few spots, so keep wandering around until it pops. You'll find the Map Urn for the north part of Feywood if you head straight east from where you entered until you hit a dead end. That won't make your mini-map stop glitching out. While taking out the Cerberus foes, watch out for an important drop: Hell-Gate's Flame. It is very rare, but if you plan on crafting the Khumba and Tournesoul, you'll need 10 of these total to craft the 5 Gemsteels you will need. Add these to the list of loot to not sell until you have what you need to make 1 Gemsteel at a time.

Once you've finished off the Crypt Bunny, take one of the west exits into **Walk of Dancing Shadow**. Here, make your way to the northwest corner to crossover into **Henne Mines**. Turn back around into **The Feywood** and make your way southeast into **Antiquity's End**. If you run into any Mirrorknights, be advised that they tend to cast Reflect on themselves, so don't cast any offensive magick on them.

Now that you've reached **Antiquity's End**, it's a good place to save. There is a small chance for a one-time chest with an Embroidered Tippet right next to the exit back to **Walk of Dancing Shadows**. If you wish, you may want to make another run through **Walk of Stolen Truths** to farm for some Hell-Gate's Flame before heading south. When you do head south, you will be thrust into your next boss battle.

> ##### Boss: Rafflesia
>
>
> | **Level** | 43 | **LP** | 31 | **CP** | 4280 |
> | **HP** | 73393 | **Steal** | Screamroot (55/80%), X-Potion (10/30%), Putrid Liquid (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Wind |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Earth<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Blind, Silence, Sap, Slow |
>
> What makes this fight difficult is that your MP will be drained quickly by some environmental effect. Turn off any Charge gambits, and change all of your healing and status removal gambits to use items. If you are low on remedies, you'll need several gambits to take care of Confuse (Nu Khai Sand), Blind (Eye Drops), and Disease (Serum). If you don't want to mess up your current gambit setup, you can set up to three gambit lists per character by using L2 and R2.
>
> Make sure you cast Dispel right at the beginning of battle before you lose too much MP. Also have at least one ranged character and try to keep Rafflesia focused away from that person. This should help prevent your entire party being confused at once. Try to Silence Rafflesia using either weapons that have Silence as an added effect (Mage Masher, Silent Shot), or with magick before your MP gets drained. You can also inflict Sap and Slow using weapons that cause those statuses On Hit.

After the battle, return north to restore MP with the Save Crystal. Now you can head south back through **Redolent Glade**, but be sure to grab the two one-time guaranteed treasures with a Domaine Calvados by the north exit and an X-Potion by the south exit. When you enter **White Magick's Embrace**, you will run into the rare game Juggernaut as long as your game clock is reading between 0 minutes and 10 minutes. Make your way south through some Preying Mantises and Mirrorknights and look behind a large stone column (looks like a check mark on the map screen) for a treasure with the magick Dispelga. Head south along the east wall from there to find the Map Urn to obtain the Feywood Candle. Check your map now and notice the dead end south of you. There should be a one-time guaranteed chest with a Golden Amulet at the end of it. Now work over to the south exit into **Ice Field of Clearsight**.

This zone is the first with random treasures that contain gear - and there's a bit. Along the east wall you can find a Giant's Helmet, a Carabineer Mail, and a Chakra Band. In the center is a guaranteed one-time Bubble Belt, as well as a chance for a Jade Gown and Hypnocrown. In the northwest there may be a Power Vest, and on the west edge to the north and south of ... might be a Kaiser Shield and Power Armlet, respectively. For all of these (except the Bubble Belt), you should expect about a 15-20% chance each time you enter the zone. Find the shrine marked on your map with an exclamation mark and interact with the Feywood Glyph in the Center. If nothing happens, move to a different shrine and try again. If it gives you a message about illusions, lower your camera so it is about level with the ground, and spin until you see an "illusion". Check your map for which direction you are facing and head to the shrine you are looking at. Do the same thing here. The last one should point you to the exit. If you did it wrong, you'll be placed back at the zone's entrance. Otherwise you will be in **The Edge of Reason**.

In order to proceed, you'll need to do the same puzzle here as you did in **Ice Field of Clearsight**. There is a lot of gear to be had here, as well. You might find a Platinum Dagger on the east wall and a guaranteed one-time chest with a Defender further south. You may find a Trident and a Deathbringer along the north wall. There may be a Spica gun south of the center shrine, but the odds of getting it are slightly lower than the other gear. A Morning Star and a Traitor's Bow might show up along the south wall. There may be an Ivory Pole in the center of the west wall. Most importantly, the Reflectga magick is guaranteed in a one-time treasure sitting on a trap between the central and southwestern shrines. Once you've been pointed toward the exit, use the Gigas Gate.

If you haven't already unlocked Belias on the License Board, you'll need to. If you aren't sure who to give it to, note that giving it to a Foebreaker opens the way to Horology, giving it to a Bushi opens the way to Libra, and giving it to a Knight opens the way to Potion Lore 1. If you like using technicks, give it to the Foebreaker. If you don't, give it to the Knight. You probably have plenty of characters with Libra and really only need one per party. Whoever has Belias needs to be in the active party and should summon Belias. Now touch the gate for a scene.

### Exploring Giruvegan

| **Treasures** | 5 ( **The Trimahla Water-Steps**), 3 ( **The Aahda Water-Steps**), 1 ( **The Haalmikah Water-Steps**), 3 ( **Gate of Fire**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Sash, Aegis Shield, Sleepga (magick), Elixir, Amber Armlet, Domaine Calvados |
| **Enemies** | Daedalus (boss), Behemoth, Gargoyle Baron, Mytril Golem, Vivian, Tyrant (boss) |

First things first - head west to the Teleport Crystal. Check your item stocks to see if you need to head to a city to replenish. When ready, continue west from the Save Crystal and use the Way Stone. Once you arrive in **Gate of Water**, head west to face the guardian of Giruvegan.

> ##### Boss: Daedalus
>
>
> | **Level** | 42 | **LP** | 33 | **CP** | 3600 |
> | **HP** | 65644 | **Steal** | Dark Crystal (55/80%), Forbidden Flesh (10/30%), Damascus Steel (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Dark<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Silence |
>
> If you recall fighting other headless foes, this one isn't much difference. It can hit hard, but that can be dealt with by keeping one or two characters ranged and healing. It can also be silenced, so bringing Silent Shot can inhibit it from casting Darkra and Haste. Pulsar Wave can cause Disable, so having Black Belts equipped may be advisable. It also can deal ice damage, so any accessory that decreases that damage is handy too, such as Tourmaline Ring and Cat-ear hood (if you don't need LP).
>
> Daedalus does begin by casting Haste, so be sure to Dispel that right away. If you are using your Foebreaker, it might be time to start getting use to using your technicks, specifically Expose. Healing magick damages it if you feel the need. Shields stop being effective once its HP drops below 60%, and its attack frequency goes up once it reaches HP critical. If it is overwhelming, try to get a nice Quickening Chain to finish it off.

After the battle, a Way Stone appears in the middle of the room. Use this to reach **The Trimahla Water-Steps**. Head south down the ramp and activate the Avrio Gate Stone. Continue down the ramps going south until you reach the platform with the Mythril Golems. There is a one-time guaranteed treasure (looks like a blue balloon) with a Sash. Activate the Chthes Gate Stone. Head back up to the previous platform. This platform is where the map urn will appear, but only after the story has progressed past Giruvegan. You'll need to return later.

Head north down the ramp (not up toward the previous Gate Stone). When you reach the next platform, make a U-turn and head south down another ramp. The path is linear to the end of the area now. When you approach the dead end with the Mythril Golems, the hologlyphic (I made up that word) bridge to the next area will appear.

Now in **The Aadha Water-Steps**, continue down the ramp in front of you and activate the Paron Gate Stone between the two Mythril Golems. Head back north east down the ramping switchbacks until you find the Parelthon Gate Stone between two more Mythril Golems. Activate the stone and head back to the Paron Gate Stone and notice the glyph barrier to the southwest is now gone. Head down the newly opened path and watch for a one-time guaranteed treasure with an Aegis Shield on the last ramp before you have to make a U-turn. Keep following the path until you find Tychi Gate Stone and activate it. Continue following the path along until you almost reach the Parelthon Gate Stone again. Take the U-turn and approach the northwest corner of the last platform to activate the hologlyphic bridge.

After the scene, you'll be in **The Haalmikah Water-Steps**. When given the chance, turn right to find the one-time guaranteed chest with Sleepga magick. Turn back around and approach the Bulwark Cronos door to access a Save Crystal. On the next wall is the Bulwark Hemera that lead to **Gate of Fire**. Head up the ramp to your left, and while you walk along this walkway to the northwest, hug the wall on your right. You will trigger another hologlyphic bridge leading to the central crystal, aka **The Great Crystal**. But before you reach it, there are a few treasures to grab. Head up the stairs behind you toward the southwest and follow that as far as it goes. There is an Elixir in a one-time guaranteed treasure gem here. The other two treasures are also one-time guaranteed and are at the opposite "wings": the platform to the far north and the far east. The far north platform holds an Amber Armlet, the far south holds a bottle of Domaine Calvados. Now head back to the hologlyphic bridge and make your way toward the Great Crystal to face another guardian.

> ##### Boss: Tyrant
>
>
> | **Level** | 43 | **LP** | 33 | **CP** | 6300 |
> | **HP** | 180428 | **Steal** | N/A |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Ice |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Lightning<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Water, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse |
>
> You cannot use technicks in this battle, so turn off anything like Charge, Expose, or Steal. The only status ailment it is susceptible to is Confuse. While it has a weakness to Ice, you may want to save casting Blizzaga until its health is a bit lower so you don't run out of MP (unless you have a LOT of MP, then use it early). Alternatively, you can set up a gambit to use Ethers instead of Charge. Tyrant uses a lot of -ga level elemental spells and can bite fairly hard. It also can inflict Poison on hit. Piercing Graviga deals 50% of your characters max HP, so be sure to heal up if anyone drops below 60%. Sadly, shields are not very effective, so your best bet is to get Protect, Shell, and Bubble up. If you plan on using items for healing, you can also put Reflect on so you don't get hit by as much magick damage, but be sure to disable all of your status boosting gambits if you do.

After the battle, you may want to return to the Save Crystal. When ready, use the Way Stone. Welcome to **The Great Crystal**.

### The Great Crystal

| **Treasures** | see walkthrough |
| **Notable Loot** | see walkthrough |
| **Enemies** | Ose, Mythril Golem, Mom Bomb |

The treasures through here generally fall into one of two categories: guaranteed one-time chests with gear or magick and 5% chests with Meteorites. There are two chests that don't fit the description, but in general this is not a gear farming location. I'll cover each gear as it is available.

The map of this area is completely unhelpful, so you can either check out a map somewhere online or follow my instructions and trust I'll get you everything. You start off in **A Prama Vikaari**. There is a Scorpio Gate that you can't pass through and two other gaps in the platform. One gap spawns a bridge leading up, the other a bridge leading down. Take the bridge sloping down. In **Kabonii Jilaam Pratii'vaa**, there is a treasure in the center on a trap with a Cloud Staff. There is one other exit off this platform. Take it down to **Kabonii Jilaam Avaa** to find another chest on a trap with a Thief's Cap. Take the only other exit down to **Dha Vikaari Bhrum** and use the Way Stone VII.

In **A Vikaari Kabonii**, there are three exits. Take the center exit to a platform in **Bhrum Pis Avaa** with a Holy Rod. Take the other exit off this platform to reach **Sthaana Cancer**. Fight of the Mythril Golems and activate the Cancer Gate Stone. Take the other exit to arrive back at **A Vikaari Kabonii**. This time take the rightmost of the three exits to **Bhrum Pis Pratii** which holds a platform with a Gold Hairpin treasure. Continue across and down to **Dha Vikaari Trahk** and use Way Stone V.

In **A Vikaari Bhrum**, there are three exits, but one is the closed Gate Pisces. Take the left of the other two exits up to **Trahk Jilaam Praa'dii** for a treasure gem holding Ninja Gear. Continue across and up to **Sthaana Aries** to activate the Aries Gate Stone. Return back to **A Vikaari Bhrum** and use the other unblocked exit down to **Trahk Pis Praa**. There is a treasure gem here with Gaia Gear. Use the other exit down to **Sthaana Pisces**. You can activate the Pisces Gate Stone, but with the Aries Gate Stone activated your way is already unblocked. Take the other exit up to **Dha Vikaari Jula** and use the Way Stone III and watch the scene.

Head down the ramp and save your game. This Way Stone I will return you to **Giruvegan / Gate of Wind** where you will face another tough battle behind the Bulwark Minas.

> ##### Boss: Shemhazai
>
>
> | **Level** | 45 | **LP** | 47 | **CP** | 4500 |
> | **HP** | 91136 | **Steal** | Sagittarius Gem (55/80%), Hi-Ether (10/30%), High Arcana (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Varies |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Varies |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Immobilize, Slow |
>
> If you want to be able to cast your spells, you will want your casters protected against Silence with a Rose Corsage. Maintaining Shell and Bubble will go a long way, as will Haste if possible. Have a gambit setup to use Serum when needed, as well. Be sure to Dispel Shemhazai's Haste at the beginning of battle. Don't rely on elemental magick or damage as its weakness is randomized and it absorbs all other elements. Syphon can be a problem for MP management, so try to keep one dedicated healer with two damage dealers. The sooner you finish, the better. Don't hesitate to use Quickenings once its health gets low, maybe even swapping in your reserves for a second chain.

After the battle, don't miss the two treasure gems with a Reverse Mote and a Hastega Mote. Use the Bulwark Aeon to the south, and then the Empyrean Way Stone. After the scenes, you'll receive the Treaty-Blade. Once you are able, use the Tellurian Way Stone to return to the Teleport Crystal. Now may be a good time to grab the map of Giruvegan back in **The Trimahla Water-Steps**, since it isn't too far away. After grabbing it, head back to the Teleport Crystal to get back to **Balfonheim Port**.

#### Balfonheim Business

There are new items to purchase in the shops now. First, hit up Quayside Magickery to grab Arise, Shock, and Graviga. The weapon store almost certainly has better gear than what you have now, unless you've been using RNG manipulators in Cerobi Steppe. The armor you may have farmed in The Feywood, though. Check out what you lack, and sell loot if you need more gil. In addition to what you're already holding back, don't sell Hell-Gate's Flame. Also only sell one Behemoth Steak at a time as it can repeatedly generate an X-Potion pack with 10 X-Potions. If you sell multiple at once, however, it still only creates the item once. Only sell one when you need to add 10 X-Potions to your stock. You may also have the loot ready to sell for the Mesa, now. Here's what you may have unlocked:

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Libra Gem (x3)<br>Mirror Scale (x2)<br>Tyrant Bone (x2) | Stone Shot | Stone Shot | 1480 gil | N/A |
| Bomb Shell (x1)<br>Fire Crystal (x3) | Noisome Incendiaries | Fumarole<br>Poison Bombs | 3280 gil | 6520 gil |
| Demon Feather (x6)<br>Quality Lumber (x4)<br>Storm Crystal (x7) | Mystic Staff | Cloud Staff | 3600 gil | N/A |
| Behemoth Steak (x1) | X-Potion Pack | X-Potion (x10) | 4444 gil | 756 gil |
| Cancer Gem (x3)<br>Coeurl Whisker (x2)<br>Sickle-Blade (x2) | Serpent Blade | Mesa | 90000 gil | N/A |

Once you're done shopping, head into **Saccio Lane** and head for Reddas' place. Speak to the Manse Watch and ask to enter. After the scenes, there is one simple thing to do before progressing the story. Teleport to Barheim Passage (assuming you did either the [Bloodwing](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts) Hunt or obtained the esper [Zalera](#lists-for-trophies) already). If you make your way north to find the merchant Burrogh, he now sells the magick Scourge. This is the only place to obtain that magick in the game. Once you have that, teleport back to Balfonheim.

Head for the Aerodrome in the southeast area of **Balfoneim Port**. One of the four gate attendants is labelled Private Airships. Speak with her. Say you wish the board and now you control _The Strahl_! At this point, just about every sidequest should be available, but by working through the next portion of the story you will also open up the last of the areas where sidequests can take place. I'd recommend pushing ahead and then finishing all optional material after the next bit of story progression. As such, select **Ridorana Cataract** to proceed.

### Ridorana Cataract

| **Treasures** | 9 ( **Echoes from Time's Garden**), 10 ( **Colosseum**), 9 ( **City of Other Days**), 1 ( **Path of Hidden Blessing**) |
| **Notable Loot** | map of Ridorana Cataract, Pheasant Netsuke, Gillie Boots, Sweep, |
| **Enemies** | Deathclaw, Cassie, Pallicant (rare game) |

Head up the steps and use the Save Crystal. In the next zone, **Echoes from Time's Garden**, you can find a few pieces of gear, if you need them. A Cleric's Robe might appear in the first alcove to the north, a Celebrant's Miter just south of the first alcove to the north, a Gigas Chestplate by a column just before the last alcove to the north, and a Gigas Hat down the steps just below the column where the Gigas Chestplate may appear. From here, head south into the **Colosseum**.

If you didn't buy these in **Balfonheim**, there are some weapons available here along with the map. Starting with the getting the map, head to the center of the **Colosseum**. Watch out for traps. Float may be called for here. Head through the north exit and take the first left. Follow this to a T-junction and turn right. The Map Urn will be at the dead end. The Great Axe and Holy Lance are in the southwest portion of the outer circle reached by going south from the central arena. The Ame-no-Murakumo is also reached by going south from the arena, but is in the inner circle in the southeast quadrant. A Yoichi Bow and Save the Queen are in the southeast deadend of the outer circle and are reached by going east from the arena and following the long path. The Yoichi Bow is in a small portion not on the map accessible through a break in the wall. After grabbing what you want here, head north into **City of Other Days**.

As you follow the path along, you should spot a one-time guaranteed treasure when you turn west containing a Pheasant Netsuke. In the north alcove, you can find a handful of treasure barrels, one of which is also a one-time guaranteed treasure with Gillie Boots. If you kill all the enemies, leave, and re-enter you can encounter the rare game Pallicant at the T-junction in the north part of the map. Continue into **Path of Hidden Blessing**, and follow the path through into the next location, stopping to use the Save Crystal on the way. Be sure to grab the one-time guaranteed chest with a Sweep for any pole user you have.

### Pharos - First Ascent

| **Treasures** | 4 ( **They Who Thirst Not**), 2 ( **The Wellspring**), 7 ( **Wellspring Labyrinth**), 7 ( **Wellspring Ravel - 1st Flight**), 4 ( **Wellspring Ravel - 2nd Flight**), 6 ( **Wellspring Ravel - 3rd Flight**), 2 ( **Wellspring Ravel - 4th Flight**), 4 ( **Horizon's Cusp**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Armguard, Ruby Ring, Hermes Sandals, map of Pharos: First Ascent, Demon Shield, Dragon Helm, Chaperon, Perseus Bow, Holy (magick), Gungnir, Black Mask |
| **Enemies** | Hydro (boss), Chimera Brain, Mimeo, Mistmare, Pandaemonium (boss), Brainpan, Aeronite, Deidar, Vishno (rare game), Slyt (boss) |

As you move toward the Pharos building, just after you pass through the first doorway you are cast into battle with an undead guardian.

> ##### Boss: Hydro
>
>
> | **Level** | 47 | **LP** | 35 | **CP** | 6930 |
> | **HP** | 203800 | **Steal** | Maggoty Flesh (55/80%), Corpse Fly (10/30%), Wyrm Bone (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Dark<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Oil, Slow |
>
> The main concern is this fight (other than the massive damage Hydro can deal) is Fearga. This drains a lot of MP in an area, which means you may not be able to do much healing with magick. As such, you might want to rely on Phoenix Downs and X-Potions. Similarly, while you could inflict Hydro with Oil and cast Firaga or use Cure magicks on it, the MP drain of Fearga negates these advantages. The only status vulnerability it has worth exploiting is Slow. The boss is already slow to move, so Decoy on a ranged fighter that is constantly moving can greatly reduce the amount of damage you take. It can inflict a number of status ailments, including Sap, Disease, Confuse, and Doom. Having Nu Khai Sand in a gambit is helpful. Doom may take too long to actually kill you, so only set up a Remedy gambit if you are struggling to kill it quickly. Reddas has a built-in Serum gambit that needs to be moved in front of the attack gambits. Sap can be taken care of with Esuna or ignored until after the battle.
>
> As usual, kick off the battle with Dispel. After that, if you can inflict Slow with one of your weapons, go for it. Keep Haste and Protect (and maybe Bubble) up, and keep whittling away. Shields can be effective until it drops below 60% HP. After that, Hydro gets a boost to damage as well. Keep the X-Potions coming, though, and it will go down eventually.

After the battle, there can be up to four one-time chests in the courtyard, but only the one with a Ruby Ring is guaranteed. The others can contain an Armguard, a Cura Mote, and a Bubble Mote. Continue east to the next area for a scene. Once you enter, up ahead to the left is a Teleport Crystal to activate and replenish your MP.

Leave the room by taking the exit south of the Way Stone. As you fight some Chimera Brains, watch for a floating light. It does not stick around long, so be ready to interact with the Black Orb when it does appear. If you take your first right and head to the first dead end going south, there may be a treasure with an Argyle Armlet on the side of the room (not center).

While this zone is called a Labyrinth, it is fairly linear with only a few branches. Make your way east, killing Mistmares and Chimera Brains for their Black Orbs, until you reach a T-intersection and head back west into the hallway in the center. At the end of this is a dead end with the Map Urn. Head back the way you came until you are on the southeast side of **The Wellspring** and use the entrance there to a small portion of the inner ring. Make your way around to the Altar of Night and place a Black Orb.

Head back into **Wellspring Labyrinth**, and this time work toward the northeast entrance to **The Wellspring**. On your way, there is a guaranteed one-time treasure with Hermes Sandals right by the darkened room on the east side of your map. When you reach the northeast section of **The Wellspring**, find the next Altar of Night off to your right and place another Black Orb.

Back in **Wellspring Labyrinth**, your destination is the exit back into **The Wellspring** that is north of the Way Stone. Just before you get there, you may want to peak into the western dead end to look for a treasure that might hold Magick Gloves. When you make it back to the Way Stone, head southeast around the inner ring to find the last Altar of Night and place another Black Orb. Now make your way back to the previously blocked door by where you found the Hermes Sandals. Interact with the Threshold of Night to open the door.

You are now in **Dunes of Profaning Wind**...which seems out of place. Head uphill until you get a short scene.

> ##### Boss: Pandaemonium
>
>
> | **Level** | 45 | **LP** | 36 | **CP** | 6120 |
> | **HP** | 116678 | **Steal** | Ancient Turtle Shell (55/80%), Aries Gem (10/30%), Scarletite (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Wind |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Earth<br>\[Immune\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Silence, Oil |
>
> This turtle-class boss fights like most of the others. It can cause Blindness and Petrify, so be ready with appropriate gambits. When its health drops to 50%, it will use Perfect Defense and be immune to physical and magical damage. Given that its attacks can hit multiple people, having two ranged characters can be useful. Protect, Haste, and Bubble are helpful, but Shell is useless. Notice that it is immune to most elements, so be sure to not have any weapons that deal elemental damage unless it is Wind.

After the fight, you arrive back in the Pharos. Head back to the Teleport Crystal to save. The Way Stone is now available for use. This will deposit you in **Wellspring Ravel - 1st Flight**. You may also see that a floor indicator underneath your minimap has changed from 01F to 10F. Activate the door in front of you and make your way north after fighting the Chimera Brains. At the apparent dead end is a Fool's Facade that you can strike once you get close enough. On the other side is a guaranteed one-time chest with a Demon Shield. Head back to the intersection and turn east up the stairs. Once you eliminate the two Brainpans, a walkway will appear. From now on, each walkway requires you to kill all the accessible Brainpans in order to activate. Follow the linear path. When you reach 13F, there may be a treasure with a Leather Gorget.

Once you reach 14F, turn south and head up the stairs before killing the Brainpan in front of you. In the north chamber of the three rooms on 15F, look for a one-time guaranteed treasure with a Dragon Helm behind a Brainpan. Return to 14F and proceed west across the bridge after killing the Brainpan. If the bridge doesn't activate, then you missed on of the Brainpans up on 15F. Follow the path until you enter **Wellspring Ravel - 2nd Flight**.

This zone is also fairly linear, but you need to kill every Brainpan again. On 20F, you can explore the side rooms for the possible treasures with motes. There is another Fool's Facade in the western room on the north wall. When you reach 23F, immediately turn south and find another Fool's Facade. Follow the hidden path up to 24F and find a guaranteed one-time treasure with a Chaperon in the southeast corner of the landing. There's another Fool's Facade that will allow you to rejoin the main path. Head up and take down the Brainpans on 25F to enable the walkway. If the walkway doesn't activate, you missed a Brainpan somewhere. After you cross the walkway, look to the west to see a one-time guaranteed chest that you can't reach. It holds a Perseus bow, but the creatures that you need to defeat to cross the walkway are still above you. For now, head on to **Wellspring Ravel - 3rd Flight**.

Again, you will want to defeat all the Brainpans you find. Additionally, you'll want to avoid killing Deidars for now. Deidars look like Brainpans, but they have a red flame. Killing these causes already built bridge sections to become unbuilt. Once the bridge is set in stone, however, the Deidars can be killed to create red bridges. This will be essential for reaching the Perseus Bow as well as Holy magick and Gungnir. For the time being, if you see a Deidar, do your best to avoid it by fleeing with L2 and R2.

Find your first two Brainpans on 29F. On 30F, if you turn north there is a Fool's Facade at the top of the stairs. Behind it may be a treasure with a Power Armlet. Head back south through the 4-way intersection and look for an Ancient Door on the right. Straight west is another Brainpan. Continue south from the Ancient door to find another Brainpan at the top of the steps. On 31F, there is a Deidar and two Brainpans. On 32F, there is a Brainpan and a Deidar. On 33F, there may be a Bangle treasure. On 34F, there are two Brainpans as you arrive. If you turn north and go up the steps, there are two more Brainpans with a Deidar further back. If you go east instead, you can find two more Brainpans. Keep fighting Brainpans in this loop until you can build the bridge across. Once it is in stone, kill all the Deidars you've encountered so far. When you reach 35F, you should see a bridge with 4 red sections. One more Deidar should finish that, but only after you've knocked off the Brainpans for the next bridge. Continue up to **Wellspring Ravel - 4th Flight**.

There are two Brainpans immediately in front of you. To the east is a Deidar you need to run past. On 38F, there are two Deidars and a Brainpan close together. Kill the Brainpan and flee up the steps. On 40F, there are two Brainpans and a Deidar in the large room as well as a guaranteed one-time Scathe Mote. On 41F is another Deidar to run past. A Brainpan sits to your right when you reach 42F. On 43F, there is a Brainpan to the left and a Deidar to the right. Flee up to 44F after killing the Brainpan. On 45F, be sure to check the small rooms for another Brainpan. The south room has a Fool's Facade hiding a one-time guaranteed Elixir. Once you make your way up to 46F, step on the bridge to cement it and backtrack to kill all the Deidars. Head all the way back into **Wellspring Ravel - 3rd Flight** and cement the red bridge. Attack the Fool's Facade on the other side to reveal a room with two one-time guaranteed chests: Gungnir and Holy magick. If you can't reach the Holy magick yet, run around killing Deidars on this floor that have respawned. You don't have to worry about Brainpans as they are now dormant after having activated all the green bridges. If you aren't getting any Deidar spawns, you may need to step foot into **Horizon's Break**, but turn back around immediately if you do. If you head back up to the last green bridge, you will encounter the rare game Vishno up the steps for killing 10 Deidars.

To get the bridge for the Perseus Bow, continue into **Wellspring Ravel - 2nd Flight**. If it hasn't appeared, you have at least zoned far enough to retrigger the Deidar's on **4th Flight**. Head up there and kill all you can find and check the bridge again. If any Brainpans suddenly become aggressive, be sure to flee. Once you have the Perseus Bow, you can kill any aggressive Brainpan or Deidar without worry. Head now to **Horizon's Break** and look for the Save Crystal.

Head south and enter the staircase area on the southwestern side of the ring. Upstairs, use the Ancient Door to reach **Horizon's Cusp**. Head straight down the hall in front of you until it curves to the east. Before going through the door, look for guaranteed one-time treasures to the left and right of the door containing a Reflectga Mote and Black Mask, respectively. Interact with the Ancient Door, and open it when ready to face your next boss in another illusory room. Head toward the waterfall and the fight will begin soon.

> ##### Boss: Slyt
>
>
> | **Level** | 47 | **LP** | 36 | **CP** | 3780 |
> | **HP** | 92661 | **Steal** | Yensa Scale (55/80%), Pisces Gem (10/30%), Yensa Fin (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Fire |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Water<br>\[Immune\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Confuse, Reverse, Oil, Disease, Slow |
>
> Check your weapons for any elemental effect and make sure it is either none or Fire. Wyrmfire Shot, Gungnir, Burning Bow with Fiery Arrows, and Oil Bombs (for aiding with the fire damage) are your best options. The boss is purely physical in attacks, but maintains a constant Regen effect. Thus, outperforming its Regen with Fire magick or fire-attuned weapons is your best bet. You can always take the easy route and hit it with Reverse, Renew, and a Potion since this is one of the few bosses susceptible to Reverse. Overall, this boss is very easy until its health gets low and it starts dealing quite a bit of damage very quickly. Keep those healing gambits in place and it'll be over quick.

Head through the east door and follow the path around to the Way Stone. Use it to teleport to **Pharos - Second Ascent**.

### Pharos - Second Ascent

| **Treasures** | 9 ( **Station of Banishment**), 7 ( **Station of Suffering**), 9 ( **Station of Acension**), 4 ( **The Bounds of Truth**), 4 ( **Cleft of Profaning Wind** \- only during boss fight) |
| **Notable Loot** | Opal Ring, Quasimodo Boots, Minerva Bustier, Zeus Mace, Gastrophetes S, map of Pharos - Second Ascent, Dragon Mail, Sash, Muramasa |
| **Enemies** | Crusader, Abaddon, Reaver, Bune, Dragon Lich, Necrofiend, Zombie Warlock, Fenrir (boss) |

On the west side of the ring you'll find a Save Crystal. It's a good idea to save here as your choices now affect something later in the game. You can only open one of the four doors off the ring. The doors are opened by the corresponding Altar in front of the door. Each one will remove some aspect of your gameplay until you leave **Pharos - Second Ascent**:

| Direction | Door Chosen | Limitation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Northwest | Steel | No use of weapons |
| Southwest | Magicks | No use of magick |
| Southeast | Knowledge | No use of Mini-map |
| Northeast | Wealth | No use of items |

Choose what you wish, but be prepared to reset gambits if you don't select Knowledge. If you do select Knowledge, you still have use of the game Map, just not the Mini-map while moving around. Whatever you choose, go through the door and up the stairs into the **Station of Banishment**.

For orientation purposes, find the quickest way to the center ring. The north half of the zone connects the two north exits from the ring, and the south half does the same. Starting with the south half, there may be a Fuzzy Miter in the room to the west of the southwest exit from the ring. If you go all the way south from the southwest exit and head into the room to the east you will find a guaranteed one-time treasure with an Opal Ring.

In the north half, start with the northwest exit from the ring and head all the way west for a guaranteed one-time treasure with a Minerva Bustier in the northeast corner. If you head back east and turn north when you are able, the room to the northwest may have a Bowline Sash. From there, make your way to the northeastern quadrant. The northeasternmost room may have a Rose Corsage, and two rooms south of that is guaranteed to have the one-time treasure with Quasimodo Boots. From here you should see the stairs up to the next level off to your east. Head up them and use the Ancient Door to reach the **Station of Suffering**.

In the south half, both of the two center dead ends may contain gear: a Tourmaline Ring in the west and Lead Bolts in the east. From the southwestern exit from the ring, head south two rooms and west one room. In this room is a guaranteed one-time treasure with a Zeus Mace. If you like fighting Crusaders or stealing X-Potions, there is a Fool's Facade next to the Zeus Mace. From here, make your way all the way to the east and then turn north to find the stairs to the next level. The Ancient Door gives you access to the **Station of Ascension**.

If you didn't already get it, you can find the Gil Toss technick in a treasure on the southeast side of the ring in the center. From the ring, take the southwest exit. The dead-end hallway to the east may contain a Black Belt. The room reached by going west and south from the ring will have a guaranteed Dragon Mail. Head back to the ring and take the northeast exit this time. The room to the east may have Oil Bombs in the southeast corner. The dead-end hallway to the west will have a guaranteed Sash. The other dead-end hallway may have Bamboo Arrows. Go back to the northeast exit from the ring and head all the way north. Cross the long horizontal room to the west and keep following the path from room to room until you reach the stairs to the next level. Use the Ancient Door to access **Reach of the Damned**. Use the nearby Save Crystal to save your game - preferably in a new slot in case you decide your choice of doors at the beginning was unwise. Head south to find the stairs to the next level: **The Bounds of Truth**.

As before, when you follow the hallway around to the east, there are two guaranteed treasures to be opened before going through the door. One contains a Muramasa, the other a Hastega Mote. When you open the door, you will be in another illusory area and immediately thrust into battle with Fenrir.

> ##### Boss: Fenrir
>
>
> | **Level** | 49 | **LP** | 36 | **CP** | 6480 |
> | **HP** | 189992 | **Steal** | Beastlord Hide (55/80%), Beastlord Horn (10/30%), <br>Behemoth Steak (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Earth |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Wind<br>\[Immune\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Sleep, Oil |
>
> Check your weapons for any elemental effect and make sure it is either none or Earth (a Koga Blade may be your only strong earth-element weapon). The boss is almost purely physical in attacks with the exception of Icebreak. It starts with Protect and Bravery which ought to be dispelled. Keep up Protect and Haste, with good healing gambits. Shields are also useful as it does not have the Ignore Evade that so many other bosses have. Again, not too tough, but if you chose to forego items you should probably equip your healer with a Rose Corsage to prevent Silence.

Continue forward after the battle until you reach the ring again. Find the altar corresponding to the one you touched at the beginning to regain your locked action or Mini-map. This also summons the Dais of Ascendance to the west side of the ring, which you can use to access 67F. There is a nearby Save Crystal, upon arriving, as well as the Way Stone ready to take you to **Pharos - Third Ascent**.

### Pharos - Third Ascent

| **Treasures** | 5 ( **Spire Ravel - 1st Flight**), 6 ( **Spire Ravel - 2nd Flight**), 4 ( **Empyrean Ravel**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Antares, Flare (magick), map of Pharos - Third Ascent, Ardor (magick), White Robes, Ultima Blade, Megalixir, Dark Matter |
| **Enemies** | Purobolos, Zombie Warlock, Dead Bones, Necrofiend, Aeronite, Cataract Aevis, Tower (rare game), Hashmal (esper), Famfrit (esper), Judge Gabranth (boss), Dr. Cid (boss) |

Head forward and clear out the Purobolos. If you want the map, you will need to do something rather challenging. First, set up some gambits that include using Nu Khai Sand, Chronos Tear, Serum, Blindna, Protect, Shell, Regen, Bubble, and Phoenix Down. Try to duplicate these on as many characters as you can. Change your attack gambit to target the foe with the highest max HP. Once you have all those set up, touch any Way Stone except the Black Sigil Way Stone twice. You will be teleported to a secret room. Look south to see the Map Urn. You will be ambushed as you approach. There will always be four undead, and as soon as you kill one another spawns in its place. The most dangerous of these is the Necrofiend, which is why you want to have the target as the foe with highest max HP. After a while, you will get a message saying that the door is opened and the undead will quit spawning. Grab the map and head back to the room with all the Way Stones. Remember to change your gambits back to how you like.

Touch the Black Sigil Way Stone to reach the next platform. Head east, and turn south when able. At the dead-end, you may find Wyrmfire Shot. Strike the nearby Fool's Facade to reveal a secret area. You'll find two guaranteed one-time treasures with an Antares and Flare magick. You'll also find a Green Sigil Way Stone that will teleport you to a secret room in **Spire Ravel - 2nd Flight**. There are two guaranteed one-time chests in the room you teleport into. One contains Ardor magick, the other White Robes. Step on the trap where you pick up the White Robes to be healed. The other traps aren't as nice, so cast float or wall scrape to go down either hall. Break through any Fool's Facades barring you to make your way southwest until you reach a platform with an Aeronite and more Way Stones. Use the Red Sigil Way Stone here to continue. Grab the Ultima Blade from the one-time guaranteed treasure nearby. The Way Stone you choose here depends on what you chose back in **Pharos - Second Ascent**:

| Altar in 2nd Ascent | Way Stone to activate |
| --- | --- |
| Steel - no weapons | White |
| Magicks - no magick | Purple |
| Knowledge - no Mini-map | Pink |
| Wealth - no items | Yellow |

After choosing the correct Way Stone, you'll be back in the main map. Head north and look for another Fool's Facade near the dead-end. Head down the hall toward the Way Stone and look for a treasure that may contain Blazer Gloves. Use the Way Stone. Look north when you arrive for a guaranteed one-time Elixir. There is a chance for the rare game Tower to appear here. It is a random spawn, so you may have to come back later or use the nearby Way Stone to go back and forth until it appears. If it doesn't appear after a few attempts, you may want to go play through from 79F instead of just using the nearby Way Stone. If there are too many enemies spawned in **Spire Ravel - 2nd Flight**, Tower won't be able to spawn. You'll need to clear out as many enemies as possible by using the intended method of getting through 2nd Flight instead of using the secret areas described above. In short, from the Way Stone that dropped you off at 79F, work through the area where you got your map to the Black Sigil Way Stone (which you've used before), follow the path and touch the Green Sigil Way Stone. Work then to the Red Sigil Way Stone, then use the Sacrifice Sigil Way Stone whose color corresponds to what you gave up in **Second Ascent** (see the table above). Then use the hidden Way Stone to arrive at 88F, just like before. Now you should be able to teleport back and forth between 79F and 88F to get Tower to spawn.

You can now use the Dais of Ascendance, but be prepared for the upcoming battle. If necessary, use an Ether because the battle will begin once you are on the Dais.

> ##### Boss: Hashmal
>
>
> | **Level** | 50 | **LP** | 52 | **CP** | 5400 |
> | **HP** | 209060 | **Steal** | Leo Gem (55/80%), Hi-Ether (10/30%), <br>High Arcana (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Wind |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Earth<br>\[Immune\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Oil |
>
> The two most important statuses for this fight are Float and Bubble. These make you immune to Hashmal's Quakeja and Disease. Haste and Protect are nice, as well. Be sure to Dispel Hashmal at the beginning, then hack away with any wind-based weapons you have (Ame-no-Murakumo or Zephyr Pole), but don't use any weapons that do other elemental type damage.

After the battle, you'll get back on the Dais and arrive at **Empyrean Ravel**. Follow the linear path, but watch out for treasures that will contain Dark Matter and Megalixirs on the way. One of each is guaranteed one-time, but there is another of each that has a small chance to appear with each entry. You'll actually bypass one during the scene, so backtrack to the northeast corner to find the one you missed. Head to the Save Crystal and save your game before the next boss battle begins. Head up the steps and use the final Way Stone. After the scenes, you'll face a series of two bosses. First up:

> ##### Boss: Judge Gabranth
>
>
> | **Level** | 47 | **LP** | 18 | **CP** | 3600 |
> | **HP** | 64049 | **Steal** | Potion (55/80%), Hi-Potion (10/30%), <br>X-Potion (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | N/A |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | N/A |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Silence, Oil |
>
> This boss is primarily physical. He starts by casting Protect, which you should probably Dispel. For an extra scene, make sure Basch is in your party. Until his HP drops below 50%, shields can help mitigate damage. Protect, Bubble, and Haste are probably the most important statuses for your characters. Hopefully by now, those are automatic in your gambits. His HP isn't that high compared to what you've been facing, so you'd expect this battle to go fairly quickly. The only snag is that at 50% he becomes immune to magick for two minutes. As long as you are pummeling him with physical attacks, it shouldn't slow you down.

More scenes will follow, then you get:

> ##### Boss: Dr. Cid
>
>
> | **Level** | 50 | **LP** | 18 | **CP** | 3960 |
> | **HP** | 82093 | **Steal** | Hi-Potion (55/80%), Ketu Board (10/30%), <br>Magepower Shishak (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Dark<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Oil |
>
> ##### Boss: Famfrit
>
>
> | **Level** | 52 | **LP** | 52 | **CP** | 5400 |
> | **HP** | 149060 | **Steal** | Aquarius Gem (55/80%), Elixir (10/30%), <br>High Arcana (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Fire |
> | **Elemental Resistance** | \[Absorb\] Water<br>\[Immune\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Oil |
>
> Start the battle like every other one: cast Dispel. After that, pound away with your best non-elemental weapons. Famfrit will join the battle when his HP drops below 50%. At that point, Dr. Cid is immune to everything until Famfrit is gone. Technically, the paling will drop after two minutes, but he'll just reapply it. While Famfrit is present, Dr. Cid will continually buff it. You will want your gambits to focus on the enemy with highest max HP so you don't waste time trying to attack Dr. Cid. You may also want to apply Oil and cast Firaga and switch to fire-based weapons to speed things along. Once Famfrit goes down, keep punishing Dr. Cid with non-elemental or Holy-based weapons. If he is prepping one of his machine gun attacks, maneuver behind him to avoid getting hit.
>
> As always, the best way to make this easy on yourself is the gambits you set up to buff yourself. Protect, Bubble, and Haste are always key. When Famfrit arrives, it's a good idea to add Shell in the mix as it uses high-powered water magick. Be ready to remove Silence, or equip your healer and buffer with a Rose Corsage. With the right setup, this battles should also be fairly simple with a party near level 50.

Enjoy your cutscenes...but the game isn't over yet. Before you continue, now that you're back in **Balfonheim Port**, how about selling off some loot? I list the Magepower Shishak down below, but I would recommend not purchasing it until you have crafted your first High Arcana so as not to lose the Feystone. If you have enough of the Leo Gems, Nihopalaoa is definitely a useful accessory - especially for Trial Mode.

| **Loot to sell** | **Bazaar Good** | **Item** | **Cost** | **Savings** |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Quality Stone (x5) | Memories of Yore | Pebble (x99) | 999 gil | N/A |
| Chimera Head (x2)<br>Orichalcum (x2)<br>Taurus Gem (x3) | Darksteel Blade | Stoneblade | 17800 gil | N/A |
| Blood-stained Necklace (x3)<br>Death's-Head (x2)<br>Leo Gem (x3) | Cursed Necklace | Nihopalaoa | 28000 gil | N/A |
| Charger Barding (x5)<br>Chimera Head (x2)<br>Feystone (x1) | Magepower Helm | Magepower Shishak | 7500 gil | N/A |

You can head straight for the [_Bahamut_](#walkthrough-part-6---finale) now, but this is your last chance to [finish up any sidequests](#optional---endgame-sidequests).

## OPTIONAL - Endgame Sidequests

I wouldn't expect you to play through all of these sections straight through. It's best to read through the Ultimate Weapon section early so that you can learn the easiest ways to get the stuff you need. A lot of the needed loot, for example, can be obtained easily from rare monsters that are part of the Hunt Club, but if you already finished the Hunt Club you will have to rely on less efficient methods of gaining the loot. Similarly, some of the Clan Centurio hunts, Hunt Club rare monsters, and Espers overlap really well. I'll try to point out when this is the case, but for simplicity I'll cover these one at a time rather than trying to do multiple things simultaneously. It's easier to keep track of that way.

Here's what you can do from here:

- [Obtaining Ultimate Weapons](#optional---endgame-sidequests)
- [The Hunt Club](#the-hunt-club)
- [Clan Centurio](#optional---endgame-sidequests)
- [Fishing](#optional---endgame-sidequests)
- [Espers](#lists-for-trophies)
- [Secret Bosses](#optional---endgame-sidequests)
- [Anything else needed for unlocking trophies](#optional---endgame-sidequests)

### Ultimate Weapons

The Zodiac Age release has updated versions of the original weapons, along with new weapons. The Zodiac Spear is no longer the most powerful weapon in the game (although it's still way up there). Below I will walk you through how to get the best offensive weapons in the game (I'm skipping measures). Some of these are ridiculously difficult and may have not really been intended to be available (such as the Seitengrat). As such, for some of the weapon types, I've included a few different weapons so you can choose how much effort you are willing to spend. At the very least, I try to cover the best weapon from each class that can be crafted with the bazaar, if one is available. For items needed for crafting, many of them require you to have already purchased the associated monograph, so assume that these cannot be obtained until you have purchased all of them from the Bazaar. Similarly, if a weapon is listed as being on of the extremely rare treasures in **Cerobi Steppe**, it does require the Diamond Armlet to be equipped by the character opening the treasure.

#### 1-Handed Sword

##### Simha

| License | Classes |
| --- | --- |
| Swords 9 | Knight<br>Time Battlemage (behind Zodiark) |

Simha must be crafted in the bazaar as the Well-forged Blade

| Items needed | Quantity | Recommended Source | Method | Region | Zones |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Leshach Halcyon | 1 | Leshach Entite | steal / drop | Paramina Rift | Head of the Silverflow<br>Icebound Flow <br>(requires heavy snow) |
| Lifewick | 3 | Necrofiend | steal / drop | Henne Mines | Special Charter Shaft |
| Ring Wyrm Scale | 4 | Shield Wyrm | steal / drop | Cerobi Steppe | North Liavell Hills<br>The Terraced Bank<br>Feddik River<br>Crossfield<br>Old Elanise Road |

##### Great Trango

| License | Classes |
| --- | --- |
| None needed | All |

Great Trango boasts the highest damage of any one-handed sword (153 vs 117 for Khumba). It also has the highest combo rate of all swords except for Khumba. It has no evasion, boost, however. It is invisible when equipped. Great Trango can be obtained in two ways.

- Very rare steal (3% / 6%) from esper [Zodiark](#lists-for-trophies). If something else is stolen, reset and try again.
- Rare treasure with Diamond Armlet equipped in **Pharos - Subterra / Abyssal - South**. Treasure has 2% appearance rate, 20% item drop, 5% if item. This turns out to a 1 in 5000 chance. Manipulation of random number generator is probably the only reasonable way to obtain it this way.

#### Dagger

##### Zwill Crossblade

| License | Classes |
| --- | --- |
| Daggers 6 | Shikari |

The Zwill Crossblade is the best dagger you can get from the Bazaar. It can also be obtained as the rarest drop from a Vampyr ( **Lhusu Mines / Transitway 1, Site 3, Site 9, Site 11**). The easiest method is probably stealing from a Deathclaw ( **Ridorana Cataract / Echoes from Times Garden, City of Other Days**).

| Items needed | Quantity | Recommended Source | Method | Region | Zones |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Malboro Flower | 7 | Vivian | drop / poach | Giruvegan | The Trimahla Water-steps<br>The Aadha Water-steps <br>Gate of Fire |
| Wind Crystal | 9 | Charybterix | drop | Cerobi Steppe | North Liavell Hills<br>The Terraced Bank<br>Crossfield<br>Old Elanise Road |
| Windslicer Pinion | 5 | Charybterix | steal / drop / poach | Cerobi Steppe | North Liavell Hills<br>The Terraced Bank<br>Crossfield<br>Old Elanise Road |

##### Shikari Nagasa

| License | Classes |
| --- | --- |
| Shikari Nagasa & Mina | Shikari |

There are two ways to obtain the Shikari Nagasa. It is an extremely rare treasure (1 in 10,000) in **Cerobi Steppe / Northsward**, appearing on the edge of the bluff on the north area. This is probably only reasonable to obtain using RNG manipulation. The other way is by stealing it from the rare game Vagrant Soul. Vagrant Soul appears in **Pharos - Subterra / Abyssal - North** after The Seer from the Hunt Club has been defeated. It has a 25% appearance rate in the hidden chamber where the technick Whither is obtains. If you do defeat Vagrant Soul, the Luxollid enemy can spawn which has the bow [Sagittarius](#sagittarius), so it may be wortwhile picking that up as well.

##### Mina

| License | Classes |
| --- | --- |
| Shikari Nagasa & Mina | Shikari |

The Mina can only be obtained as a rare drop from the Larva Eater, one of the most challenging rare games to spawn. Larva Eater can appear by almost every Waystone (not I, II, XI, XII, XVIII, XIX, or higher than XX) when you have killed 256 enemies since your last travel with a Way Stone. Using dividing Necrophobes by the Leo Gate Stone is one method.

#### Axe

##### Golden Axe

There are a few ways to obtain a Golden Axe. It can be stolen in **Pharos - Second Ascent** from a Reaver, or **Pharos - Subterra** from a Reaver or a High Reaver. It has a very slight chance of appearing next to where the Cleanse magick was obtained in **Cerobi Steppe - The Terraced Bank**. The most certain way to obtain it is from a one-time guaranteed chest in **Necrohol of Nabudis** **/ Cloister of Reason**, but you must defeat the boss Fury in the room first. The final way for obtaining the Golden Axe is crafting the Golden Battle Axe in the Bazaar.

##### Bonebreaker

| License | Classes |

The Bonebreaker is an extremely rare drop from Omega Mark XII. If you really want it, however, it is either this or use the RNG abuse method to find it in the southern section of **Cerobi Steppe / Feddik River**. This is one of those weapons the developers really didn't want anyone to get.

##### Bone of Byblos

| License | Classes |
| --- | --- |
| Maces 5 | Red Battlemage |

The Bone of Byblos is obtainable as a steal from Yiazmat (3% / 6% with Thief's Cuffs). It is also a one-time treasure in **Lhusu Mines / Site 5**. It has a 25% spawn rate, but is guaranteed to be the weapons if it spawns. It is in the northwesternmost dead-end. It also is one of the extremely rare items in **Cerobi Steppe / The Northsward**, requiring the Diamond Armlet equipped. It spawns near the center of the region.

#### Greatsword

##### Wyrmhero Blade

| License | Classes |
| --- | --- |
| None needed | All |

The Wymrhero Blade bestows the wielder with Bravery and Faith. It has an extremely high combo rate, and only the Tournesol has a higher damage. The charge time, however, is extremely long. Given that it requires no license, it is a very strong versatile weapon, as long as you can mitigate the charge time. It is only available by crafting The Dragon Crest in the Bazaar. The items needed for crafting are badges given as rewards for different activities in the game.

| Badge | Activity |
| --- | --- |
| Godslayer's Badge | Defeating the mark Yiazmat |
| Lu Shang's Badge | [Catching King of the Nebra 10 times in the Fishing minigame](#optional---endgame-sidequests) |
| Omega Badge | Defeating the mark Omega Mark XII |

##### Tournesol

| License | Classes |
| --- | --- |
| Tournesol | Knight |

The Tournesol is possibly the most difficult weapon to craft in the game. All three of the components have to be crafted themselves, and it's possible that even the High Arcana used to craft the materials needs to be crafted. The Gemsteel is also used in the Kumbha, so it is best to sell the items for the Tournesol and Kumbha at the same time to ensure that they both unlock. It also means that you will only need a total of 3 Gemsteels to craft both. If you purchase one before the other is unlocked, you will have to craft more Gemsteel for the other. The Tournesol shows up as The Sunflower in the Bazaar. To start with, here are the items you need to craft the Tournesol.

| Items needed | Quantity |
| --- | --- |
| Gemsteel | 3 |
| Empyreal Soul | 3 |
| Serpentarius | 3 |

Keep in mind when farming that you need enough of the materials to make 3 of each.

| Items needed for one Gemsteel | Quantity | Recommended Source | Method | Region | Zones |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Damascus Steel | 2 | Bune | steal / poach | Pharos - Second Ascent | Station of Banishment<br>Station of Suffering<br>Station of Ascension |
| Hell's-gate Flame | 2 | Cerberus | drop | The Feywood | Walk of Stolen Truths |
| Scarletite | 1 | Aspidochelon\* | steal | Cerobi Steppe | Feddik River |

\*Aspidochelon can only be killed once, so it is best to go in with two or three characters that can steal and turn off the gambits for anyone else. It has a 40% chance to appear, usually just across the first bridge approached from the south. Once you succesfully steal, leave back south to **North Liavell Hills** and the run northwest to **The Terraced Bank** to reset the steal. Return to **Feddick River** and keep zoning back to **North Liavell Hills** until it reappears. It took me about 5-10 to get 5 Scarletite this way. If you missed your chance here, your other options are a 6% drop from Emeralditan in **Nabreus Deadlands / Echoes of the Past** or **The Slumbermead**. You can also get it as a reward in the [Fishing](#optional---endgame-sidequests) minigame, but only after you've unlocked all the areas.

| Items needed for one Empyreal Soul | Quantity | Recommended Source | Method | Region | Zones |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| High Arcana | 1 | Espers and Bazaar | steal / craft | N/A | N/A |
| Soul Powder | 1 | Etém | drop / steal | Henne Mines | Special Charter Shaft |
| Wargod's Band | 2 | Victanir\* | steal | Nam-Yensa Sandsea | Yellow Sands |

\*Victanir, like the Aspidochelon, is part of the Hunt Club. If you kill it, it will never respawn. It appears near the exit to Zertinan Caverns, so you can use that exit to move two zones to reset the steal. It has a 40% appearance rate and is invisible until you are close. Fortunately, it is aggressive so if you do get close you won't miss it. The Wargod's Band is the only item that Victanir has available to steal, making this the best option for the item. If you missed getting enough Wargod's Bands from Victanir, your other option is to kill (6% drop) or poach (5%) Leynir in **Nabreus Deadlands / Lifeless Strand**.

| Items needed for one Serpentarius | Quantity | Recommended Source | Method | Region | Zones |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| High Arcana | 1 | Espers and Bazaar | steal / craft | N/A | N/A |
| Serpent Eye | 2 | Basilisk | drop / poach | The Feywood | White Magick's Embrace<br>Ice Field of Clearsight<br>The Edge of Reason |
| Snake Skin | 4 | Wild Snake | drop / steal / poach | Giza Plains (the Dry) | Toam Hills |

It's possible to only need 3 High Arcana. If you sell one High Arcana with just enough of everything else needed to make one Empyereal Soul and one Serpentarius, the High Arcana will count toward both. Never sell more than the quantity needed to craft one of each item. The excess will be wasted and will not count toward the next time you craft it. If you need to craft High Arcana, it is important that you already purchased the Canopic Jar from the Bazaar so that you will have obtained plenty of Arcana as random drops.

| Items needed for one High Arcana | Quantity | Recommended Source | Method | Region | Zones |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Arcana | 10 | Random drops after obtaining <br>Canopc Jar / Morbid Urn | drops | All | All |
| Feystone | 1 | Elementals and Entites | drop / steal | All | Most |
| Soul of Thamasa | 1 | Ishteen\* | steal | Barheim Passage | East-West Bypass<br>The Zeviah Span |

\*Farming Soul of Thamasa from Ishteen is very easy, but once you kill it Ishteen will never respawn. Ishteen is part of the [Hunt Club](#optional---endgame-sidequests). To spawn it, head to **Barheim Passage** **/ East-West Bypass**. Kill a few of the foes and hang out by the exit to **The Zeviah Span**. Turn off all attack gambits and have two in your party that have a gambit to steal from the foe with highest max HP. After a few minutes, Ishteen will spawn on top of you. It may summon some undead. You can manually target those with your third character. As soon as you see the successful steal, move into **Zeviah Span**. In a couple of seconds Ishteen will appear with a fresh Soul of Thamasa to steal. Once you have stolen it, flee back to the Teleport Crystal. This resets both steals and allows you to repeat. The Soul of Thamasa is the only item available to be stolen from Ishteen, so you should be able to get as many as you need fairly quickly. If you miss obtaining the Soul of Thamasas you need, your other option is killing Oversouls throughout the **Necrohol of Nabudis**. These have a 6% drop rate for the Soul of Thamasa.

### The Hunt Club

After progressing the story passed Draklor Laboratory, the Hunt Club becomes available. Once it has been initiated, 30 unique monsters can appear, each giving a trophy when dispatched. These trophies can then be exchanged for different rewards, but your reward is dependent on who you turn you trophy in to. As such, you cannot get everything, but nothing is unique either (albeit some items are very rare). It's not a bad idea to wait to initiate this until after you've done most or all of the story through **Pharos** and even much of the other sidequests such as the Clan Centurio hunts. This prevents high-level unique monsters spawning where you weren't ready for them. It's also a little easier to keep track if you do them intentionally in a sequence. That said, there's nothing stopping you from jumping right in as soon as you are able, only be warned that some foes will not be accessible until you are about to finish the game.

To kick things off, head to **Phon Coast** and head south from the Teleport Crystal until you find some Bangaas hanging around a tall wooden building. Talk to Huntmaster, standing in front of the door. Tell him you are a bird of prey and he will give you an initiation hunt: Thalassinon. Go to **Vaddu Strand** and consult the _Conditions_ in the table below to get Thalassinon to spawn. Once you have finished off the beast, return to Huntmaster. Welcome to the Club!

You can turn your trophies into Atak, Blok, and Stok. Before doing so randomly, decide which items you want to unlock with the Shifty-Eyed Man merchant. Until you know, don't turn in trophies. Consult the item list below in the [Rewards](#rewards) section for more details.

#### Rewards

The items available for sell by the Shifty-Eyed Man all depend on how many trophies each of the Bangaas have received from you. Additionally, the Huntmaster will give a reward based on who received the most trophies and if you gave that Bangaa more than half once you have turned in all 30. The best way to make your decision is to see how things will play out based on who you focus on. Unfortunately, for perfect visualization, we would need a three-dimensional table or a very large ternary graph. We'll have to settle for lists.

| Reward from Huntmaster for giving the most trophies to: |
|  | Atak | Blok | Stok |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Given more than 15 trophies | Zodiac Spear | Zodiac Escutcheon | Ribbon |
| Given 15 or fewer trophies | Staff of the Magi | Ensanguined Shield | Sage's Ring |

Notice that you can only get one of these six items as a reward. As you'll see below, it is possible to make both the Zodiac Spear and Zodiac Escutcheon available _for purchase_ by forgoing everything from the table and splitting the trophies equally among the Bangaas. Ribbon's can also be stolen on stage 49 of Trial Mode from Hashmal. As such, it's still best to see what items can become available for purchase based on who gets what. Unsurprisingly, making Atak your recipient puts more weapons in the shop, Blok adds armor, and Stok unlocks accessories. Meeting the requirement in the table automatically unlocks the item, regardless of who wins in the end. For example, if you split the trophies evenly across the Bangaas, you'll see Atak's first 4 weapons, Blok's first 4 armors, and Stok's first 3 accessories.
