There are a lot of very powerful items to choose from, but many of them can be obtained elsewhere or you only really need one and can be crafted in the bazaar (like the Nihopalaoa). As such, Genji Gloves are probably the best item to shoot for, and if you give Stok 20 instead of 15 you get a Ribbon as well. If you aren't going for Genji Gloves, then the even split to make both Zodiac items available is probably the next best option. Once you've turned in all 30 trophies, speak to the Outfitter (Shifty-eyed Man) to purchase the items unlocked. You can only buy one of each item. Then, return to **Archades / Highgarden Terrace** and find the Hunt Club Owner. He looks like Colonel Sanders talking to a handful of other nobles. He will reward you with one of each of the Zodiac gems: Aries, Libra, Taurus, Scorpio, Gemini, Sagittarius, Cancer, Capricorn, Leo, Aquarius, Virgo, and Pisces.

### Clan Centurio - Finishing Prior Hunts

Some of these hunts still to do have been available for a while. The reason I've waited until now has been two-fold. First, when they were first available they were quite a bit above my level, so I thought it best to wait. Second, several of them can be taken on in a single run and can be done in tandem with finishing the Medallion quest and gaining another Esper. Specifically, [Carrot](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/ps4/191202-final-fantasy-xii-the-zodiac-age/faqs/78871/clan-centurio#carrot-stalk), [Roblon](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/ps4/191202-final-fantasy-xii-the-zodiac-age/faqs/78871/clan-centurio#adding-insult-to-injury), [Goliath](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/ps4/191202-final-fantasy-xii-the-zodiac-age/faqs/78871/clan-centurio#the-creature-collector), and [Deathscythe](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/ps4/191202-final-fantasy-xii-the-zodiac-age/faqs/78871/clan-centurio#dead-city-watch) all take place in northern Salikawood, Nabreus Deadlands, and Necrohol of Nabudis. Once you finish them, you should pop over to the walkthrough that covers obtaining [Chaos](#lists-for-trophies) to finish up.

The [Belito](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/ps4/191202-final-fantasy-xii-the-zodiac-age/faqs/78871/clan-centurio#a-dark-rumor) hunt can be taken on before or after those. I initiated it unintentionally while I was working through the first portions of the [Hunt Club](#optional---endgame-sidequests) and doing some side exploration of Zertinan Caverns.

The [Ancient Man of Mystery](https://gamefaqs.gamespot.com/ps4/191202-final-fantasy-xii-the-zodiac-age/faqs/78871/clan-centurio#battle-on-the-big-bridge) can be done before or after the rest as well, but it may be best to save it for last so that you have a few more levels. There are some important items you need to steal, so you want to be able to take a beating while you attempt to steal. Once you've finished all of these, there will be a whole new set of hunts to gather and take on.

#### A Dark Rumor

To find your mark, head over to **Ogir-Yensa Sandsea / Central Junction** and make your way to the northeast exit into **Zertinan Caverns**. The **Canopy of Clay** is isolated from the rest of **Zertinan Caverns**, until you break open a path by interacting with a rock at the southernmost point. A bit to the east of the rock is a one-time guaranteed chest with the Sight Unseeing technick. Make your way to the northwest exit of **Canopy of Clay** into the isolated portion of **Nam-Yensa Sandsea / Withering Shores**. When you approach the far platform, you'll be treated to a scene and a fight with something other than Belito.

> ##### Hunt: Ba'Gamnan
>
>
> | **Level** | 49 | **LP** | 38 | **CP** | 4160 |
> | **HP** | 115659 | **Steal** | Elixir (10/30%), Dark Matter (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Water |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Fire<br>\[Half Damage\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Silence, Sap, Slow, Lure |
>
> This battle seems busy as it is a four-on-four once Monid joins you. In addition to Ba'Gamnan, you have to take on his henchmen Rinok, Gijuk, and Bwagi. If Balthier is in the party, Ba'Gamnan will focus completely on him. The battle finishes when Ba'Gamnan dies. It still may be wise taking out the henchmen as they aren't too tough while Ba'Gamnan can get pretty difficult to hurt when his health gets low. He also starts doing quite a bit more damage, so having fewer aggressors towards the end is helpful. Protect, Shell, Haste, and Bubble are all great statuses, and shield users can block a lot of the physical attacks. Dispel any Bangaa that aren't going down quickly enough, and be sure to dispel Ba'Gamnan once the other three Bangaa are gone. Ba'Gamnan's attacks Eviscerator and Shining Ray can do quite a bit of damage, so be ready with some healing gambits.

| **Hunt Completed** | Ba'Gamnan |
| **Rewards** | 5100 Gil<br>Megalixir (x1) |

After the battle, Monid will explain the situation and hand over your reward. If you return to the platform after leaving the zone, you can spawn the rare game Imdugud by waiting in the middle of the platform for a little while.

#### Carrot Stalk

To get Carrot to spawn, you must reach **Sun-dappled Path** without anything dying in **The Salikawood**. The best way to do this is to teleport to **The Salikawood** and either turn off all gambits or flee through **Garden of Decay**. When you arrive, Krjn will inform you if you were successful. Carrot spawns near the exit to **Trunkwall Road**.

> ##### Hunt: Carrot
>
>
> | **Level** | 49 | **LP** | 28 | **CP** | 3960 |
> | **HP** | 110842 | **Steal** | Dark Crystal (55/80%), Foul Liquid (10/30%), Putrid Liquid (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Dark<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Silence, Slow |
>
> As with all Malboros, the real danger here is all of the status effects. Particularly challenging are Disable and Stop. You can use accessories to protect against one or the other, but not both. Do not attempt this without plenty of Remedies as you will need to toss those on anyone that gets affected by Putrid Breath.
>
> Start the battle with Dispel, and be prepared to use it again whenever Carrot uses Hero's March. After try to keep two characters distant by using ranged weapons. Slow is effective, so a crossbow with Long Bolts may be useful. Once Carrot's HP drops below 50%, it will use Growing Threat and its level will double. Once it's HP is below 20%, it becomes very difficult to inflict damage. At this point, the most effective way to finish it off is to toss X-Potions at it with someone with as many of the Potion Lores unlocked as possible and while wearing a Pheasant Netsuke. This will do several thousand damage per X-Potion and should be able to finish the battle with about half a dozen or so.

| **Hunt Completed** | Carrot |
| **Rewards** | 5200 Gil<br>Stink Bombs (x1)<br>Putrid Liquid (x1) |

#### Adding Insult to Injury

Roblon is found at the summit of **Nabreus Deadlands / Overlooking Eternity**. This is one of the locations visited during the [Medallions quest](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts). Check out that quest if you need a list of treasures, enemies, and key loot. If you haven't been here before, there is a secret path in the northwest corner of **The Slumbermead** that leads to **The Fog Mutters**. As you ascend **Overlooking Eternity**, there are numerous Dead Bones that spawn. The spawn points can regenerate about three times. If you aren't careful, you can easily get overwhelmed. It's a good idea to try to spawn enemies and pull them down the hill to avoid initiating the fight with the mark. Do what you can to eliminate all the Dead Bones before starting the main fight.

> ##### Hunt: Roblon
>
>
> | **Level** | 43 | **LP** | 26 | **CP** | 4410 |
> | **HP** | 177265 | **Steal** | Fire Stone (55/80%), Iron Ore (10/30%), Fire Crystal (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Water |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Fire<br>\[Half Damage\] Ice, Lightning, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Blind, Sap, Slow, Libra |
>
> Roblon is pretty straightforward. It has high defense, so it may take a while. It casts elemental spells, and the Dead Bones can occasionally Blind your party. Keep the Dead Bones in check, have a decent healer, and a Blindna gambit set up. Other than that, there are no particularly dangerous or annoying issues. Be sure not to attack it with a fire-attuned weapon and it will fall eventually.

| **Hunt Completed** | Roblon |
| **Rewards** | 3100 Gil<br>Giant's Helmet (x1)<br>Mythril (x1) |

If you are taking on all of the Nalbina hunts at once, you'll want to continue on to the Teleport Crystal in the Nabreus Deadlands as that's where the next hunt picks up. When you are ready for your reward, head back to **Nalbina Town / West Ward** to tell your great deed to Morgen.

#### The Creature Collector

| **Treasures** | 10 ( **Lifeless Strand**), 7 ( **Field of the Fallen Lord**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Warp (magick), Opal Ring, Cross Scale |
| **Enemies** | Leynir, Baknamy, Foobar, Shield Wyrm |

Starting from the Nabreus Crystal, head north into **Lifeless Strand**. When you reach the central platform in the north, look on the north edge for a secret path through the reeds to find a one-time guaranteed treasure with Warp magick. The one-time treasure with an Opal Ring is only a 25% spawn rate in the southern tip of the eastern section. It is worth rezoning until you find it. In **Field of the Fallen Lord**, the Cross Scale is in a one-time guaranteed treasure at the end of the hook-shaped dead-end in the center of the north edge. Do not cross into the **Necrohol of Nabudis** without the Float status on. There are multiple traps right at the entry that you will spawn on. Your mark is just up ahead around the corner.

> ##### Hunt: Goliath
>
>
> | **Level** | 48 | **LP** | 27 | **CP** | 4830 |
> | **HP** | 224294 | **Steal** | Holy Magicite (55/80%), Iron Ore (10/30%), Holy Crystal (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Dark |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Holy<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Slow, Libra |
>
> This mark is another golem-like automaton. There are a few important differences between this battle and the one with Roblon. For starters, Goliath likes to us a lot of Gravity-based magick. This makes Bubble less valuable unless bestowed by a Bubble Belt. Second, instead of Dead Bones you'll be surrounded be Baknamy. If you take them out without starting the fight with Goliath, the fight is easier to manage. Third, Goliath will put up a magick barrier very quickly, so if you aren't fast with Dispel you won't be able to removes its beneficial statuses.
>
> Bubble Belt, Haste, and Protect are great statuses for your melee character. The others should focus on healing and removing Disable. If you have the Expose technick at your disposal, it is probably worth tossing that a few times. When Robon's health becomes critical, it attacks much faster, does more damage, and its defense increases. Guns and Crossbows seem to do more damage than melee weapons at this point.

| **Hunt Completed** | Goliath |
| **Rewards** | 3600 Gil<br>Save the Queen (x1)<br>Einherjarium (x1) |

Like the other Nalbina hunts, you can continue on to find the next, or return for your rewards. Once you are ready to collect, head back to **Barrong** in **Nalbina Town / West Ward**. Continuing on, however, also gives you the chance to get the rewards of completing the [Medallion quest](#optional---post-draklor-sidequests-and-hunts): two bosses and an esper (along with some nice loot). If you haven't done the Medallion quest yet, I'd recommend doing that so you can take on all of this together.

#### Dead City Watch

| **Treasures** | 6 ( **Hall of Slumbering Might**), 7 ( **Hall of the Ivory Covenant**), 1 ( **Cloister of Reason**), 11 ( **Cloister of the Highborn**), 9 ( **Cloister of the Distant Song**), 1 ( **Cloister of Solace**), 8 ( **Hall of Effulgent Light**) |
| **Notable Loot** | Jade Collar, Faith (magick), Golden Axe, Black Robes, Ensanguined Shield, Bravery (magick), Protectga (magick), White Mask, Scathe Mote, Silencega (magick), Hermes Sandals |
| **Enemies** | Baknamy, Elvoret, Oversoul, Babil, Zombie Warlock, Pandora, Helvinek (rare game), Goliath (mark), Deathscythe (mark), Fury (boss), Humbaba Mistant (boss), Chaos (esper) |

There is a lot of great loot to be had here in the **Necrohol**, including much of the remaining magicks. Bravery and Faith do for your offense what Protect and Shell do for your defense. Given that Deathscythe will not appear unless certain conditions are met, it should be straightforward to collect all the loot and magicks before facing Deathscythe (or the other bosses involved in obtaining the esper Chaos). Most of the chests in here have low spawn rates (around 30%), so only waste your time farming if you REALLY want something. I'll still point out where some of the goods are along the way.

Deathscythe is fairly close to the middle of the region, so getting to him from the Nabreus Crystal and the Salikawood Crystal requires about the same amount of travel. Assuming you are continuing on from defeating Goliath, you should be at the north end of the **Necrohol of Nabudis** in the **Hall of Slumbering Might**. Start by heading south from where you fought Goliath. If you follow this path as far as you can, you may find a treasure that might hold a Doom Mace. Start making your way east, now. There is a small room off the south wall that may hold a Power Vest. If you continue following the path past the stairwell, there is a one-time guaranteed chest in the last room holding a Jade Collar. Go into the stairwell and descend into **Hall of the Ivory Covenant**.

As you exit the stairwell, look north for a shot at a Zephyr Pole. Head south and then west. At the intersection, check the room to the north for a chance for a Giant's Helmet, then make your way south. When forced to turn east, the room right below you may contain an Orichalcum Dirk. Across the hall from that room is the Door of Loathing. If you are also working towards gaining the esper Chaos and have completed the Medallions quest, this is the room where you find [Fury](#lists-for-trophies). If you fight Fury now, make sure you grab the Golden Axe sitting on the trap before leaving the room. This is also where Vorres will spawn if you are doing the Hunt Club. It will only spawn, however, if you are engaged with a Dark Elemental near the doorway. Continuing south, when you come to a four-way intersection, the room to the south contains a one-time guaranteed treasure with the Faith magick. You should be able to see the stairwell on your minimap, so make your way there.

In **Cloister of the Highborn**, turn north out of the stairwell and follow the path to the half-submerged room. There may be a Kaiser Shield for grabs here by the water edge. Make your way back past the stairwell and start heading west. The first room to the north may hold a Carabineer Mail, and the second room may hold a Jade Gown. Head north at the large hallway. The first room to the west has a one-time guaranteed treasure with Black Robes. If you head all the way north to the small room, there is a 3% chance to get the one-time chest with the Ensanguined Shield. Just out of that room to the west is an invisible merchant. This is the only place you can get the magick Esunaga. He also has good accessories and items. Head back down the hallway south and look for the next route south through the Babil foes. In the room south of you, there may be a Runeblade. This is also the route to Chaos after you defeat Fury and Humbaba Mistant. Head back to the east-west hallway and continue northwest. When you reach the chamber with the exit to the next zone, stop first in the room to the northwest for the one-time guaranteed treasure with Bravery magick. By this point, you may have encountered several of the Oversoul enemies. Once you defeat enough, the rare game Helvinek will automatically spawn. This is the room where I encountered him the first time. Head south into **Cloister of Distant Song**.

Follow the path south and west. At the intersection, head south. There is a four way intersection with two Babil guarding the route west. Look east for a chance for a Yakei, then head west. Behind another Babil is a one-time guaranteed chest with Protectga magick. This is also the Door of Horrors location where you can fight [Humbaba Mistant](#lists-for-trophies). There is a Scathe Mote sitting on a trap in the room after defeating Humbaba Mistant. Back at the four-way intersection head south and follow the path as it takes you west. The room in front of you may contain a Burning Bow. If you continue past the nearby stairwell, there is a guaranteed one-time chest with a White Mask in the last room, and a chance for a Hypnocrown in the room south of that. Use the stairwell to get to **Hall of Effulgent Light**.

As you begin making your way north, the room to the east may contain a Penetrating Crossbow. When you come to an intersection take the hallway north to the last room and notice that the room extends south even though it isn't shown on your minimap. Inside the first unmapped chamber is a guaranteed one-time treasure with Silencega magick. The next chamber has a 25% chance for a one-time treasure with Hermes Sandals as well as a mimic-style enemy called Pandora. Make your way back south and head east to the next room. If you head south and look in the last dead-end to the east, you may find a Chakra Band. From here, you can head out into **The Salikawood** using the zone exit off to the east so you can save, or you can return to take on Deathscythe (and possibly Chaos if you wish) back in **Cloister of the Highborn**.

It's a good idea to clear out some foes from this area before spawning Deathscythe. Once you do, turn your gambits off and attack one of your characters until their HP is below 10%. If Deathscythe doesn't spawn, do it to a second character. Be ready to turn gambits back on and toss some X-Potions or Elixirs at whoever took the damage.

> ##### Hunt: Deathscythe
>
>
> | **Level** | 44 | **LP** | 27 | **CP** | 4490 |
> | **HP** | 125601 | **Steal** | Book of Orgain-Mille (55/80%), Book of Orgain (10/30%), Capricorn Gem (3/6%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | Holy |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Absorb\] Dark<br>\[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Silence, Sap, Slow, Lure |
>
> It's been so long, but the Berserk/Blind trick is finally available again! Be aware that its normal attacks can cause stop, though. Having Power Armlets equipped can protect you, otherwise have some gambits set up with Chronos Tears. Holy weapons, Holy magick, and curative magick can all deal out a good bit of damage. At 50% HP, Deathscythe will autmotically become immune to physical damage for two minutes. After that wears off, it will use Death on one of your characters, so don't bide your time. Instead consider casting Curaga on it as much as possible.

| **Hunt Completed** | Deathscythe |
| **Rewards** | 2800 Gil<br>Hi-Ether (x2)<br>Soul of Thamasa (x1) |

Now you can continue on to fight [Chaos](#lists-for-trophies) if you've already taken care of Fury and Humbaba Mistant. This is also the last of the Nalbina hunts - each of which has a scene with Jovy when turning in for rewards. Tell Popol in **Nalbina Town / Jajim Bazaar** to get your rewards. If you've done the other three above and spoken to their petitioners, find Jovy running around in **West Ward** to get an extra reward. You also get an Elixir and a Knot of Rust (yes, the story is the better reward - a nice touch, in my opinion).

#### Battle on the Big Bridge

If you teleport to **Lhusu Mines**, the moment you move west into **Tasche Span** the battle will begin.

> ##### Hunt: Gilgamesh - First Encounter
>
>
> | **Level** | 45 | **LP** | 5 | **CP** | 3960 |
> | **HP** | 123103 | **Steal** | _Above 60% health_: <br>Potion (55/80%), Hi-Potion (10/30%), 1000 gil (3/6%)<br>_40%-60% health_: Potion (55/80%), 2000 gil (3/6%)<br>_20%-40% health_: Genji Shield (10/30%)<br>_Below 20% health_: Genji Gloves (10/30%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | N/A |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Slow, Libra |
>
> The most important thing to know going into this battle: Gilgamesh can be stolen from multiple times. When Gilgamesh's health drops to certain levels, there is a scene or transition. Immediately, turn on your steal gambits and turn off your attack gambits. Make sure that your steal gambit is set to foe:any. Do not miss grabbing the Genji Shield and Genji Gloves. Once you get the successful steal, attack again until the next transition.
>
> Overall, this fight isn't too difficult. Gilgamesh is accompanied by his dog, Enkidu. If you take out Enkidu quickly, it's one less foe to deal with. Gilgamesh has a couple of special attacks, but they don't do much damage if you keep Protect, Bubble, and Haste up. Once you've stolen the Genji Gear, you may want to equip it so that you can damage more quickly. This battle will make it seem like this is going to be easy...

Now you get to track Gilgamesh down. As you are making your way through the next area, focus on having the party you plan on using reach levels that are not divisible by 2, 3, or 4. Levels 55, 59, and 61 are good examples. Once they hit one of those levels, swap them out. For reference, I beat Gilgamesh with my two stealers at level 55 and my buffer at 59.

Continue west into **Site 9**. You should not be able to be on this hunt unless you had already done the Antlion hunt. Hopefully you already grabbed the Site 11 Key from the man in the **Hunters' Camp**. You'll need it to open the Wrought Iron Gate in the central path north from the southern section. This gives you access to **Site 11** and beyond.

| **Treasures** | 7 ( **Site 11**), 1 ( **Lasche Span**), 12 ( **Site 5**), 5 ( **Site 6 South**), 2 ( **Site 6 North**), 1 ( **Staging Area**), 1 ( **Site 7** |
| **Notable Loot** | Orochi, Zwill Crossblade, Golden Skullcap, Renewing Morion, Dragon Whisker, Bone of Byblos, Quasimodo Boots, Glimmering Robes, Arcturus, Lhusu Candle, Magepower Shishak, Jackboots, Maximillian, Whale Whisker, Scathe (magick), Masamune |
| **Enemies** | Killer Mantis, Vampyr, Bug, Malboro, Aeronite, Abysteel, Dark Lord, Pandora, Gemhorn (rare game), Bombshell (rare game), Gilgamesh (mark) |

As you make your way to the east and north, there may be a treasure with Oil Bombs in the small chamber with two Killer Mantises. When you reach an intersection, head south into a chamber with two Malboros. There are two one-time guaranteed chests containing an Orochi and a Zwill Crossblade. Be sure to inspect any other treasures as well as one is actually a Bug. Head back to the intersection and go west this time. Head all the way west before going down the stairs to find a one-time guaranteed Golden Skullcap. Make your way all the way up north. When you reach the tracks, head to the west end for a one-time guaranteed Renewing Morion. Check the platform for another Bug. Operate the nearby Gate Switchboard to raise the gate blocking your path. Kill the remaining enemies and Gemhorn will have spawned back where the Malboros were. If it didn't, you missed an enemy (or the last enemy you killed was too close and you need to move away a bit without leaving the zone). For the sake of creating a shortcut, head back to the middle east exit into **Site 9** and use the Gate Switchboard. Return to **Site 11** and this time take the northernmost exit.

In the **Lasche Span** there is a one-time guaranteed treasure in the middle of the zone with a Dragon Whisker. You also have a 20% chance of encountering the rare game Bombshell on one of the two ends of the bridge. In the next zone, **Site 5**, follow the path to a four-way intersection and turn north. In the small chamber, two treasures may appear. There is a 25% chance for a one-time treasure with a Bone of Byblos. If it doesn't spawn, you may want to rezone and try again if you like maces. Back at the four-way, head south as far as you can. Here you may find a one-time treasure with Quasimodo Boots and you will find the Candle Urn revealing the rest of the map. Return to the previous intersection and head east. Where the path turns north, there is a one-time guaranteed treasure with Glimmering Robes on the east wall. Head all the way north to where the path turns back west. You may find a quiver of Lead Bolts there. Continue west and turn north at the intersection. In the last chamber before the exit you will find a one-time guaranteed Arcturus for your gun-user. Take the nearby exit into **Site 6 South**.

In the first chamber, look north for a hidden path and take it to **Site 6 North**. Watch out for the colorful treasures here as they are very powerful Pandoras. The treasures you are looking for are dull and in the back room. There is a one-time guaranteed Whale Whisker for your pole user, and a Maximillian for your heavy armor wearers. Head back to **Site 6 South**. At the intersection head east to the dead-end to find a one-time guaranteed Magepower Shishak. Return once more to the intersection and finally head south. Follow this until it turns east. In the chamber where it turns south you may find Wyrmfire Shot. In the small chamber before the southern exit, there is a 25% chance to find the one-time treasure with Germinas Boots. Given its proximity to the exit, you may want to rezone a few times if you don't see it at first. When you reach **Staging Area** with the Save Crystal, be sure to grab the nearby Scathe magick from a one-time treasure. Now be prepared for a much tougher version of Gilgamesh.

> ##### Hunt: Gilgamesh - Second Encounter
>
>
> | **Level** | 70 | **LP** | 10 | **CP** | 6700 |
> | **HP** | 473246 | **Steal** | _Above 60% health_: <br>Hi-Potion (55/80%), X-Potion (10/30%), 3000 gil (3/6%)<br>_40%-60% health_: Hi-Potion (55/80%), 4000 gil (3/6%)<br>_20%-40% health_: Genji Helm(10/30%)<br>_Below 20% health_: Genji Armor(10/30%) |
> | **Elemental Weaknesses** | N/A |
> | **Elemental Resistances** | \[Half Damage\] Fire, Ice, Lightning, Water, Wind, Earth, Dark, Holy |
> | **Status Susceptibilities** | Libra |
>
> The most important thing to know going into this battle: Gilgamesh can be stolen from multiple times. When Gilgamesh's health drops to certain levels, there is a scene or transition. Immediately, turn on your steal gambits and turn off your attack gambits. Do not miss grabbing the Genji Helm and Genji Armor. Once you get the successful steal, attack again until the next transition.
>
> Second most imporant thing: don't use anyone with levels divisible by 2, 3, or 4. He casts severe status impairments that are level based. Even so, you may still get afflicted with Stop or Sap at some point.
>
> In terms of a party, you absolutely need two people who can steal, someone who can cast Curaja, Arise, and Esuna, and the ability to put on Protect, Bubble, and Haste. Bravery is a nice touch, too.
>
> This battle is much tougher all around. Don't let the level 70 scare you, though. Open up with Dispel on Enkidu followed by a strong Quickening Chain. Spread your party out and take down Enkidu first. The dog seems to be very good at insta-KO shots, so be ready with some good Phoenix Down or Arise gambits. Once Enkidu is out of the picture, start to work on Gilgamesh. Once he reaches 40% health, be sure to steal the Genji Helm. You might want to be wearing all the Genji Gear on your primary physical attacker. His combos start becoming even deadlier, so expect a KO here and there.
>
| **Hunt Completed** | Gilgamesh |
| **Rewards** | 10000 Gil<br>Excalipur (x1) |

After the battle, be sure to grab the guaranted one-time chest with the Masamune, then make your way out of the mines back to Montblanc. If you've done all of the hunts I've covered so far, you'll also get some other rewards. Knight of the Round: _Megalixir (x2), Teleport Stone (x3)_; defeating Rafflesia: _1800 gil_; defeating Daedalus: _1900 gil_; defeating Tyrant: _1900 gil_; defeating Hydro: _2000 gil_; defeating Humbaba Mistant: _2100 gil_; defeating Fury: _2100 gil, Bacchus's Wine (x1)_; commanding 8 espers: _Gemsteel (x1), Teleport Stone (x2)_. You can also pick up to Elite Marks: Ixion and The Seer (aka Shadowseer). Montblanc is the petitioner for the Seer, so you will get to learn more about it immediately.

Now that you've beaten Gilgamesh, all of the Fishing sidequest can be completed. Additionally, there should be several new hunts to begin at a Notice Board. Your rank of Knight of the Round also means that all Green Magicks are available to purchase from the Clan Provisioner. Reverse should be the last you still need.

Now is also a good time to sell loot. Here are some of the items that I had come available or you might if you've done what I've done. FYI, I had done most of the Hunt Club by this point and used several of those hunts to farm some of the loot needed for the ultimate weapons. Be sure to only sell just enough to get the High Arcana to appear. Then purchase it and craft it again until you have at least 3, but preferable more in case you make a mistake with selling them later.
